window.runRenderGroup5SelfTests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSurfaceMaterial!=='function')errors.push('RenderSurfaceMaterial missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('BasicDirectRenderer missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator.normal!=='function')errors.push('SurfaceInterpolator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  PhysicalBSDF,SurfaceInterpolator,BasicDirectRenderer,RenderGroup5Contract

const LightTypes=Object.freeze(['point','spot','rectangle','disc','sphere','sun','dome','mesh']);

const LightDefaults=Object.freeze({
  point:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:2,range:0,coneAngle:45,penumbra:.15}),
  spot:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:2,range:0,coneAngle:45,penumbra:.15}),
  rectangle:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,width:50,height:50,range:0,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0}),
  disc:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0}),
  sphere:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0}),
  sun:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:.5,range:0,coneAngle:.53,penumbra:0}),
  dome:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:100,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0}),
  mesh:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0})
});

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;
window.__3DLiteLightEnergy3983=true;
window.__3DLiteLightDefaultDirection3983=true;
window.ThreeDLiteLightEnergy3983=Object.freeze({version:'3.99.0',baselineIntensity:1,referenceDistance:100});

var EditorLightSystem={
  nextId:1,
  isLightObject(obj){return !!obj?.userData?.renderLight},

  iconGeometry(type){
    const pts=[];
    const add=(a,b)=>{pts.push(...a,...b)};
    const circle=(plane,r,z=0,segments=12,start=0,end=Math.PI*2)=>{
      for(let i=0;i<segments;i++){
        const a=start+(end-start)*i/segments,b=start+(end-start)*(i+1)/segments;
        let p0,p1;
        if(plane==='xy'){p0=[Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r,z];p1=[Math.cos(b)*r,Math.sin(b)*r,z]}
        else if(plane==='xz'){p0=[Math.cos(a)*r,z,Math.sin(a)*r];p1=[Math.cos(b)*r,z,Math.sin(b)*r]}
        else {p0=[z,Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r];p1=[z,Math.cos(b)*r,Math.sin(b)*r]}
        add(p0,p1);
      }
    };
    const frame=(w,h,z=0)=>{const x=w*.5,y=h*.5;add([-x,-y,z],[x,-y,z]);add([x,-y,z],[x,y,z]);add([x,y,z],[-x,y,z]);add([-x,y,z],[-x,-y,z])};
    const tick=(len=.72)=>{add([0,0,-.12],[0,0,-len]);add([0,0,-len],[.12,0,-len+.16]);add([0,0,-len],[-.12,0,-len+.16])};
    switch(type){
      case 'point':
        add([-.42,0,0],[.42,0,0]);add([0,-.42,0],[0,.42,0]);add([0,0,-.42],[0,0,.42]);
        add([-.18,-.18,0],[.18,.18,0]);add([.18,-.18,0],[-.18,.18,0]);break;
      case 'spot':
        circle('xy',.16,0,8);add([-.16,0,0],[-.42,0,-.62]);add([.16,0,0],[.42,0,-.62]);
        add([0,-.16,0],[0,-.42,-.62]);add([0,.16,0],[0,.42,-.62]);circle('xy',.42,-.62,10);tick(.9);break;
      case 'rectangle': frame(.9,.58,0);tick(.72);break;
      case 'disc': circle('xy',.42,0,12);add([-.18,0,0],[.18,0,0]);add([0,-.18,0],[0,.18,0]);tick(.7);break;
      case 'sphere': circle('xy',.4,0,12);circle('xz',.4,0,10);break;
      case 'sun':
        circle('xy',.22,0,10);for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/4,c=Math.cos(a),q=Math.sin(a);add([c*.32,q*.32,0],[c*.48,q*.48,0])}tick(.86);break;
      case 'dome':
        add([-.48,0,0],[.48,0,0]);circle('xz',.48,0,8,0,Math.PI);circle('yz',.48,0,8,0,Math.PI);break;
      case 'mesh':
        add([0,.46,0],[.46,0,0]);add([.46,0,0],[0,-.46,0]);add([0,-.46,0],[-.46,0,0]);add([-.46,0,0],[0,.46,0]);
        add([-.28,0,0],[.28,0,0]);add([0,-.28,0],[0,.28,0]);tick(.7);break;
      default: add([-.4,0,0],[.4,0,0]);add([0,-.4,0],[0,.4,0]);
    }
    const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pts,3));
    g.computeBoundingSphere();
    g.userData={...(g.userData||{}),lightViewportIcon:true,iconType:type,uiThemed:true};
    return g;
  },

  updateViewportIconScale(obj,camera,renderer){
    if(!obj||!camera||!renderer)return;
    const h=Math.max(1,renderer.domElement?.clientHeight||renderer.domElement?.height||720);
    const px=Number(obj.userData?.lightViewportIcon?.pixels)||22;
    let worldPerPixel=1/h;
    if(camera.isPerspectiveCamera){
      const op=obj.getWorldPosition(new THREE.Vector3()),cp=camera.getWorldPosition(new THREE.Vector3());
      const d=Math.max(.01,op.distanceTo(cp));
      worldPerPixel=2*d*Math.tan(THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(camera.fov||50)*.5)/h;
    }else if(camera.isOrthographicCamera){
      worldPerPixel=Math.abs((camera.top-camera.bottom)/(camera.zoom||1))/h;
    }
    const sc=Math.max(.001,worldPerPixel*px);
    obj.scale.setScalar(sc);
  },

  create(type,opts={}){
    type=LightTypes.includes(type)?type:'point';
    const d=LightDefaults[type]||LightDefaults.point;
    const size=Math.max(.01,Number(opts.size??d.size??25));
    const geometry=this.iconGeometry(type);
    const color=new THREE.Color(opts.color||'#fff2c8');
    const material=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
      color:new THREE.Color('#aeb4bc'),transparent:true,opacity:.82,
      depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,toneMapped:false
    });
    const obj=new THREE.LineSegments(geometry,material);
    obj.userData.lightViewportIcon={theme:'editor-neutral',pixels:22,version:'3.98.3'};
    obj.onBeforeRender=(renderer,_scene,camera)=>this.updateViewportIconScale(obj,camera,renderer);
    obj.renderOrder=1000;
    obj.name=(type[0].toUpperCase()+type.slice(1))+' Light '+this.nextId++;
    obj.userData.renderLight={
      id:'light-'+Date.now().toString(36)+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),
      type,
      enabled:opts.enabled!==false,
      color:'#'+color.getHexString(),

      worldPerPixel=2*d*Math.tan(THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(camera.fov||50)*.5)/h;
    }else if(camera.isOrthographicCamera){
      worldPerPixel=Math.abs((camera.top-camera.bottom)/(camera.zoom||1))/h;
    }
    const sc=Math.max(.001,worldPerPixel*px);
    obj.scale.setScalar(sc);
  },

  create(type,opts={}){
    type=LightTypes.includes(type)?type:'point';
    const d=LightDefaults[type]||LightDefaults.point;
    const size=Math.max(.01,Number(opts.size??d.size??25));
    const geometry=this.iconGeometry(type);
    const color=new THREE.Color(opts.color||'#fff2c8');
    const material=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
      color:new THREE.Color('#aeb4bc'),transparent:true,opacity:.82,
      depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,toneMapped:false
    });
    const obj=new THREE.LineSegments(geometry,material);
    obj.userData.lightViewportIcon={theme:'editor-neutral',pixels:22,version:'3.98.3'};
    obj.onBeforeRender=(renderer,_scene,camera)=>this.updateViewportIconScale(obj,camera,renderer);
    obj.renderOrder=1000;
    obj.name=(type[0].toUpperCase()+type.slice(1))+' Light '+this.nextId++;
    obj.userData.renderLight={
      id:'light-'+Date.now().toString(36)+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),
      type,
      enabled:opts.enabled!==false,
      color:'#'+color.getHexString(),
      intensity:Math.max(0,Number(opts.intensity??d.intensity??10)),
      range:Math.max(0,Number(opts.range??d.range??0)),
      size,
      width:Math.max(.01,Number(opts.width??d.width??size*2)),
      height:Math.max(.01,Number(opts.height??d.height??size*2)),
      coneAngle:Math.max(1,Math.min(179,Number(opts.coneAngle??d.coneAngle??45))),
      penumbra:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(opts.penumbra??d.penumbra??.15))),
      castShadows:opts.castShadows!==false,
      renderOnly:true
    };
    obj.userData.isRenderLight=true;
    obj.position.set(0,0,Math.max(20,size));
    scene.add(obj);objects.push(obj);
    window.__3DLiteLightRevision=(window.__3DLiteLightRevision||0)+1;
    selectOnly(obj);
    refreshModifyPanel();
    requestRender();
    return obj;
  },

  updateIcon(
-- hit 4 line 7064 --
rea(light){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'||light.type==='mesh')return Math.max(this.minArea,light.width*light.height);
    if(light.type==='disc')return Math.max(this.minArea,Math.PI*Math.pow(light.size*.5,2));
    if(light.type==='sphere')return Math.max(this.minArea,4*Math.PI*Math.pow(light.size*.5,2));
    return 1;
  }
});

class RenderSceneLight{
  constructor(snapshot){
    this.id=String(snapshot.id||'light');
    this.name=String(snapshot.name||'Light');
    this.type=RenderLightPolicy.type(snapshot.type);
    this.enabled=snapshot.enabled!==false;
    this.color=Object.freeze(RenderColor.hexToLinear(snapshot.color||'#ffffff'));
    this.intensity=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.intensity,0,0,1e12);
    this.range=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.range,0,0,1e12);
    this.size=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.size,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.width=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.width,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.height=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.height,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.coneAngle=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.coneAngle,45,1,179)*Math.PI/180;
    this.penumbra=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.penumbra,0,0,1);
    this.castShadows=snapshot.castShadows!==false;
    this.position=Object.freeze(RenderLightPolicy.finite3(snapshot.position,[0,0,0]));
    const rawDir=RenderLightPolicy.finite3(snapshot.direction,[0,0,-1]);
    let dir=RayMath.normalize(rawDir);
    if(!dir.every(Number.isFinite)||RayMath.length(dir)<1e-10)dir=[0,0,-1];
    this.direction=Object.freeze(dir);
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

var SceneLightCompiler=Object.freeze({
  compile(renderScene){
    const compiled=[];
    for(const snapshot of (renderScene?.lights||[])){
      if(snapshot?.enabled===false)continue;
      try{compiled.push(new RenderSceneLight(snapshot));}
      catch(err){throw new LightError(`${snapshot?.name||snapshot?.id||'Light'}: ${String(err?.message||err)}`,err);}
    }
    return Object.freeze(compiled);
  }
});

const DirectLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point){
    if(light.type==='dome'){
      return {kind:'ambient',direction:null,distance:Infinity,radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light)};
    }
    if(light.type==='sun'){
      return {kind:'directional',direction:RayMath.mul(light.direction,-1),distance:Infinity,radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light)};
    }

    const to=RayMath.sub(light.position,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-4);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);
    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);
    if(attenuation<=0)return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0]};

    if(light.type==='spot'){
      const fromLight=RayMath.mul(dir,-1);
      const cosTheta=RayMath.dot(light.direction,fromLight);
      const outer=Math.cos(light.coneAngle*.5);
      const inner=Math.cos(light.coneAngle*.5*(1-light.penumbra));
      const cone=inner<=outer?(cosTheta>=outer?1:0):Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(cosTheta-outer)/(inner-outer)));
      if(cone<=0)return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0]};
      attenuation*=cone;
    }

    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation)
    };
  }
});

function updateSelectedLightPanel(){
  const section=document.getElementById('lightModifySection');
  if(!section)return;
  const obj=selected;
  const isLight=EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(obj);
  section.style.display=isLight?'':'none';
  if(!isLight)return;

  const l=obj.userData.renderLight;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v};
  set('lightTypeReadout',l.type);
  set('lightIntensity',l.intensity);
  set('lightColor',l.color);
  set('lightRange',l.range);
  set('lightSize',l.size);
  set('lightWidth',l.width);
  set('lightHeight',l.height);
  set('lightConeAngle',l.coneAngle);
  set('lightPenumbra',l.penumbra);
  const en=document.getElementById('lightEnabled');if(en)en.checked=l.enabled!==false;
  const sh=document.getElementById('lightCastShadows');if(sh)sh.checked=l.castShadows!==false;
}

function markRenderLightDirty(){
  window.__3DLiteLightRevision=(window.__3DLiteLightRevision||0)+1;
  requestRender();
}

function bindLightUI(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-light-create]').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      EditorLightSystem.create(btn.dataset.lightCreate,{
        intensity:Number(document.getElementById('newLightIntensity')?.value)||10,
        color:document.getElementById('newLightColor')?.value||'#ffffff',
        size:Number(document.getElementById('newLightSize')?.value)||25,
        range:Number(document.getElementById('newLightRange')?.value)||0
      });
    });
  });

  const bindings=[
    ['lightIntensity','intensity','number'],
    ['lightColor','color','string'],
    ['lightRange','range','number'],
    ['lightSize','size','number'],
    ['lightWidth','width','number'],
    ['lightHeight','height','number'],
    ['lightConeAngle','coneAngle','number'],
    ['lightPenumbra','penumbra','number']
  ];

  for(const [id,key,kind] of bindings){
    document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
      if(!EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected))return;
      selected.userData.renderLight[key]=kind==='number'?Number(e.target.value):e.target.value;
      /* v3.98.3 viewport icon is independent of physical size/color */
      markRenderLightDirty();
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('lightEnabled')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected)){
      selected.userData.renderLight.enabled=!!e.target.checked;
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
  document.getElementById('lightCastShadows')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected)){
      selected.userData.renderLight.castShadows=!!e.target.checked;
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
}

var SceneLightCompiler=Object.freeze({
  compile(renderScene){
    const compiled=[];
    for(const snapshot of (renderScene?.lights||[])){
      if(snapshot?.enabled===false)continue;
      try{compiled.push(new RenderSceneLight(snapshot));}
      catch(err){throw new LightError(`${snapshot?.name||snapshot?.id||'Light'}: ${String(err?.message||err)}`,err);}
    }
    return Object.freeze(compiled);
  }
});

const DirectLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point){
    if(light.type==='dome'){
      return {kind:'ambient',direction:null,distance:Infinity,radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light)};
    }
    if(light.type==='sun'){
      return {kind:'directional',direction:RayMath.mul(light.direction,-1),distance:Infinity,radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light)};
    }

    const to=RayMath.sub(light.position,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-4);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);
    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);
    if(attenuation<=0)return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0]};

    if(light.type==='spot'){
      const fromLight=RayMath.mul(dir,-1);
      const cosTheta=RayMath.dot(light.direction,fromLight);
      const outer=Math.cos(light.coneAngle*.5);
      const inner=Math.cos(light.coneAngle*.5*(1-light.penumbra));
      const cone=inner<=outer?(cosTheta>=outer?1:0):Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(cosTheta-outer)/(inner-outer)));
      if(cone<=0)return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0]};
      attenuation*=cone;
    }

    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation)
    };
  }
});

function updateSelectedLightPanel(){
  const section=document.getElementById('lightModifySection');
  if(!section)return;
  const obj=selected;
  const isLight=EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(obj);
  section.style.display=isLight?'':'none';
  if(!isLight)return;

  const l=obj.userData.renderLight;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v};
  set('lightTypeReadout',l.type);
  set('lightIntensity',l.intensity);
  set('lightColor',l.color);
  set('lightRange',l.range);
  set('lightSize',l.size);
  set('lightWidth',l.width);
  set('lightHeight',l.height);
  set('lightConeAngle',l.coneAngle);
  set('lightPenumbra',l.penumbra);
  const en=document.getElementById('lightEnabled');if(en)en.checked=l.enabled!==false;
  const sh=document.getElementById('lightCastShadows');if(sh)sh.checked=l.castShadows!==false;
}

function markRenderLightDirty(){
  window.__3DLiteLightRevision=(window.__3DLiteLightRevision||0)+1;
  requestRender();
}

function bindLightUI(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-light-create]').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      EditorLightSystem.create(btn.dataset.lightCreate,{
        intensity:Number(document.getElementById('newLightIntensity')?.value)||10,
        color:document.getElementById('newLightColor')?.value||'#ffffff',
        size:Number(document.getElementById('newLightSize')?.value)||25,
        range:Number(document.getElementById('newLightRange')?.value)||0
      });
    });
  });

  const bindings=[
    ['lightIntensity','intensity','number'],
    ['lightColor','color','string'],
    ['lightRange','range','number'],
    ['lightSize','size','number'],
    ['lightWidth','width','number'],
    ['lightHeight','height','number'],
    ['lightConeAngle','coneAngle','number'],
    ['lightPenumbra','penumbra','number']
  ];

  for(const [id,key,kind] of bindings){
    document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
      if(!EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected))return;
      selected.userData.renderLight[key]=kind==='number'?Number(e.target.value):e.target.value;
      /* v3.98.3 viewport icon is independent of physical size/color */
      markRenderLightDirty();
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('lightEnabled')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected)){
      selected.userData.renderLight.enabled=!!e.target.checked;
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
  document.getElementById('lightCastShadows')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected)){
      selected.userData.renderLight.castShadows=!!e.target.checked;
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
}

function runRenderGroup6SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const point=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'p',name:'p',type:'point',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
    range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:0,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });

  if(ps.kind!=='local'||Math.abs(ps.distance-10)>1e-5||ps.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Point light sample failed');

  const sun=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'s',name:'s',type:'sun',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:1,
    range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:.53,penumbra:0,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,0],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const ss=DirectLightSampler.sample(sun,[0,0,0]);
  if(ss.kind!=='directional'||Math.abs(ss.direction[2]-1)>1e-5)errors.push('Sun direction sample failed');

  const spot=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'sp',name:'sp',type:'spot',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
    range:100,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:.2,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const sp=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[0,0,0]);
  if(sp.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)||sp.radiance.every(x=>x<=0))errors.push('Spot light on-axis sample failed');
  const spOutside=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[10,0,0]);
  if(spOutside.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x))||spOutside.radiance.some(x=>x!==0))errors.push('Spot light outside-cone cutoff failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderSampling=Object.freeze({
  hash32(x){
    x=(x|0)+0x6D2B79F5|0;
    x=Math.imul(x^(x>>>15),x|1);
    x^=x+Math.imul(x^(x>>>7),x|61);
    return ((x^(x>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296;
  },
  sample2D(seed,index){
    return [
      this.hash32((seed*73856093)^(index*19349663)),
      this.hash32((seed*83492791)^(index*2971215073))
    ];
  },
  concentricDisk(u1,u2){
    const sx=2*u1-1,sy=2*u2-1;
    if(sx===0&&sy===0)return [0,0];
    let r,theta;
    if(Math.abs(sx)>Math.abs(sy)){
      r=sx;theta=(Math.PI/4)*(sy/sx);
    }else{
      r=sy;theta=(Math.PI/2)-(Math.PI/4)*(sx/sy);
    }
    return [r*Math.cos(theta),r*Math.sin(theta)];
  }
});

const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;

        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.pos

  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){

    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-d',

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){
    return EnvironmentSampler.background(
      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    return PathIntegrator.trace(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit
    );
  },

  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    return ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance);
  },

  validate(){

    const g7=validateRenderGroup7();if(!g7.ok)errors.push(...g7.errors);
    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);
    if(typeof this.evaluateSurfaceDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core D direct evaluator missing');
    if(typeof this.tracePath!=='function')errors.push('Core D path tracer missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateEnvironment!=='function')errors.push('Core D environment evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

function validateRenderGroup5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSurfaceMaterial!=='function')errors.push('RenderSurfaceMaterial missing');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;
window.__3DLiteLightEnergy3983=true;
window.__3DLiteLightDefaultDirection3983=true;
window.ThreeDLiteLightEnergy3983=Object.freeze({version:'3.99.0',baselineIntensity:1,referenceDistance:100});

var SceneLightCompiler=Object.freeze({
  compile(renderScene){
    const compiled=[];
    for(const snapshot of (renderScene?.lights||[])){
      if(snapshot?.enabled===false)continue;
      try{compiled.push(new RenderSceneLight(snapshot));}
      catch(err){throw new LightError(`${snapshot?.name||snapshot?.id||'Light'}: ${String(err?.message||err)}`,err);}
    }
    return Object.freeze(compiled);
  }
});

const DirectLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point){
    if(light.type==='dome'){
      return {kind:'ambient',direction:null,distance:Infinity,radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light)};
    }
    if(light.type==='sun'){
      return {kind:'directional',direction:RayMath.mul(light.direction,-1),distance:Infinity,radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light)};
    }

    const to=RayMath.sub(light.position,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-4);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);
    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);
    if(attenuation<=0)return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0]};

    if(light.type==='spot'){
      const fromLight=RayMath.mul(dir,-1);
      const cosTheta=RayMath.dot(light.direction,fromLight);
      const outer=Math.cos(light.coneAngle*.5);
      const inner=Math.cos(light.coneAngle*.5*(1-light.penumbra));
      const cone=inner<=outer?(cosTheta>=outer?1:0):Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(cosTheta-outer)/(inner-outer)));
      if(cone<=0)return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0]};
      attenuation*=cone;
    }

    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation)
    };
  }
});

function updateSelectedLightPanel(){
  const section=document.getElementById('lightModifySection');
  if(!section)return;
  const obj=selected;
  const isLight=EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(obj);
  section.style.display=isLight?'':'none';
  if(!isLight)return;

  const l=obj.userData.renderLight;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v};
  set('lightTypeReadout',l.type);
  set('lightIntensity',l.intensity);
  set('lightColor',l.color);
  set('lightRange',l.range);
  set('lightSize',l.size);
  set('lightWidth',l.width);
  set('lightHeight',l.height);
  set('lightConeAngle',l.coneAngle);
  set('lightPenumbra',l.penumbra);
  const en=document.getElementById('lightEnabled');if(en)en.checked=l.enabled!==false;
  const sh=document.getElementById('lightCastShadows');if(sh)sh.checked=l.castShadows!==false;
}

function markRenderLightDirty(){
  window.__3DLiteLightRevision=(window.__3DLiteLightRevision||0)+1;
  requestRender();
}

function bindLightUI(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-light-create]').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      EditorLightSystem.create(btn.dataset.lightCreate,{
        intensity:Number(document.getElementById('newLightIntensity')?.value)||10,
        color:document.getElementById('newLightColor')?.value||'#ffffff',
        size:Number(document.getElementById('newLightSize')?.value)||25,
        range:Number(document.getElementById('newLightRange')?.value)||0
      });
    });
  });

  const bindings=[
    ['lightIntensity','intensity','number'],
    ['lightColor','color','string'],
    ['lightRange','range','number'],
    ['lightSize','size','number'],
    ['lightWidth','width','number'],
    ['lightHeight','height','number'],
    ['lightConeAngle','coneAngle','number'],
    ['lightPenumbra','penumbra','number']
  ];

  for(const [id,key,kind] of bindings){
    document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
      if(!EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected))return;
      selected.userData.renderLight[key]=kind==='number'?Number(e.target.value):e.target.value;
      /* v3.98.3 viewport icon is independent of physical size/color */
      markRenderLightDirty();
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('lightEnabled')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected)){
      selected.userData.renderLight.enabled=!!e.target.checked;
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
  document.getElementById('lightCastShadows')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{
    if(EditorLightSystem.isLightObject(selected)){
      selected.userData.renderLight.castShadows=!!e.target.checked;
      markRenderLightDirty();
    }
  });
}

function runRenderGroup6SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const point=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'p',name:'p',type:'point',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
    range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:0,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const ps=DirectLightSampler.sample(point,[0,0,0]);
  if(ps.kind!=='local'||Math.abs(ps.distance-10)>1e-5||ps.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Point light sample failed');

  const sun=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'s',name:'s',type:'sun',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:1,
    range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:.53,penumbra:0,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,0],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const ss=DirectLightSampler.sample(sun,[0,0,0]);
  if(ss.kind!=='directional'||Math.abs(ss.direction[2]-1)>1e-5)errors.push('Sun direction sample failed');

  const spot=new RenderSceneLight({
    id:'sp',name:'sp',type:'spot',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
    range:100,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:.2,
    castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
  });
  const sp=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[0,0,0]);

ht);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — CORE D
   CLEAN GROUP 7 + GROUP 8 CONSOLIDATION
   ============================================================ */

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-d',

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){
    return EnvironmentSampler.background(
      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    return PathIntegrator.trace(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit
    );
  },

  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    return ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g7=validateRenderGroup7();if(!g7.ok)errors.push(...g7.errors);
    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);
    if(typeof this.evaluateSurfaceDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core D direct evaluator missing');
    if(typeof this.tracePath!=='function')errors.push('Core D path tracer missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateEnvironment!=='function')errors.push('Core D environment evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreD=RenderCoreD

function validateRenderGroup12(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderAOVSet!=='function')errors.push('RenderAOVSet missing');
  if(typeof RenderAOVUtil?.primary!=='function')errors.push('RenderAOV primary extraction missing');

}

  names:RenderAOVNames,
  create(width,height){return new RenderAOVSet(width,height);}
});
Object.assign(window,{
  RenderAOVNames,RenderAOVSet,RenderAOVUtil,
  RenderGroup12Contract,runRenderGroup12SelfTests,validateRenderGroup12,RenderGroup12Core
});


/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.79.28.2 — GROUP 13 GEN3 REWRITE
   DENOISING + TONE MAPPING
   Fresh implementation built directly on Group 12 AOVs.
   No editor startup, viewport, selection or modeling ownership.
   ============================================================ */
const RenderGroup13Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'renderer-only denoising',
    'final framebuffer tone mapping',
    'exposure',
    'Denoised Beauty presentation'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze(['Beauty','Albedo','Normal','Depth']),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor startup','viewport','scene objects','selection','transforms',
    'render geometry','BVH','materials','lights','path transport'
  ])
});

const RenderToneMapper13=Object.freeze({
  map(v,mode='ACES'){
    const x=Math.max(0,Number(v)||0);
    if(mode==='Linear')return Math.min(1,x);
    if(mode==='Reinhard')return x/(1+x);
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(x*(2.51*x+.03))/(x*(2.43*x+.59)+.14)));
  },
  srgb(v){
    const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,v));
    return x<=.0031308?12.92*x:1.055*Math.pow(x,1/2.4)-.055;
  },
  rgb(c,settings={}){
    const gain=Math.pow(2,Math.max(-10,Math.min(10,Number(settings.exposure)||0)));
    const mode=['ACES','Reinhard','Linear'].includes(settings.toneMapping)?settings.toneMapping:'ACES';
    return [
      this.srgb(this.map((c?.[0]||0)*gain,mode)),
      this.srgb(this.map((c?.[1]||0)*gain,mode)),
      this.srgb(this.map((c?.[2]||0)*gain,mode))
    ];
  }
});

const RenderDenoiser13=Object.freeze({
  process(channels,mode='Off'){
    const beauty=channels?.get?.('Beauty');
    if(!beauty)return null;
    const w=channels.width|0,h=channels.height|0;
    if(w<1||h<1)return null;
    if(mode==='Off')return new Float32Array(beauty);

    const albedo=channels.get('Albedo');
    const normal=channels.get('Normal');
    const depth=channels.get('Depth');
    const radius=mode==='Quality'?2:1;
    const out=new Float32Array(beauty.length);

    const colorDist=(a,b)=>{
      const dr=beauty[a*3]-beauty[b*3],dg=beauty[a*3+1]-beauty[b*3+1],db=beauty[a*3+2]-beauty[b*3+2];
      return dr*dr+dg*dg+db*db;
    };
    const guideWeight=(a,b)=>{
      let wgt=1;
      if(albedo){
        const dr=albedo[a*3]-albedo[b*3],dg=albedo[a*3+1]-albedo[b*3+1],db=albedo[a*3+2]-albedo[b*3+2];
        wgt*=Math.exp(-(dr*dr+dg*dg+db*db)*8);
      }
      if(normal){
        const d=normal[a*3]*normal[b*3]+normal[a*3+1]*normal[b*3+1]+normal[a*3+2]*normal[b*3+2];
        wgt*=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(d-.5)*2));
      }
      if(depth){
        const da=depth[a],db=depth[b],scale=Math.max(1e-4,Math.max(Math.abs(da),Math.abs(db)));
        wgt*=Math.exp(-Math.abs(da-db)/(scale*.03+1e-4));
      }
      return wgt;
    };

    for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
      for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
        const i=y*w+x;
        let r=0,g=0,b=0,sum=0;
        for(let oy=-radius;oy<=radius;oy++){
          const yy=Math.max(0,Math.min(h-1,y+oy));
          for(let ox=-radius;ox<=radius;ox++){
            const xx=Math.max(0,Math.min(w-1,x+ox)),j=yy*w+xx;
            const spatial=Math.exp(-(ox*ox+oy*oy)/(radius===2?3:1.5));
            const edge=Math.exp(-colorDist(i,j)*2.5);
            const wt=Math.max(1e-8,spatial*edge*guideWeight(i,j));
            r+=beauty[j*3]*wt;g+=beauty[j*3+1]*wt;b+=beauty[j*3+2]*wt;sum+=wt;
          }
        }
        const inv=1/Math.max(1e-8,sum);
        out[i*3]=r*inv;out[i*3+1]=g*inv;out[i*3+2]=b*inv;
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
});

const RenderPresenter13=Object.freeze({
  linearFrame(data,width,height,settings,metadata={}){
    if(!data||data.length!==width*height*3)return null;
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<width*height;i++){
      const c=RenderToneMapper13.rgb([data[i*3],data[i*3+1],data[i*3+2]],settings);
      rgba[i*4]=Math.round(c[0]*255);
      rgba[i*4+1]=Math.round(c[1]*255);
      rgba[i*4+2]=Math.round(c[2]*255);
      rgba[i*4+3]=255;
    }
    return {width,height,rgba,metadata:Object.freeze({...metadata})};
  },
  frame(job,name){
    const channels=job?.renderChannels||job?.beautyFrame?.channels;
    if(!channels)return name==='Beauty'?job?.beautyFrame||null:null;
    const settings=job.settings||{};
    if(name==='Beauty'){
      return this.linearFrame(channels.get('Beauty'),channels.width,channels.height,settings,{channel:'Beauty',group13:true});
    }
    if(name==='Denoised Beauty'){
      const data=RenderDenoiser13.process(channels,settings.denoise||'Off');
      return this.linearFrame(data,channels.width,channels.height,settings,{channel:'Denoised Beauty',denoise:settings.denoise||'Off',group13:true});
    }
    return channels.toFrame(name,{
      depthMax:job?.renderScene?.camera?.far||100,
      noiseMax:settings.noiseThreshold||.1
    });
  }
});

function runRenderGroup13SelfTests(){
  const errors=[],expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};
  try{
    const t=RenderToneMapper13.rgb([1,.5,.25],{toneMapping:'ACES',exposure:0});
    expect(t.length===3&&t.every(Number.isFinite),'Tone mapper output invalid');

    const a=new RenderAOVSet(2,1);
    a.set3('Beauty',0,[1,0,0]);a.set3('Beauty',1,[0,1,0]);
    a.set3('Albedo',0,[1,0,0]);a.set3('Albedo',1,[0,1,0]);
    a.set3('Normal',0,[0,0,1]);a.set3('Normal',1,[0,0,1]);
    a.set1('Depth',0,1);a.set1('Depth',1,1);
    const d=RenderDenoiser13.process(a,'Fast');
    expect(d?.length===6&&d.every(Number.isFinite),'Denoiser output invalid');

    const f=RenderPresenter13.linearFrame(a.get('Beauty'),2,1,{toneMapping:'Linear',exposure:0});
    expect(f?.rgba?.length===8,'Group13 framebuffer presentation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push(String(e?.message||e));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

var RenderRegion14=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(region){

    const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.x)||0));
    const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.y)||0));
    const width=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-x,Number(region.width)||0));
    const height=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-y,Number(region.height)||0));
    if(width<1e-4||height<1e-4)return null;
    return Object.freeze({x,y,width,height});
  },

  resolve(job,maxWidth=640,maxHeight=360){

    const sourceH=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||1);
    const scale=Math.min(1,maxWidth/sourceW,maxHeight/sourceH);

    const fullH=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceH*scale));

      return Object.freeze({
        region:false,fullW,fullH,
        x0:0,y0:0,width:fullW,height:fullH,
        normalized:null
      });

    const y0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullH-1,Math.floor(r.y*fullH)));
    const x1=Math.max(x0+1,Math.min(fullW,Math.ceil((r.x+r.width)*fullW)));
    const y1=Math.max(y0+1,Math.min(fullH,Math.ceil((r.y+r.height)*fullH)));

    return Object.freeze({
      region:true,fullW,fullH,x0,y0,
      width:x1-x0,height:y1-y0,
      normalized:r
    });
  },

var RenderRegion14=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(region){
    if(!region)return null;
    const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.x)||0));
    const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.y)||0));
    const width=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-x,Number(region.width)||0));
    const height=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-y,Number(region.height)||0));
    if(width<1e-4||height<1e-4)return null;
    return Object.freeze({x,y,width,height});
  },

  resolve(job,maxWidth=640,maxHeight=360){
    const sourceW=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||1);
    const sourceH=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||1);
    const scale=Math.min(1,maxWidth/sourceW,maxHeight/sourceH);
    const fullW=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceW*scale));
    const fullH=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceH*scale));
    const r=this.sanitize(job?.region);

    if(!r){
      return Object.freeze({
        region:false,fullW,fullH,
        x0:0,y0:0,width:fullW,height:fullH,
        normalized:null
      });
    }

    const x0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullW-1,Math.floor(r.x*fullW)));
    const y0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullH-1,Math.floor(r.y*fullH)));
    const x1=Math.max(x0+1,Math.min(fullW,Math.ceil((r.x+r.width)*fullW)));
    const y1=Math.max(y0+1,Math.min(fullH,Math.ceil((r.y+r.height)*fullH)));

    return Object.freeze({
      region:true,fullW,fullH,x0,y0,
      width:x1-x0,height:y1-y0,
      normalized:r
    });
  },

  mapNested(base,local){
    const b=this.sanitize(base);
    const l=this.sanitize(local);
    if(!l)return null;
    if(!b)return l;
    return this.sanitize({
      x:b.x+l.x*b.width,
      y:b.y+l.y*b.height,
      width:l.width*b.width,
      height:l.height*b.height

/* ============================================================
   3DLite / LiteTrace — GROUP 14 GEN3 REWRITE
   RENDER REGION + RENDER LAST + SAVE
   Fresh implementation built on browser-confirmed Group 13.
   Does not own editor startup, viewport or modeling.
   ============================================================ */

const RenderGroup14Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'normalized render-region policy',
    'region-to-camera pixel mapping',
    'last successful render state',
    'render-last replay',
    'framebuffer save naming',
    'interactive render-region selection'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Group 13 final presentation',
    'Group 12 AOV buffers',
    'existing Fast renderer',
    'existing Path renderer',
    'existing framebuffer'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor startup','viewport','selection','transforms','mesh editing',
    'scene evaluation','BVH','materials','lights','path transport'
  ])
});

var RenderRegion14=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(region){
    if(!region)return null;
    const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.x)||0));
    const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.y)||0));
    const width=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-x,Number(region.width)||0));
    const height=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-y,Number(region.height)||0));
    if(width<1e-4||height<1e-4)return null;
    return Object.freeze({x,y,width,height});
  },

  resolve(job,maxWidth=640,maxHeight=360){
    const sourceW=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||1);
    const sourceH=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||1);
    const scale=Math.min(1,maxWidth/sourceW,maxHeight/sourceH);
    const fullW=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceW*scale));
    const fullH=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceH*scale));
    const r=this.sanitize(job?.region);

    if(!r){
      return Object.freeze({
        region:false,fullW,fullH,
        x0:0,y0:0,width:fullW,height:fullH,
        normalized:null
      });
    }

    const x0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullW-1,Math.floor(r.x*fullW)));
    const y0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullH-1,Math.floor(r.y*fullH)));
    const x1=Math.max(x0+1,Math.min(fullW,Math.ceil((r.x+r.width)*fullW)));
    const y1=Math.max(y0+1,Math.min(fullH,Math.ceil((r.y+r.height)*fullH)));

    return Object.freeze({
      region:true,fullW,fullH,x0,y0,
      width:x1-x0,height:y1-y0,
      normalized:r
    });
  },

  mapNested(base,local){
    const b=this.sanitize(base);
    const l=this.sanitize(local);
    if(!l)return null;
    if(!b)return l;
    return this.sanitize({
      x:b.x+l.x*b.width,
      y:b.y+l.y*b.height,
      width:l.width*b.width,
      height:l.height*b.height
    });
  }
});

    expect(rr.x0>=0&&rr.y0>=0&&rr.x0+rr.width<=rr.fullW&&rr.y0+rr.height<=rr.fullH,'Region bounds invalid');

    const nested=RenderRegion14.mapNested(
      {x:.25,y:.25,width:.5,height:.5},
      {x:.5,y:.5,width:.5,height:.5}
    );
    expect(nested&&Math.abs(nested.x-.5)<1e-9&&Math.abs(nested.y-.5)<1e-9,'Nested region mapping failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push(String(e?.message||e));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  if(typeof RenderRegion14?.resolve!=='function')errors.push('RenderRegion14 resolve missing');

var RenderProfiler16=Object.freeze({
  now(){return performance.now();},
  begin(){return performance.now();},
  elapsed(start){return Math.max(0,performance.now()-start);},
  mark(counter,name,start){
    const ms=this.elapsed(start);
    if(counter?.stageMs&&name in counter.stageMs)counter.stageMs[name]+=ms;
    return ms;
  }
});
window.RenderProfiler16=RenderProfiler16;

var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
  constructor(){
    this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
    this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
    this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
    this.flags[2]=1;
  }
  cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
  reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
  pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
  resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
  cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
  paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}
window.RenderControlToken16=RenderControlToken16;

var RenderPerformanceCounters16 = class RenderPerformanceCounters16{
  constructor(){
    this.cameraRays=0;
    this.pathRays=0;
    this.shadowRays=0;
    this.probeRays=0;
    this.samples=0;
    this.yields=0;
    this.stageMs={snapshot:0,geometry:0,worker:0,bvh:0,shading:0,lightCache:0,render:0,denoise:0,toneMap:0,post:0};
    this.startedAt=RenderProfiler16.now();
  }
  addRay(type){
    if(type===RenderRayType.CAMERA)this.cameraRays++;
    else if(type===RenderRayType.SHADOW)this.shadowRays++;
    else this.pathRays++;
  }
  addProbe(){this.probeRays++;}
  markStage(name,start){
    return RenderProfiler16.mark(this,name,start);
  }
  snapshot(){
    const elapsed=Math.max(.001,performance.now()-this.startedAt);
    const totalRays=this.cameraRays+this.pathRays;
    return Object.freeze({
      cameraRays:this.cameraRays,
      pathRays:this.pathRays,
      shadowRays:this.shadowRays,
      probeRays:this.probeRays,
      totalRays:totalRays+this.shadowRays+this.probeRays,
      samples:this.samples,
      yields:this.yields,
      raysPerSecond:Math.round(totalRays/(elapsed/1000)),
      stageMs:Object.freeze({...this.stageMs})
    });
  }
}

const RenderPerformanceRuntime16=Object.freeze({
  attach(job){
    if(!job.performanceState)job.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    return job.performanceState;
  },
  markStage(job,name,start){
    this.attach(job).markStage(name,start);
  },
  snapshot(job){
    return job?.performanceState?.snapshot?.()||null;
  }
});

function runRenderGroup16SelfTests(){
  const errors=[],expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};
  try{
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'8'}})===8,'Tile size 8 failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'64'}})===64,'Tile size 64 failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'Auto',quality:'Draft'}})===32,'Auto Draft tile failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.yieldRows({settings:{tileSize:'16'}})===4,'Yield-row policy failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Performance policy: '+String(e?.message||e));}
  try{
    const c=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    c.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
    c.addRay(RenderRayType.DIFFUSE);
    c.samples=2;
    const snap=c.snapshot();
    expect(snap.totalRays===2&&snap.cameraRays===1&&snap.pathRays===1,'Ray counters failed');
    expect(snap.samples===2&&Number.isFinite(snap.raysPerSecond),'Sample/RPS counters failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Performance counters: '+String(e?.message||e));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

var RenderRayBatch16 = class RenderRayBatch16{
  constructor(capacity=128){
    this.capacity=Math.max(1,capacity|0);
    this.origins=new Float64Array(this.capacity*3);
    this.directions=new Float64Array(this.capacity*3);
    this.pixelIndices=new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.seeds=new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.count=0;
  }
  clear(){this.count=0;return this;}
  push(ray,pixelIndex,seed){
    if(this.count>=this.capacity)return false;
    const i=this.count++,o=i*3;
    this.origins[o]=ray.origin[0];this.origins[o+1]=ray.origin[1];this.origins[o+2]=ray.origin[2];
    this.directions[o]=ray.direction[0];this.directions[o+1]=ray.direction[1];this.directions[o+2]=ray.direction[2];
    this.pixelIndices[i]=pixelIndex>>>0;this.seeds[i]=seed>>>0;
    return true;
  }
  ray(i,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA){
    const o=i*3;
    return new RenderRay(
      [this.origins[o],this.origins[o+1],this.origins[o+2]],
      [this.directions[o],this.directions[o+1],this.directions[o+2]],
      1e-6,Infinity,type,0
    );
  }
  trace(acceleration,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA){
    const hits=new Array(this.count);
    for(let i=0;i<this.count;i++)hits[i]=acceleration.trace(this.ray(i,type));
    return hits;
  }
}
window.RenderRayBatch16=RenderRayBatch16;

function runRenderGroup5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'t',name:'t',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.3,.2],
    roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(brdf.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Physical BRDF finite/positive test failed');

  const metallic=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'m',name:'m',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.9,.7,.2],
    roughness:.25,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });

  const tm=RenderColor.aces([10,3,1]);

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  updateViewportIconScale(obj,camera,renderer){

      worldPerPixel=Math.abs((camera.top-camera.bottom)/(camera.zoom||1))/h;

    const q=obj.getWorldQuaternion(new THREE.Quaternion());
    const p=obj.getWorldPosition(new THREE.Vector3());
    const dir=new THREE.Vector3(0,0,-1).applyQuaternion(q).normalize();
    return {
      id:l.id||obj.uuid,
      name:obj.name||'Light',
      type:l.type,
      enabled:l.enabled!==false&&obj.visible!==false,
      color:l.color||'#ffffff',

      castShadows:l.castShadows!==false,
      position:p.toArray(),
      quaternion:q.toArray(),
      direction:dir.toArray()
    };
  }
};

const RenderLightPolicy=Object.freeze({
  supported:Object.freeze(['point','spot','rectangle','disc','sphere','sun','dome','mesh']),
  minLinearSize:0.01,
  minArea:1e-4,
  finite3(a,fallback=[0,0,0]){
    if(!Array.isArray(a)||a.length<3||a.slice(0,3).some(v=>!Number.isFinite(Number(v))))return fallback.slice();
    return a.slice(0,3).map(Number);
  },
  type(t){return this.supported.includes(t)?t:'point';},
  scalar(v,fallback=0,min=-Infinity,max=Infinity){
    v=Number(v);if(!Number.isFinite(v))v=fallback;
    return Math.max(min,Math.min(max,v));
  },
  area(light){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'||light.type==='mesh')return Math.max(this.minArea,light.width*light.height);
    if(light.type==='disc')return Math.max(this.minArea,Math.PI*Math.pow(light.size*.5,2));
    if(light.type==='sphere')return Math.max(this.minArea,4*Math.PI*Math.pow(light.size*.5,2));
    return 1;
  }
});

class RenderSceneLight{
  constructor(snapshot){
    this.id=String(snapshot.id||'light');
    this.name=String(snapshot.name||'Light');
    this.type=RenderLightPolicy.type(snapshot.type);
    this.enabled=snapshot.enabled!==false;
    this.color=Object.freeze(RenderColor.hexToLinear(snapshot.color||'#ffffff'));
    this.intensity=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.intensity,0,0,1e12);
    this.range=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.range,0,0,1e12);
    this.size=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.size,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.width=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.width,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.height=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.height,1,RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize,1e9);
    this.coneAngle=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.coneAngle,45,1,179)*Math.PI/180;
    this.penumbra=RenderLightPolicy.scalar(snapshot.penumbra,0,0,1);
    this.castShadows=snapshot.castShadows!==false;
    this.position=Object.freeze(RenderLightPolicy.finite3(snapshot.position,[0,0,0]));
    const rawDir=RenderLightPolicy.finite3(snapshot.direction,[0,0,-1]);
    let dir=RayMath.normalize(rawDir);
    if(!dir.every(Number.isFinite)||RayMath.length(dir)<1e-10)dir=[0,0,-1];
    this.direction=Object.freeze(dir);
    Object.freeze(this);

-- hit 4 line 9172 --
t test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.79.28.2 — GROUP 13 GEN3 REWRITE
   DENOISING + TONE MAPPING
   Fresh implementation built directly on Group 12 AOVs.
   No editor startup, viewport, selection or modeling ownership.
   ============================================================ */
const RenderGroup13Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'renderer-only denoising',
    'final framebuffer tone mapping',
    'exposure',
    'Denoised Beauty presentation'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze(['Beauty','Albedo','Normal','Depth']),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor startup','viewport','scene objects','selection','transforms',
    'render geometry','BVH','materials','lights','path transport'
  ])
});

const RenderToneMapper13=Object.freeze({
  map(v,mode='ACES'){
    const x=Math.max(0,Number(v)||0);
    if(mode==='Linear')return Math.min(1,x);
    if(mode==='Reinhard')return x/(1+x);
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(x*(2.51*x+.03))/(x*(2.43*x+.59)+.14)));
  },
  srgb(v){
    const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,v));
    return x<=.0031308?12.92*x:1.055*Math.pow(x,1/2.4)-.055;
  },
  rgb(c,settings={}){
    const gain=Math.pow(2,Math.max(-10,Math.min(10,Number(settings.exposure)||0)));
    const mode=['ACES','Reinhard','Linear'].includes(settings.toneMapping)?settings.toneMapping:'ACES';
    return [
      this.srgb(this.map((c?.[0]||0)*gain,mode)),
      this.srgb(this.map((c?.[1]||0)*gain,mode)),
      this.srgb(this.map((c?.[2]||0)*gain,mode))
    ];
  }
});

const RenderDenoiser13=Object.freeze({
  process(channels,mode='Off'){
    const beauty=channels?.get?.('Beauty');
    if(!beauty)return null;
    const w=channels.width|0,h=channels.height|0;
    if(w<1||h<1)return null;
    if(mode==='Off')return new Float32Array(beauty);

    const albedo=channels.get('Albedo');
    const normal=channels.get('Normal');
    const depth=channels.get('Depth');
    const radius=mode==='Quality'?2:1;
    const out=new Float32Array(beauty.length);

    const colorDist=(a,b)=>{
      const dr=beauty[a*3]-beauty[b*3],dg=beauty[a*3+1]-beauty[b*3+1],db=beauty[a*3+2]-beauty[b*3+2];
      return dr*dr+dg*dg+db*db;
    };
    const guideWeight=(a,b)=>{
      let wgt=1;
      if(albedo){
        const dr=albedo[a*3]-albedo[b*3],dg=albedo[a*3+1]-albedo[b*3+1],db=albedo[a*3+2]-albedo[b*3+2];
        wgt*=Math.exp(-(dr*dr+dg*dg+db*db)*8);
      }
      if(normal){
        const d=normal[a*3]*normal[b*3]+normal[a*3+1]*normal[b*3+1]+normal[a*3+2]*normal[b*3+2];
        wgt*=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(d-.5)*2));
      }
      if(depth){
        const da=depth[a],db=depth[b],scale=Math.max(1e-4,Math.max(Math.abs(da),Math.abs(db)));
        wgt*=Math.exp(-Math.abs(da-db)/(scale*.03+1e-4));
      }
      return wgt;
    };

    for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
      for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
        const i=y*w+x;
        let r=0,g=0,b=0,sum=0;
        for(let oy=-radius;oy<=radius;oy++){
          const yy=Math.max(0,Math.min(h-1,y+oy));
          for(let ox=-radius;ox<=radius;ox++){
            const xx=Math.max(0,Math.min(w-1,x+ox)),j=yy*w+xx;
            const spatial=Math.exp(-(ox*ox+oy*oy)/(radius===2?3:1.5));
            const edge=Math.exp(-colorDist(i,j)*2.5);
            const wt=Math.max(1e-8,spatial*edge*guideWeight(i,j));
            r+=beauty[j*3]*wt;g+=beauty[j*3+1]*wt;b+=beauty[j*3+2]*wt;sum+=wt;
          }
        }
        const inv=1/Math.max(1e-8,sum);
        out[i*3]=r*inv;out[i*3+1]=g*inv;out[i*3+2]=b*inv;
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
});

const RenderPresenter13=Object.freeze({
  linearFrame(data,width,height,settings,metadata={}){
    if(!data||data.length!==width*height*3)return null;
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<width*height;i++){
      const c=RenderToneMapper13.rgb([data[i*3],data[i*3+1],data[i*3+2]],settings);
      rgba[i*4]=Math.round(c[0]*255);
      rgba[i*4+1]=Math.round(c[1]*255);
      rgba[i*4+2]=Math.round(c[2]*255);
      rgba[i*4+3]=255;
    }
    return {width,height,rgba,metadata:Object.freeze({...metadata})};
  },
  frame(job,name){
    const channels=job?.renderChannels||job?.beautyFrame?.channels;
    if(!channels)return name==='Beauty'?job?.beautyFrame||null:null;
    const settings=job.settings||{};
    if(name==='Beauty'){
      return this.linearFrame(channels.get('Beauty'),channels.width,channels.height,settings,{channel:'Beauty',group13:true});
    }
    if(name==='Denoised Beauty'){
      const data=RenderDenoiser13.process(channels,settings.denoise||'Off');
      return this.linearFrame(data,channels.width,channels.height,settings,{channel:'Denoised Beauty',denoise:settings.denoise||'Off',group13:true});
    }
    return channels.toFrame(name,{
      depthMax:job?.renderScene?.camera?.far||100,
      noiseMax:settings.noiseThreshold||.1
    });
  }
});

function runRenderGroup13SelfTests(){
  const errors=[],expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};
  try{
    const t=RenderToneMapper13.rgb([1,.5,.25],{toneMapping:'ACES',exposure:0});
    expect(t.length===3&&t.every(Number.isFinite),'Tone mapper output invalid');

    const a=new RenderAOVSet(2,1);
    a.set3('Beauty',0,[1,0,0]);a.set3('Beauty',1,[0,1,0]);
    a.set3('Albedo',0,[1,0,0]);a.set3('Albedo',1,[0,1,0]);
    a.set3('Normal',0,[0,0,1]);a.set3('Normal',1,[0,0,1]);
    a.set1('Depth',0,1);a.set1('Depth',1,1);
    const d=RenderDenoiser13.process(a,'Fast');
    expect(d?.length===6&&d.every(Number.isFinite),'Denoiser output invalid');

    const f=RenderPresenter13.linearFrame(a.get('Beauty'),2,1,{toneMapping:'Linear',exposure:0});
    expect(f?.rgba?.length===8,'Group13 framebuffer presentation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push(String(e?.message||e));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup13(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderToneMapper13?.rgb!=='function')errors.push('Tone mapper missing');
  if(typeof RenderDenoiser13?.process!=='function')errors.push('Denoiser missing');
  if(typeof RenderPresenter13?.frame!=='function')errors.push('Presenter missing');
  const t=runRenderGroup13SelfTests();
  if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  denoise:RenderDenoiser13,
  toneMap:RenderToneMapper13,
  presenter:RenderPresenter13
});

  if(typeof RenderDenoiser13?.process!=='function')errors.push('Denoiser missing');

  const t=runRenderGroup13SelfTests();
  if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  version:'3.79.28.2-group13-rewrite-gen3',
  validate:validateRenderGroup13,
  denoise:RenderDenoiser13,
  toneMap:RenderToneMapper13,
  presenter:RenderPresenter13
