/* ============================================================
   3DLite / LiteTrace — GROUP 14 GEN3 REWRITE
   RENDER REGION + RENDER LAST + SAVE
   Fresh implementation built on browser-confirmed Group 13.
   Does not own editor startup, viewport or modeling.
   ============================================================ */

const RenderGroup14Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'normalized render-region policy',
    'region-to-camera pixel mapping',
    'last successful render state',
    'render-last replay',
    'framebuffer save naming',
    'interactive render-region selection'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Group 13 final presentation',
    'Group 12 AOV buffers',
    'existing Fast renderer',
    'existing Path renderer',
    'existing framebuffer'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor startup','viewport','selection','transforms','mesh editing',
    'scene evaluation','BVH','materials','lights','path transport'
  ])
});

var RenderRegion14=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(region){
    if(!region)return null;
    const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.x)||0));
    const y=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(region.y)||0));
    const width=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-x,Number(region.width)||0));
    const height=Math.max(0,Math.min(1-y,Number(region.height)||0));
    if(width<1e-4||height<1e-4)return null;
    return Object.freeze({x,y,width,height});
  },

  resolve(job,maxWidth=640,maxHeight=360){
    const sourceW=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||1);
    const sourceH=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||1);
    const scale=Math.min(1,maxWidth/sourceW,maxHeight/sourceH);
    const fullW=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceW*scale));
    const fullH=Math.max(1,Math.round(sourceH*scale));
    const r=this.sanitize(job?.region);

    if(!r){
      return Object.freeze({
        region:false,fullW,fullH,
        x0:0,y0:0,width:fullW,height:fullH,
        normalized:null
      });
    }

    const x0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullW-1,Math.floor(r.x*fullW)));
    const y0=Math.max(0,Math.min(fullH-1,Math.floor(r.y*fullH)));
    const x1=Math.max(x0+1,Math.min(fullW,Math.ceil((r.x+r.width)*fullW)));
    const y1=Math.max(y0+1,Math.min(fullH,Math.ceil((r.y+r.height)*fullH)));

    return Object.freeze({
      region:true,fullW,fullH,x0,y0,
      width:x1-x0,height:y1-y0,
      normalized:r
    });
  },

  mapNested(base,local){
    const b=this.sanitize(base);
    const l=this.sanitize(local);
    if(!l)return null;
    if(!b)return l;
    return this.sanitize({
      x:b.x+l.x*b.width,
      y:b.y+l.y*b.height,
      width:l.width*b.width,
      height:l.height*b.height
    });
  }
});

function runRenderGroup14SelfTests(){
  const errors=[],expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};
  try{
    const r=RenderRegion14.sanitize({x:.2,y:.25,width:.5,height:.5});
    expect(!!r&&Math.abs(r.x-.2)<1e-9&&Math.abs(r.width-.5)<1e-9,'Region sanitize failed');

    const fake={progress:{resolution:[1000,500]},region:{x:.25,y:.2,width:.5,height:.4}};
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(fake,640,360);
    expect(rr.region&&rr.width>0&&rr.height>0,'Region resolve failed');
    expect(rr.x0>=0&&rr.y0>=0&&rr.x0+rr.width<=rr.fullW&&rr.y0+rr.height<=rr.fullH,'Region bounds invalid');

    const nested=RenderRegion14.mapNested(
      {x:.25,y:.25,width:.5,height:.5},
      {x:.5,y:.5,width:.5,height:.5}
    );
    expect(nested&&Math.abs(nested.x-.5)<1e-9&&Math.abs(nested.y-.5)<1e-9,'Nested region mapping failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push(String(e?.message||e));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup14(){
  const errors=[];
  i

          }
        }
        const inv=1/Math.max(1e-8,sum);
        out[i*3]=r*inv;out[i*3+1]=g*inv;out[i*3+2]=b*inv;
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
});

const RenderPresenter13=Object.freeze({
  linearFrame(data,width,height,settings,metadata={}){
    if(!data||data.length!==width*height*3)return null;
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<width*height;i++){
      const c=RenderToneMapper13.rgb([data[i*3],data[i*3+1],data[i*3+2]],settings);
      rgba[i*4]=Math.round(c[0]*255);
      rgba[i*4+1]=Math.round(c[1]*255);
      rgba[i*4+2]=Math.round(c[2]*255);
      rgba[i*4+3]=255;
    }
    return {width,height,rgba,metadata:Object.freeze({...metadata})};
  },
  frame(job,name){
    const channels=job?.renderChannels||job?.beautyFrame?.channels;
    if(!channels)return name==='Beauty'?job?.beautyFrame||null:null;
    const settings=job.settings||{};
    if(name==='Beauty'){
      return this.linearFrame(channels.get('Beauty'),channels.width,channels.height,settings,{channel:'Beauty',group13:true});
    }
    if(name==='Denoised Beauty'){
      const data=RenderDenoiser13.process(channels,settings.denoise||'Off');
      return this.linearFrame(data,channels.width,channels.height,settings,{channel:'Denoised Beauty',denoise:settings.denoise||'Off',group13:true});
    }
    return channels.toFrame(name,{
      depthMax:job?.renderScene?.camera?.far||100,
      noiseMax:settings.noiseThreshold||.1
    });
  }
});

function runRenderGroup13SelfTests(){
  const errors=[],expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};
  try{
    const t=RenderToneMapper13.rgb([1,.5,.25],{toneMapping:'ACES',exposure:0});
    expect(t.length===3&&t.every(Number.isFinite),'Tone mapper output invalid');

    const a=new RenderAOVSet(2,1);
    a.set3('Beauty',0,[1,0,0]);a.set3('Beauty',1,[0,1,0]);
    a.set3('Albedo',0,[1,0,0]);a.set3('Albedo',1,[0,1,0]);
    a.set3('Normal',0,[0,0,1]);a.set3('Normal',1,[0,0,1]);
    a.set1('Depth',0,1);a.set1('Depth',1,1);
    const d=RenderDenoiser13.process(a,'Fast');
    expect(d?.length===6&&d.every(Number.isFinite),'Denoiser output invalid');

    const f=RenderPresenter13.linearFrame(a.get('Beauty'),2,1,{toneMapping:'Linear',exposure:0});
    expect(f?.rgba?.length===8,'Group13 framebuffer presentation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push(String(e?.message||e));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup13(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderToneMapper13?.rgb!=='function')errors.push('Tone mapper missing');
  if(typeof RenderDenoiser13?.process!=='function')errors.push('Denoiser missing');
  if(typeof RenderPresenter13?.frame!=='function')errors.push('Presenter missing');
  const t=runRenderGroup13SelfTests();
  if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderGroup13Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.28.2-group13-rewrite-gen3',
  validate:validateRenderGroup13,
  denoise:RenderDenoiser13,
  toneMap:RenderToneMapper13,
  presenter:RenderPresenter13
});

Object.assign(window,{
  RenderGroup13Contract,RenderToneMapper13,RenderDenoiser13,RenderPresenter13,
  runRenderGroup13SelfTests,validateRenderGroup13,RenderGroup13Core
});

  frame(job,name='Beauty'){
    return window.RenderPresenter13?.frame?.(job,name)||null;
  },
  present(job,name='Beauty'){
    const frame=this.frame(job,name);
    const fb=window.RenderFramework?.ui?.frameBuffer||null;
    if(!frame||!fb)return false;
    fb.channel=name;
    fb.present(frame);
    window.RenderFramework?.updateFrameActions?.();
    return true;
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteRenderPresentationService=ThreeDLiteRenderPresentationService;

const ThreeDLiteRenderRegionService=Object.freeze({
  owner:'RenderRegion14',
  sanitize(region){return window.RenderRegion14?.sanitize?.(region)||null;},
  resolve(job,maxWidth=640,maxHeight=360){return window.RenderRegion14?.resolve?.(job,maxWidth,maxHeight)||null;},
  mapNested(base,local){return window.RenderRegion14?.mapNested?.(base,local)||null;},
  render(region=null){return window.RenderFramework?.renderRegion?.(region)??false;}
});
window.ThreeDLiteRenderRegionService=ThreeDLiteRenderRegionService;

  owner:'RenderFramework',
  save(){return window.RenderFramework?.saveFrame?.()??false;},

});
window.ThreeDLiteRenderImageService=ThreeDLiteRenderImageService;

  owner:'RenderFramework',
  last(){return window.RenderFramework?.renderLast?.()??false;},
  restart(){return window.RenderFramework?.restart?.()??false;},
  completed(){
    const rf=window.RenderFramework;
    return Object.freeze({
      hasCompleted:!!rf?.lastCompletedSettings,

    });
  }

function get3DLiteRenderOutputSnapshot(job=null){
  const rf=window.RenderFramework;
  const j=job||rf?.jobs?.activeJob||rf?.jobs?.lastJob||null;
  return Object.freeze({
    channel:rf?.ui?.frameBuffer?.channel||'Beauty',
    hasFrame:!!rf?.ui?.frameBuffer?.hasValidFrame,
    aovCount:ThreeDLiteRenderChannelService.names().length,
    denoise:j?.settings?.denoise||'Off',
    toneMapping:j?.settings?.toneMapping||'ACES',
    region:rf?.lastCompletedRegion?Object.freeze({...rf.lastCompletedRegion}):null,
    canReplay:!!(rf?.lastCompletedSettings||rf?.lastSettings)
  });
}

const ThreeDLiteErrorBoundaryService=Object.freeze({
  owner:'boot3DLite',
  report(scope,error){
    const message=String(error?.message||error||'Unknown error');
    try{window.ThreeDLiteAppContext?.fail?.(scope,message);}catch(_e){}
    console.error(`[3DLite:${scope}]`,error);
    window.__3DLiteLastRuntimeError=Object.freeze({scope,message,time:Date.now()});
    return Object.freeze({scope,message});
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteErrorBoundaryService=ThreeDLiteErrorBoundaryService;

const ThreeDLiteReleaseGateService=Object.freeze({
  owner:'ArchitectureCore',
  run(){
    const health=ThreeDLiteHealthService.check();
    const renderer=ThreeDLiteValidationService.renderer();
    const errors=[];
    if(!health.ok)errors.push('architecture-health');
    if(renderer&&renderer.ok===false)errors.push('renderer-ownership');
    return Object.freeze({ok:errors.length===0,errors:Object.freeze(errors),health,renderer});
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteReleaseGateService=ThreeDLiteReleaseGateService;

function register3DLiteBatch09Services(){
  const ctx=window.ThreeDLiteAppContext;
  if(!ctx?.register)return false;
  const services={DiagnosticsService:ThreeDLiteDiagnosticsService,ValidationService:ThreeDLiteValidationService,DependencyService:ThreeDLiteDependencyService,HealthService:ThreeDLiteHealthService,RecoveryService:ThreeDLiteRecoveryService,RuntimeSnapshotService:ThreeDLiteRuntimeSnapshotService,ErrorBoundaryService:ThreeDLiteErrorBoundaryService,ReleaseGateService:ThreeDLiteReleaseGateService,RuntimeFacade:ThreeDLiteRuntimeFacade};
  for(const [name,service] of Object.entries(services))if(!ctx.get?.(name))ctx.register(name,service);
  return true;
}
window.register3DLiteBatch09Services=register3DLiteBatch09Services;
register3DLiteBatch09Services();

  const services={DiagnosticsService:ThreeDLiteDiagnosticsService,ValidationService:ThreeDLiteValidationService,DependencyService:ThreeDLiteDependencyService,HealthService:ThreeDLiteHealthService,RecoveryService:ThreeDLiteRecoveryService,RuntimeSnapshotService:ThreeDLiteRuntimeSnapshotService,ErrorBoundaryService:ThreeDLiteErrorBoundaryService,ReleaseGateService:ThreeDLiteReleaseGateService,RuntimeFacade:ThreeDLiteRuntimeFacade};
  for(const [name,service] of Object.entries(services))if(!ctx.get?.(name))ctx.register(name,service);
  return true;
}

    this.nodes[id]=Object.freeze({...b,left,right,leaf:false,lightIndex:-1});

  }
  distanceToBounds(point,node){
    if(node.infinite)return 0;
    let d2=0;
    for(let a=0;a<3;a++){
      const v=point[a];
      const q=v<node.min[a]?node.min[a]-v:v>node.max[a]?v-node.max[a]:0;
      d2+=q*q;
    }
    return Math.sqrt(d2);
  }
  score(point,node){
    if(node.infinite)return node.power;
    const d=this.distanceToBounds(point,node);
    const ex=Math.max(1e-6,node.max[0]-node.min[0]);
    const ey=Math.max(1e-6,node.max[1]-node.min[1]);
    const ez=Math.max(1e-6,node.max[2]-node.min[2]);
    const radius=.5*Math.sqrt(ex*ex+ey*ey+ez*ez);
    const solidAngle=Math.min(1,(radius*radius)/Math.max(1e-6,d*d+radius*radius));
    return node.power*(.25+.75*solidAngle)/(1+d*d);
  }
  sample(point,seed){
    if(this.root<0)return null;
    let node=this.nodes[this.root],pdf=1,depth=0,state=seed>>>0;
    while(!node.leaf){
      const a=this.nodes[node.left],b=this.nodes[node.right];
      const sa=this.score(point,a),sb=this.score(point,b),sum=sa+sb;
      let pa=sum>0?sa/sum:.5;
      pa=Math.max(.02,Math.min(.98,pa));
      state=RenderSampling.hash32(state+0x9E3779B9+depth);
      const u=(state>>>0)/4294967296;
      if(u<pa){pdf*=pa;node=a;}else{pdf*=1-pa;node=b;}
      depth++;
    }
    const entry=this.lights[node.lightIndex];
    const light=entry?.light||null;
    if(light?.direction&&light?.position){
      const toPoint=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(point,light.position));
      const facing=Math.max(.05,Math.abs(RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(light.direction),toPoint)));
      pdf=Math.max(1e-8,pdf*facing);
    }
    return {light,sourceIndex:entry?.sourceIndex??node.lightIndex,pdf:Math.max(1e-8,Math.min(1,pdf)),depth};
  }
  stats(){
    return Object.freeze({lights:this.lights.length,nodes:this.nodes.length,depth:this.maxDepth(this.root)});
  }
  maxDepth(id){
    if(id<0)return 0;const n=this.nodes[id];if(n.leaf)return 1;
    return 1+Math.max(this.maxDepth(n.left),this.maxDepth(n.right));
  }
}

const PathGuidePolicy=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return !!job?.settings?.pathGuiding;},
  cellSize(job,acceleration){
    const scale=LightCachePolicy.sceneScale(acceleration);
    return Math.max(1e-4,scale/24);
  },
  minSamples:8,
  maxMix:.45,
  coneCos:.7071067811865476
});

class RenderPathGuideField{
  constructor(job,acceleration){
    this.cellSize=PathGuidePolicy.cellSize(job,acceleration);
    this.cells=new Map();
    this.updates=0;this.samples=0;this.guided=0;this.fallbacks=0;
  }
  key(p){return `${Math.floor(p[0]/this.cellSize)},${Math.floor(p[1]/this.cellSize)},${Math.floor(p[2]/this.cellSize)}`;}
  record(position,normal,direction,weight){
    if(!(weight>0)||!position?.every(Number.isFinite)||!direction?.every(Number.isFinite))return false;
    const d=RayMath.normalize(direction);if(RayMath.dot(normal,d)<=0)return false;
    const k=this.key(position),r=this.cells.get(k)||{sum:[0,0,0],weight:0,count:0,normal:[0,0,0]};
    r.sum=RayMath.add(r.sum,RayMath.mul(d,weight));
    r.normal=RayMath.add(r.normal,normal);
    r.weight+=weight;r.count++;this.cells.set(k,r);this.updates++;return true;
  }
  lookup(position,normal){
    const r=this.cells.get(this.key(position));
    if(!r||r.count<PathGuidePolicy.minSamples)return null;
    const dir=RayMath.normalize(r.sum);
    if(RayMath.dot(dir,normal)<=0)return null;
    return {direction:dir,count:r.count,confidence:Math.min(1,r.count/32)};
  }
  basis(n){
    const a=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const t=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(a,n)),b=RayMath.cross(n,t);
    return {t,b};
  }
  sampleCone(axis,normal,seed,bounce){
    const u1=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,40),u2=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,41);
    const cosTheta=1-u1*(1-PathGuidePolicy.coneCos);
    const sinTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosTheta*cosTheta)),phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const {t,b}=this.basis(axis);
    let d=RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(axis,cosTheta),RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(t,Math.cos(phi)*sinTheta),RayMath.mul(b,Math.sin(phi)*sinTheta)));
    d=RayMath.normalize(d);
    if(RayMath.dot(d,normal)<=0){this.fallbacks++;return null;}
    return d;
  }
  pdf(position,normal,direction){
    const g=this.lookup(position,normal);if(!g)return 0;
    const c=RayMath.dot(g.direction,direction);
    if(c<PathGuidePolicy.coneCos||RayMath.dot(normal,direction)<=0)return 0;
    return 1/(2*Math.PI*(1-PathGuidePolicy.coneCos));
  }
  sample(position,normal,seed,bounce){
    const g=this.lookup(position,normal);if(!g)return null;
    const d=this.sampleCone(g.direction,normal,seed,bounce);if(!d)return null;
    this.samples++;this.guided++;
    return {direction:d,pdf:this.pdf(position,normal,d),confidence:g.confidence};
  }
  stats(){return Object.freeze({cells:this.cells.size,updates:this.updates,samples:this.samples,guided:this.guided,fallbacks:this.fallbacks,cellSize:this.cellSize});}
}

const GuidedBSDFSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(job,material,n,v,position,seed,bounce){
    const field=job?.pathGuide;
    if(!PathGuidePolicy.enabled(job)||!field)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
    const guide=field.lookup(position,n);
    if(!guide)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);

    const mix=Math.min(PathGuidePolicy.maxMix,.15+.3*guide.confidence);
    const choose=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,42);
    let direction,lobe='guided';
    if(choose<mix){
      const gs=field.sample(position,n,seed,bounce);
      if(!gs)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
      direction=gs.direction;
    }else{
      const bs=PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
      if(!bs||bs.transmission)return bs;
      direction=bs.direction;lobe=bs.lobe;
    }
    const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,direction);
    const guidePdf=field.pdf(position,n,direction);
    const pdf=Math.max(1e-8,(1-mix)*bsdfPdf+mix*guidePdf);
    const value=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,direction);
    return {direction,pdf,weight:RenderColor.mul(value,1/pdf),lobe,guided:true};
  }
});


const Group9DirectMIS=Object.freeze({
  lightPdf(light,sample){
    if(!light||!sample)return 0;
    if(['point','spot','sun'].includes(light.type))return Infinity; // delta lights: light sampling owns the event
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const area=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,Number(sample.area)||RenderLightPolicy.area(light));
      const distance=Math.max(1e-6,Number(sample.distance)||1);
      const cosEmitter=Math.max(1e-6,Number(sample.emitterCos)||1);
      return (distance*distance)/(area*cosEmitter);
    }
    return 0;
  },

const LightCachePolicy=Object.freeze({
  sceneScale(acceleration){
    const root=acceleration?.bvh?.root;
    const node=(root!=null&&root>=0)?acceleration.bvh.nodes[root]:null;
    if(!node)return 1;
    const d=RayMath.sub(node.max,node.min);
    return Math.max(1e-4,RayMath.length(d));
  },
  radius(job,acceleration){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    const divisor=q==='Ultra'?64:q==='High'?48:q==='Draft'?20:32;
    return Math.max(RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([0,0,0])*32,this.sceneScale(acceleration)/divisor);
  },
  probes(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');

  },
  maxRecords(job){

  },
  retraceEvery(job){

  },

});

class RenderLightCache{
  constructor(job,acceleration){
    this.radius=LightCachePolicy.radius(job,acceleration);
    this.cellSize=this.radius;
    this.maxRecords=LightCachePolicy.maxRecords(job);
    this.records=[];
    this.grid=new Map();
    this.hits=0;this.misses=0;this.inserts=0;this.rejected=0;this.retraces=0;this.refreshes=0;
  }
  key(ix,iy,iz){return ix+','+iy+','+iz}
  cell(p){return [Math.floor(p[0]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[1]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[2]/this.cellSize)]}
  insert(position,normal,radiance){
    if(this.records.length>=this.maxRecords){this.rejected++;return false;}
    if(!position?.every(Number.isFinite)||!normal?.every(Number.isFinite)||!radiance?.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0)){this.rejected++;return false;}
    const rec=Object.freeze({position:position.slice(),normal:RayMath.normalize(normal),radiance:radiance.slice()});
    const id=this.records.length;this.records.push(rec);this.inserts++;
    const c=this.cell(position),k=this.key(c[0],c[1],c[2]);
    let bucket=this.grid.get(k);if(!bucket)this.grid.set(k,bucket=[]);
    bucket.push(id);return true;
  }
  query(position,normal){
    const c=this.cell(position);let sum=[0,0,0],ws=0;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id];
        const delta=RayMath.sub(r.position,position),dist=RayMath.length(delta);
        if(dist>this.radius)continue;
        const nd=RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal);if(nd<LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold)continue;
        const w=(1-dist/this.radius)*Math.max(.05,nd);
        sum=RenderColor.add(sum,RenderColor.mul(r.radiance,w));ws+=w;
      }
    }
    if(ws>0){this.hits++;return RenderColor.mul(sum,1/ws);}
    this.misses++;return null;
  }
  refreshNearest(position,normal,radiance){
    const c=this.cell(position);let best=-1,bestD=Infinity;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id],d=RayMath.length(RayMath.sub(r.position,position));
        if(d<this.radius&&d<bestD&&RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal)>=LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold){best=id;bestD=d;}
      }
    }
    if(best<0)return this.insert(position,normal,radiance);
    const old=this.records[best];
    this.records[best]=Object.freeze({
      position:old.position,normal:old.normal,
      radiance:[
        old.radiance[0]*.75+radiance[0]*.25,
        old.radiance[1]*.75+radiance[1]*.25,
        old.radiance[2]*.75+radiance[2]*.25
      ]
    });
    this.refreshes++;return true;
  }
  stats(){return Object.freeze({records:this.records.length,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,inserts:this.inserts,rejected:this.rejected,retraces:this.retraces,refreshes:this.refreshes,radius:this.radius});}
}

      const probeRay=new RenderRay(origin,d,eps,Infinity,RenderRayType.DIFFUSE,bounce+1);
      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }
});

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Mat
-- hit 4 line 8099 --
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample?.direction||!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(x=>x<=0||!Number.isFinite(x)))continue;
        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          if(shadowRays>=maxShadowRays)break;
          shadowRays++;
          if(!ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance))continue;
        }

        const scaledRadiance=useTree?RenderColor.mul(sample.radiance,1/selectionPdf):sample.radiance;
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        const conditionalPdf=this.lightPdf(light,sample);
        const lightPdf=Number.isFinite(conditionalPdf)?conditionalPdf*selectionPdf:conditionalPdf;
        const weight=Number.isFinite(lightPdf)?RenderMIS.power(lightPdf,bsdfPdf):1;
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,scaledRadiance),weight));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }
    return color;
  }
});

const LightCachePolicy=Object.freeze({
  sceneScale(acceleration){
    const root=acceleration?.bvh?.root;
    const node=(root!=null&&root>=0)?acceleration.bvh.nodes[root]:null;
    if(!node)return 1;
    const d=RayMath.sub(node.max,node.min);
    return Math.max(1e-4,RayMath.length(d));
  },
  radius(job,acceleration){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    const divisor=q==='Ultra'?64:q==='High'?48:q==='Draft'?20:32;
    return Math.max(RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([0,0,0])*32,this.sceneScale(acceleration)/divisor);
  },
  probes(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?16:q==='High'?12:q==='Draft'?3:8;
  },
  maxRecords(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?12000:q==='High'?8000:q==='Draft'?1500:4000;
  },
  retraceEvery(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?8:q==='High'?12:q==='Draft'?32:16;
  },
  normalThreshold:.75
});

class RenderLightCache{
  constructor(job,acceleration){
    this.radius=LightCachePolicy.radius(job,acceleration);
    this.cellSize=this.radius;
    this.maxRecords=LightCachePolicy.maxRecords(job);
    this.records=[];
    this.grid=new Map();
    this.hits=0;this.misses=0;this.inserts=0;this.rejected=0;this.retraces=0;this.refreshes=0;
  }
  key(ix,iy,iz){return ix+','+iy+','+iz}
  cell(p){return [Math.floor(p[0]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[1]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[2]/this.cellSize)]}
  insert(position,normal,radiance){
    if(this.records.length>=this.maxRecords){this.rejected++;return false;}
    if(!position?.every(Number.isFinite)||!normal?.every(Number.isFinite)||!radiance?.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0)){this.rejected++;return false;}
    const rec=Object.freeze({position:position.slice(),normal:RayMath.normalize(normal),radiance:radiance.slice()});
    const id=this.records.length;this.records.push(rec);this.inserts++;
    const c=this.cell(position),k=this.key(c[0],c[1],c[2]);
    let bucket=this.grid.get(k);if(!bucket)this.grid.set(k,bucket=[]);
    bucket.push(id);return true;
  }
  query(position,normal){
    const c=this.cell(position);let sum=[0,0,0],ws=0;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id];
        const delta=RayMath.sub(r.position,position),dist=RayMath.length(delta);
        if(dist>this.radius)continue;
        const nd=RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal);if(nd<LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold)continue;
        const w=(1-dist/this.radius)*Math.max(.05,nd);
        sum=RenderColor.add(sum,RenderColor.mul(r.radiance,w));ws+=w;
      }
    }
    if(ws>0){this.hits++;return RenderColor.mul(sum,1/ws);}
    this.misses++;return null;
  }
  refreshNearest(position,normal,radiance){
    const c=this.cell(position);let best=-1,bestD=Infinity;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id],d=RayMath.length(RayMath.sub(r.position,position));
        if(d<this.radius&&d<bestD&&RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal)>=LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold){best=id;bestD=d;}
      }
    }
    if(best<0)return this.insert(position,normal,radiance);
    const old=this.records[best];
    this.records[best]=Object.freeze({
      position:old.position,normal:old.normal,
      radiance:[
        old.radiance[0]*.75+radiance[0]*.25,
        old.radiance[1]*.75+radiance[1]*.25,
        old.radiance[2]*.75+radiance[2]*.25
      ]
    });
    this.refreshes++;return true;
  }
  stats(){return Object.freeze({records:this.records.length,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,inserts:this.inserts,rejected:this.rejected,retraces:this.retraces,refreshes:this.refreshes,radius:this.radius});}
}

const LightCacheEstimator=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return job?.settings?.globalIllumination!==false&&job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='Light Cache';},
  cacheable(material){return material&&material.shading!=='unlit'&&(material.transmission||0)<.05&&(material.metalness||0)<.95;},

  probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const count=LightCachePolicy.probes(job);
    let incoming=[0,0,0];
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^Math.imul(i+1,0x85EBCA77),bounce+17);
      const origin=PathIntegrator.offsetOrigin(hit,n,d);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const probeRay=new RenderRay(origin,d,eps,Infinity,RenderRayType.DIFFUSE,bounce+1);
      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }
});

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(job?.settings?.maxBounces)||5));
  },

  finiteColor(c){
    return Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);
  },

  safeColor(c){
    return this.finiteColor(c)?c:[0,0,0];
  },

  offsetOrigin(hit,n,direction){
    const sign=RayMath.dot(direction,n)>=0?1:-1;
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(hit.position);
    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

    const maxPathRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxPathRays)||64));

      const hit=(bounce===0&&initialHit)?initialHit:acceleration.trace(ray);

      if(!hit.hit){
        const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,ray.direction));

      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

    this.cellSize=this.radius;
    this.maxRecords=LightCachePolicy.maxRecords(job);
    this.records=[];
    this.grid=new Map();
    this.hits=0;this.misses=0;this.inserts=0;this.rejected=0;this.retraces=0;this.refreshes=0;
  }
  key(ix,iy,iz){return ix+','+iy+','+iz}
  cell(p){return [Math.floor(p[0]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[1]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[2]/this.cellSize)]}
  insert(position,normal,radiance){
    if(this.records.length>=this.maxRecords){this.rejected++;return false;}
    if(!position?.every(Number.isFinite)||!normal?.every(Number.isFinite)||!radiance?.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0)){this.rejected++;return false;}
    const rec=Object.freeze({position:position.slice(),normal:RayMath.normalize(normal),radiance:radiance.slice()});
    const id=this.records.length;this.records.push(rec);this.inserts++;
    const c=this.cell(position),k=this.key(c[0],c[1],c[2]);
    let bucket=this.grid.get(k);if(!bucket)this.grid.set(k,bucket=[]);
    bucket.push(id);return true;
  }
  query(position,normal){
    const c=this.cell(position);let sum=[0,0,0],ws=0;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id];
        const delta=RayMath.sub(r.position,position),dist=RayMath.length(delta);
        if(dist>this.radius)continue;
        const nd=RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal);if(nd<LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold)continue;
        const w=(1-dist/this.radius)*Math.max(.05,nd);
        sum=RenderColor.add(sum,RenderColor.mul(r.radiance,w));ws+=w;
      }
    }
    if(ws>0){this.hits++;return RenderColor.mul(sum,1/ws);}
    this.misses++;return null;
  }
  refreshNearest(position,normal,radiance){
    const c=this.cell(position);let best=-1,bestD=Infinity;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id],d=RayMath.length(RayMath.sub(r.position,position));
        if(d<this.radius&&d<bestD&&RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal)>=LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold){best=id;bestD=d;}
      }
    }
    if(best<0)return this.insert(position,normal,radiance);
    const old=this.records[best];
    this.records[best]=Object.freeze({
      position:old.position,normal:old.normal,
      radiance:[
        old.radiance[0]*.75+radiance[0]*.25,
        old.radiance[1]*.75+radiance[1]*.25,
        old.radiance[2]*.75+radiance[2]*.25
      ]
    });
    this.refreshes++;return true;
  }
  stats(){return Object.freeze({records:this.records.length,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,inserts:this.inserts,rejected:this.rejected,retraces:this.retraces,refreshes:this.refreshes,radius:this.radius});}
}

const LightCacheEstimator=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return job?.settings?.globalIllumination!==false&&job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='Light Cache';},
  cacheable(material){return material&&material.shading!=='unlit'&&(material.transmission||0)<.05&&(material.metalness||0)<.95;},

  probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const count=LightCachePolicy.probes(job);
    let incoming=[0,0,0];

      const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^Math.imul(i+1,0x85EBCA77),bounce+17);

      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);

      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;

    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }

    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

const LightCacheEstimator=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return job?.settings?.globalIllumination!==false&&job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='Light Cache';},
  cacheable(material){return material&&material.shading!=='unlit'&&(material.transmission||0)<.05&&(material.metalness||0)<.95;},

  probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const count=LightCachePolicy.probes(job);
    let incoming=[0,0,0];
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^Math.imul(i+1,0x85EBCA77),bounce+17);
      const origin=PathIntegrator.offsetOrigin(hit,n,d);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const probeRay=new RenderRay(origin,d,eps,Infinity,RenderRayType.DIFFUSE,bounce+1);
      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }
});

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(job?.settings?.maxBounces)||5));
  },

  finiteColor(c){
    return Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);
  },

  safeColor(c){
    return this.finiteColor(c)?c:[0,0,0];
  },

  offsetOrigin(hit,n,direction){
    const sign=RayMath.dot(direction,n)>=0?1:-1;
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(hit.position);
    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

  trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    let radiance=[0,0,0];
    let throughput=[1,1,1];
    let ray=primaryRay;
    let previousGuide=null;
    const maxDepth=this.maxDepth(job);
    const maxPathRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxPathRays)||64));
    let pathRays=1;

    for(let bounce=0;bounce<maxDepth;bounce++){
      const hit=(bounce===0&&initialHit)?initialHit:acceleration.trace(ray);

      if(!hit.hit){
        const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,ray.direction));
        radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,env));
        if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
          const w=Math.max(0,.2126*env[0]+.7152*env[1]+.0722*env[2]);
          job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
        }
        break;
      }

      const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
      const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

      // Next-event direct lighting at each hit. Dome is excluded here because
      // environment energy is accounted for by path miss evaluation.
      const direct=Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(
        job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed^(bounce*0x9E3779B9)
      );
      const safeDirect=this.safeColor(direct);
      radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,safeDirect));

      if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
        const w=Math.max(0,.2126*safeDirect[0]+.7152*safeDirect[1]+.0722*safeDirect[2]);
        job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
      }

      if(bounce>=1){
        if(job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='None')break;
        if(LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)){
          const cachedTail=LightCacheEstimator.estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,pixelSeed,bounce);
          if(cachedTail){
            radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,cachedTail));
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if(material.shading==='unlit')break;

      const sample=GuidedBSDFSampler.sample(job,material,n,v,hit.position,pixelSeed,bounce);
      if(!sample)break;

      throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,sample.weight);

      if(!this.finiteColor(throughput))break;
      if(Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])<1e-6)break;

      // Russian roulette after the first few bounces. Survival probability tracks path throughput.
      if(bounce>=2){
        const survive=Math.max(.08,Math.min(.95,Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])));
        if(PathSampler.scalar(pixelSeed,bounce,15)>survive)break;
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,1/survive);
      }

      if(sample.transmission&&material.thickness>0){
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(
          throughput,
          DielectricTransport.beerLambert(material.attenuationColor,material.thickness,material.attenuationDistance)
        );
      }

      previousGuide=(!sample.transmission&&job.pathGuide&&RayMath.dot(n,sample.direction)>0)
        ? {position:hit.position.slice(),normal:n.slice(),direction:sample.direction.slice()}
        : null;

      if(pathRays>=maxPathRays)break;
      pathRays++;
      const origin=this.offsetOrigin(hit,n,sample.direction);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const rayType=sample.transmission?RenderRayType.TRANSMISSION:(sample.lobe==='ggx'?RenderRayType.GLOSSY:RenderRayType.DIFFUSE);
      RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job).addRay(rayType);
      ray=new RenderRay(origin,sample.direction,eps,Infinity,rayType,bounce+1);
    }

    return radiance;
  },

  tracePixel(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed){
    const primaryHitRecord=acceleration.trace(primaryRay);
    if(!primaryHitRecord.hit){
      const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,primaryRay.direction));
      return {radiance:env,primaryHit:false,primaryHitRecord:null};
    }
    return {
      radiance:this.trace(job,acce

  tracePixel(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed){

    if(!primaryHitRecord.hit){
      const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,primaryRay.direction));
      return {radiance:env,primaryHit:false,primaryHitRecord:null};
    }
    return {
      radiance:this.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,primaryHitRecord),
      primaryHit:true,
      primaryHitRecord
    };
  }

class ProgressiveAccumulator{
  constructor(width,height){
    this.width=width;this.height=height;
    const n=width*height;
    this.sum=new Float64Array(n*3);
    this.mean=new Float64Array(n);
    this.m2=new Float64Array(n);
    this.samples=new Uint32Array(n);
    this.converged=new Uint8Array(n);
    this.active=n;
  }
  add(index,color){
    const s=++this.samples[index];
    const o=index*3;
    this.sum[o]+=color[0];this.sum[o+1]+=color[1];this.sum[o+2]+=color[2];
    const lum=.2126*color[0]+.7152*color[1]+.0722*color[2];
    const delta=lum-this.mean[index];
    this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
    this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
  }
  variance(index){
    const s=this.samples[index];
    return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
  }
  noise(index){
    const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
    const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
    return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
  }
  shouldSample(index,settings){
    if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
    if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
    return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
  }
  markConverged(index){
    if(this.converged[index])return;
    this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
  }
  resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
      const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
      const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
      rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
      rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

  variance(index){
    const s=this.samples[index];
    return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
  }
  noise(index){
    const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
    const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
    return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
  }
  shouldSample(index,settings){
    if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
    if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
    return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
  }
  markConverged(index){
    if(this.converged[index])return;
    this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
  }
  resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
      const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
      const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
      rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
      rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(j

  },
  yieldRows(job){
    // Tile Size controls cooperative row batches only.
    // This deliberately does not replace the proven scanline traversal.
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Math.round(this.tileSize(job)/4)));
  }
});

var RenderProfiler16=Object.freeze({
  now(){return performance.now();},
  begin(){return performance.now();},
  elapsed(start){return Math.max(0,performance.now()-start);},
  mark(counter,name,start){
    const ms=this.elapsed(start);
    if(counter?.stageMs&&name in counter.stageMs)counter.stageMs[name]+=ms;
    return ms;
  }
});
window.RenderProfiler16=RenderProfiler16;

var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
  constructor(){
    this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
    this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
    this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
    this.flags[2]=1;
  }
  cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
  reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
  pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
  resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
  cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
  paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}
window.RenderControlToken16=RenderControlToken16;

var RenderPerformanceCounters16 = class RenderPerformanceCounters16{
  constructor(){
    this.cameraRays=0;
    this.pathRays=0;
    this.shadowRays=0;
    this.probeRays=0;
    this.samples=0;
    this.yields=0;
    this.stageMs={snapshot:0,geometry:0,worker:0,bvh:0,shading:0,lightCache:0,render:0,denoise:0,toneMap:0,post:0};
    this.startedAt=RenderProfiler16.now();
  }
  addRay(type){
    if(type===RenderRayType.CAMERA)this.cameraRays++;
    else if(type===RenderRayType.SHADOW)this.shadowRays++;
    else this.pathRays++;
  }
  addProbe(){this.probeRays++;}
  markStage(name,start){
    return RenderProfiler16.mark(this,name,start);
  }
  snapshot(){
    const elapsed=Math.max(.001,performance.now()-this.startedAt);
    const totalRays=this.cameraRays+this.pathRays;
    return Object.freeze({
      cameraRays:this.cameraRays,
      pathRays:this.pathRays,
      shadowRays:this.shadowRays,
      probeRays:this.probeRays,
      totalRays:totalRays+this.shadowRays+this.probeRays,
      samples:this.samples,
      yields:this.yields,
      raysPerSecond:Math.round(totalRays/(elapsed/1000)),
      stageMs:Object.freeze({...this.stageMs})
    });
  }
}

const RenderPerformanceRuntime16=Object.freeze({
  attach(job){
    if(!job.performanceState)job.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    return job.performanceState;
  },
  markStage(job,name,start){
    this.attach(job).markStage(name,start);
  },
  snapshot(job){
    return job?.performanceState?.snapshot?.()||null;
  }
});

const RenderGroup16Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'tile-size cooperative row-batch policy',
    'camera/path ray counters',
    'sample counter',
    'cooperative yield counter',
    'render-stage timing',
    'rays-per-second diagnostics'
  ]),
  preserves:Object.freeze([
    'existing Fast scanline traversal',
    'existing Path progressive traversal',
    'Group 15 Light Tree and Path Guiding',
    'Group 14 region rendering',
    'editor startup order',
    'viewport ownership'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor startup','viewport','selection','modeling',
    'BVH layout replacement','worker scheduler replacement',
    'shared tile traversal','SIMD/WASM kernel'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup16SelfTests(){
  const errors=[],expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};
  try{
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'8'}})===8,'Tile size 8 failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'64'}})===64,'Tile size 64 failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'Auto',quality:'Draft'}})===32,'Auto Draft tile failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.yieldRows({settings:{tileSize:'16'}})===4,'Yield-row policy failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Performance policy: '+String(e?.message||e));}
  try{
    const c=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    c.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
    c.addRay(RenderRayType.DIFFUSE);
    c.samples=2;
    const snap=c.snapshot();
    expect(snap.totalRays===2&&snap.cameraRays===1&&snap.pathRays===1,'Ray counters failed');
    expect(snap.samples===2&&Number.isFinite(snap.raysPerSecond),'Sample/RPS counters failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Performance counters: '+String(e?.message||e));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup16(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderPerformancePolicy16?.tileSize!=='function')errors.push('Performance policy missing');
  if(typeof RenderPerformanceCounters16!=='function')errors.push('Performance counters missing');
  if(typeof RenderPerformanceRuntime16?.attach!=='function')errors.push('Performance runtime missing');
  const t=runRenderGroup16SelfTests();
  if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderGroup16Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.31-group16-safe-rewrite-gen3',
  validate:validateRenderGroup16,
  policy:RenderPerformancePolicy16,
  runtime:RenderPerformanceRuntime16
});

Object.assign(window,{
  RenderPerformancePolicy16,RenderPerformanceCounters16,RenderPerformanceRuntime16,
  RenderGroup16Contract,runRenderGroup16SelfTests,validateRenderGroup16,RenderGroup16Core
});


var RenderRayBatch16 = class RenderRayBatch16{
  constructor(capacity=128){
    this.capacity=Math.max(1,capacity|0);
    this.origins=new Float64Array(this.capacity*3);
    this.directions=new Float64Array(this.capacity*3);
    this.pixelIndices=new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.seeds=new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.count=0;
  }
  clear(){this.count=0;return this;}
  push(ray,pixelIndex,seed){
    if(this.count>=this.capacity)return false;
    const i=this.count++,o=i*3;
    this.origins[o]=ray.origin[0];this.origins[o+1]=ray.origin[1];this.origins[o+2]=ray.origin[2];
    this.directions[o]=ray.direction[0];this.directions[o+1]=ray.direction[1];this.directions[o+2]=ray.direction[2];
    this.pixelIndices[i]=pixelIndex>>>0;this.seeds[i]=seed>>>0;
    return true;
  }
  ray(i,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA){
    const o=i*3;
    return new RenderRay(
      [this.origins[o],this.origins[o+1],this.origins[o+2]],
      [this.directions[o],this.directions[o+1],this.directions[o+2]],
      1e-6,Infinity,type,0
    );
  }
  trace(acceleration,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA){
    const hits=new Array(this.count);
    for(let i=0;i<this.count;i++)hits[i]=acceleration.trace(this.ray(i,type));
    return hits;
  }
}
window.RenderRayBatch16=RenderRayBatch16;

const LightCacheEstimator=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return job?.settings?.globalIllumination!==false&&job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='Light Cache';},
  cacheable(material){return material&&material.shading!=='unlit'&&(material.transmission||0)<.05&&(material.metalness||0)<.95;},

  probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const count=LightCachePolicy.probes(job);
    let incoming=[0,0,0];
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^Math.imul(i+1,0x85EBCA77),bounce+17);
      const origin=PathIntegrator.offsetOrigin(hit,n,d);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const probeRay=new RenderRay(origin,d,eps,Infinity,RenderRayType.DIFFUSE,bounce+1);
      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }
});

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(job?.settings?.maxBounces)||5));
  },

  finiteColor(c){
    return Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);
  },

  safeColor(c){
    return this.finiteColor(c)?c:[0,0,0];
  },

  offsetOrigin(hit,n,direction){
    const sign=RayMath.dot(direction,n)>=0?1:-1;
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(hit.position);
    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

  trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    let radiance=[0,0,0];
    let throughput=[1,1,1];
    let ray=primaryRay;
    let previousGuide=null;
    const maxDepth=this.maxDepth(job);
    const maxPathRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxPathRays)||64));
    let pathRays=1;

    for(let bounce=0;bounce<maxDepth;bounce++){
      const hit=(bounce===0&&initialHit)?initialHit:acceleration.trace(ray);

      if(!hit.hit){
        const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,ray.direction));
        radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,env));
        if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
          const w=Math.max(0,.2126*env[0]+.7152*env[1]+.0722*env[2]);
          job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
        }
        break;
      }

      const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
      const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

      // Next-event direct lighting at each hit. Dome is excluded here because
      // environment energy is accounted for by path miss evaluation.
      const direct=Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(
        job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed^(bounce*0x9E3779B9)
      );
      const safeDirect=this.safeColor(direct);
      radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,safeDirect));

      if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
        const w=Math.max(0,.2126*safeDirect[0]+.7152*safeDirect[1]+.0722*safeDirect[2]);
        job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
      }

      if(bounce>=1){
        if(job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='None')break;
        if(LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)){
          const cachedTail=LightCacheEstimator.estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,pixelSeed,bounce);
          if(cachedTail){
            radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,cachedTail));
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if(material.shading==='unlit')break;

      const sample=GuidedBSDFSampler.sample(job,material,n,v,hit.position,pixelSeed,bounce);
      if(!sample)break;

      throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,sample.weight);

      if(!this.finiteColor(throughput))break;
      if(Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])<1e-6)break;

      // Russian roulette after the first few bounces. Survival probability tracks path throughput.
      if(bounce>=2){
        const survive=Math.max(.08,Math.min(.95,Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])));
        if(PathSampler.scalar(pixelSeed,bounce,15)>survive)break;
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,1/survive);
      }

      if(sample.transmission&&material.thickness>0){
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(
          throughput,
          DielectricTransport.beerLambert(material.attenuationColor,material.thickness,material.attenuationDistance)
        );
      }

      previousGuide=(!sample.transmission&&job.pathGuide&&RayMath.dot(n,sample.direction)>0)
        ? {position:hit.position.slice(),normal:n.slice(),direction:sample.direction.slice()}
        : null;

      if(pathRays>=maxPathRays)break;
      pathRays++;
      const origin=this.offsetOrigin(hit,n,sample.direction);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const rayType=sample.transmission?RenderRayType.TRANSMISSION:(sample.lobe==='ggx'?RenderRayType.GLOSSY:RenderRayType.DIFFUSE);
      RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job).addRay(rayType);
      ray=new RenderRay(origin,sample.direction,eps,Infinity,rayType,bounce+1);
    }

    return radiance;
  },

  tracePixel(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed){
    const primaryHitRecord=acceleration.trace(primaryRay);
    if(!primaryHitRecord.hit){
      const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,prim
-- hit 3 line 8411 --

  shouldSample(index,settings){

    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;

  }
  markConverged(index){

  }
  resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){

  }
  activeIndices(settings){

    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

var PathGIRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;
    const compiled=RenderPart5Core.compile(job);
    const settings=job.settings;
    job.lightCache=LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)?new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration):null;
    job.lightTree=RenderCoreH.createLightTree(job,compiled);
    job.pathGuide=RenderCoreH.createPathGuide(job,acceleration);
    job.progress.gi=settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache'?'Brute Force + Light Cache':settings.secondaryGI;
    const accumulator=new ProgressiveAccumulator(width,height);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const primaryMask=new Uint8Array(width*height);
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const rowChunk16=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;
    const maxSamples=Math.max(1,settings.maxSamples||1);
    const progressive=settings.progressive!==false;
    const passLimit=progressive?maxSamples:1;
    let completedPasses=0;

    for(let pass=0;pass<passLimit;pass++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));

      let sampledThisPass=0;
      const activePixels16=progressive?accumulator.compactActive(settings):(()=>{
        const a=new Uint32Array(width*height);for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)a[i]=i;return a;
      })();
      if(activePixels16.length===0)break;

      const workQueue16=RenderWorkQueue16.fromIndices(activePixels16,Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*4));
      await workQueue16.run(async batch=>{
        for(const i of batch){
          if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
          const x=i%width,y=(i/width)|0;
          const seed=((i+1)^Math.imul(pass+1,0x9E3779B1))>>>0;
          const jitterX=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,20)-.5;
          const jitterY=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,21)-.5;
          const ray=RenderPart5Core.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x+jitterX,rr.y0+y+jitterY,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
          const sample=RenderPart5Core.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,seed);
          perf16.samples++;
          primaryMask[i]|=sample.primaryHit?1:0;
          accumulator.add(i,sample.radiance);
          if(pass===0){
            const ph=sample.primaryHitRecord;
            if(ph?.hit)RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,ph,seed));
            else RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,null);
          }
          sampledThisPass++;
        }
        perf16.yields++;
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
      });
      completedPasses=pass+1;
      const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
      job.previewFrame={
        width,height,rgba,
        metadata:Object.freeze({
          renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
          samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
          currentPass:completedPasses,
          maxSamples,
          adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
          noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
          activePixels:accumulator.active
        })
      };
      job.progress.currentSamples=Math.round(accumulator.averageSamples());
      job.progress.maximumSamples=maxSamples;
      job.progress.noise=accumulator.averageNoise();
      onProgress(Math.min(1,completedPasses/passLimit));

      if(!progressive)break;
      if(settings.adaptive&&completedPasses>=settings.minSamples&&accumulator.active===0)break;
      if(sampledThisPass===0)break;
    }

    const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
    for(let i=0;i<width*height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,accumulator.samples[i]),o=i*3;
      const beauty=[accumulator.sum[o]/s,accumulator.sum[o+1]/s,accumulator.sum[o+2]/s];
      aovs.set3('Beauty',i,beauty);
      aovs.set1('Noise',i,Number.isFinite(accumulator.noise(i))?accumulator.noise(i):0);
      const d=aovs.get('Direct Lighting'),e=aovs.get('Emission');
      const direct=d?[d[o],d[o+1],d[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      const emission=e?[e[o],e[o+1],e[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      aovs.set3('Global Illumination',i,[
        Math.max(0,beauty[0]-direct[0]-emission[0]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[1]-direct[1]-emission[1]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[2]-direct[2]-emission[2])
      ]);
    }
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
        samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
        passes:completedPasses,
        maxBounces:PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job),
        estimator:'Path GI + direct NEE + power-heuristic MIS',
        mis:true,
        adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
        progressive:!!settings.progressive,
        russianRoulette:true,
        importanceSampling:'GGX + cosine mixture',
        noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),

        secondaryGI:

const RenderGroup8Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'multi-bounce path integration',
    'path throughput',
    'secondary-ray spawning',
    'cosine hemisphere BSDF sampling baseline',
    'environment contribution on path miss',
    'path-GI render mode'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B ray/BVH tracing',
    'Core C physical materials and lights',
    'Group 7 shadow visibility and direct-light sampling'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'progressive accumulation',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'MIS',
    'Light Cache',
    'denoising',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

Object.assign(window,{
  PathSampler,PathBSDFSampler,PathIntegrator,PathGIRenderer,RenderGroup8Contract
});

function runRenderGroup9SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const w=RenderMIS.power(.5,.5);
    expect(Math.abs(w-.5)<1e-9,'MIS equal-PDF power heuristic failed');
    expect(RenderMIS.power(1,0)===1,'MIS zero competitor failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('MIS test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const h=GGXImportanceSampler.sampleHalfVector(n,.25,.37,.61);
    expect(h.every(Number.isFinite)&&RayMath.dot(n,h)>=0,'GGX half-vector sample failed');
    const D=GGXImportanceSampler.distribution(.8,.25);
    expect(Number.isFinite(D)&&D>0,'GGX distribution failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('GGX test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'g9',name:'g9',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.7,.6,.5],
      roughness:.2,metalness:.8,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],12345,0);
    expect(!!s&&s.pdf>0&&Number.isFinite(s.pdf),'Mixed BSDF sampler failed');
    expect(s.weight.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0),'Mixed BSDF weight invalid');
  }catch(e){errors.push('BSDF importance test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const a=new ProgressiveAccumulator(2,1);
    a.add(0,[1,1,1]);a.add(0,[1,1,1]);
    a.add(1,[0,0,0]);a.add(1,[2,2,2]);
    expect(a.samples[0]===2&&a.samples[1]===2,'Accumulator sample count failed');
    expect(a.noise(0)===0,'Stable pixel noise should be zero');
    expect(Number.isFinite(a.noise(1))&&a.noise(1)>0,'Variable pixel noise estimation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Accumulator test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const s=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),

  if(typeof RenderSettingsPolicy?.sanitize!=='function')errors.push('RenderSettingsPolicy missing');
  if(typeof RenderFrameBufferView?.prototype?.present!=='function')errors.push('Framebuffer present API missing');

  if(typeof RenderFramework?.resume!=='function')errors.push('Framebuffer Resume command missing');

  if(typeof RenderFramework?.saveFrame!=='function')errors.push('Framebuffer Save command missing');
  if(typeof RenderPerformanceMetrics?.finish!=='function')errors.push('Performance metrics missing');
  if(!document.getElementById('rfPause'))errors.push('Pause button missing');

const ThreeDLiteRenderSceneService=Object.freeze({
  owner:'RenderSceneBoundary',
  snapshot(){return RenderSceneBoundary.buildSnapshot()},
  verify(scene){return RenderSceneBoundary.verifyDetached(scene)},
  validatedSnapshot(){
    const scene=RenderSceneBoundary.buildSnapshot();
    const detached=RenderSceneBoundary.verifyDetached(scene);
    if(!detached.ok)throw new Error(detached.errors.join('; '));
    return scene;
  }
});
window.ThreeDLiteRenderSceneService=ThreeDLiteRenderSceneService;

const ThreeDLiteRenderRequestService=Object.freeze({
  owner:'RendererMasterCore',
  create(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    return RendererMasterCore.createRequest(mode,region,settingsOverride);
  },
  prepare(job,pool,onProgress=()=>{}){
    return RendererMasterCore.prepare(job,pool,onProgress);
  },
  rendererFor(job){return RendererMasterCore.rendererFor(job)}
});

  concentricDisk(u1,u2){
    const sx=2*u1-1,sy=2*u2-1;
    if(sx===0&&sy===0)return [0,0];
    let r,theta;
    if(Math.abs(sx)>Math.abs(sy)){
      r=sx;theta=(Math.PI/4)*(sy/sx);
    }else{
      r=sy;theta=(Math.PI/2)-(Math.PI/4)*(sx/sy);
    }
    return [r*Math.cos(theta),r*Math.sin(theta)];
  }
});

const LightBasis=Object.freeze({
  fromDirection(direction){
    const n=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    const helper=Math.abs(n[2])<.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
    const tangent=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.cross(helper,n));
    const bitangent=RayMath.cross(n,tangent);
    return {n,tangent,bitangent};
  },
  pointOnRectangle(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const du=(u-.5)*light.width,dv=(v-.5)*light.height;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,du),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,dv)));
  },
  pointOnDisc(light,u,v){
    const b=this.fromDirection(light.direction);
    const d=RenderSampling.concentricDisk(u,v);
    const r=light.size*.5;
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(b.tangent,d[0]*r),RayMath.mul(b.bitangent,d[1]*r)));
  },
  pointOnSphere(light,u,v){
    const z=1-2*u;
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-z*z));
    const phi=2*Math.PI*v;
    const local=[r*Math.cos(phi),r*Math.sin(phi),z];
    return RayMath.add(light.position,RayMath.mul(local,light.size*.5));
  }
});

const EnvironmentSampler=Object.freeze({
  findDome(lights){return (lights||[]).find(l=>l.enabled!==false&&l.type==='dome')||null;},
  radiance(light,direction){
    if(!light||light.enabled===false||light.type!=='dome')return [0,0,0];
    return LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light);
  },
  background(renderScene,settings,lights,rayDirection){
    const dome=this.findDome(lights);
    if(dome)return this.radiance(dome,rayDirection);
    return RenderColor.hexToLinear(settings.background||'#202020');
  }
});

const ShadowVisibility=Object.freeze({
  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addRay?.(RenderRayType.SHADOW);}catch(_){}
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
    if(Number.isFinite(maxDistance)&&maxDistance<=eps*2)return true;
    const o=RayMath.add(origin,RayMath.mul(direction,eps));
    const limit=Number.isFinite(maxDistance)?Math.max(eps,maxDistance-eps):Infinity;
    const ray=new RenderRay(o,direction,eps,limit,RenderRayType.SHADOW,0);
    return !acceleration.occluded(ray);
  }
});

const AreaLightSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(light,point,u=.5,v=.5){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='disc'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnDisc(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    if(light.type==='sphere'){
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnSphere(light,u,v);
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(lp,light.position));
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),normal);
    }
    if(light.type==='mesh'){
      // Part 4 keeps Mesh Light as a rectangular proxy.
      // True emissive mesh-triangle sampling remains Group 10.
      const lp=LightBasis.pointOnRectangle(light,u,v);
      return this._local(light,point,lp,RenderLightPolicy.area(light),light.direction);
    }
    return DirectLightSampler.sample(light,point);
  },

  _local(light,point,lightPoint,area,emitterNormal=null){
    const to=RayMath.sub(lightPoint,point);
    const dist=Math.max(RayMath.length(to),1e-6);
    const dir=RayMath.mul(to,1/dist);

    if(light.range>0&&dist>=light.range){
      return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area};
    }

    let attenuation=LightEnergyPolicy.distanceAttenuation(light,dist);

    let emitterCos=1;
    if(emitterNormal){
      emitterCos=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(RayMath.normalize(emitterNormal),RayMath.mul(dir,-1)));
      if(emitterCos<=0){
        return {kind:'local',direction:dir,distance:dist,radiance:[0,0,0],lightPoint,area,emitterCos:0};
      }
    }

    const safeArea=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,area);
    return {
      kind:'local',
      direction:dir,
      distance:dist,
      // Size changes sampling footprint/softness only; intensity remains the same user-facing unit.
      radiance:LightEnergyPolicy.radiance(light,attenuation*emitterCos),
      lightPoint,
      area:safeArea,
      emitterCos
    };
  }
});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){

        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));

          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
