var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-d',

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){
    return EnvironmentSampler.background(
      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    return PathIntegrator.trace(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit
    );
  },

  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    return ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g7=validateRenderGroup7();if(!g7.ok)errors.push(...g7.errors);
    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);
    if(typeof this.evaluateSurfaceDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core D direct evaluator missing');
    if(typeof this.tracePath!=='function')errors.push('Core D path tracer missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateEnvironment!=='function')errors.push('Core D environment evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreD=RenderCoreD;

  consumes:Object.freeze([

  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'path GI',
    'MIS',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'HDRI importance sampling',
    'light cache',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){

      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){

      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){

      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit

    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);

function runRenderGroup7SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({
      objectId:'shadow-test',
      positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0]),
      normals:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,0,1]),
      uvs:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,.5,1]),
      indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])
    },0);
    store.finalize();
    const acceleration=new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,1));
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[0,0,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==false){
      errors.push('Shadow occlusion test failed');
    }
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[3,3,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==true){
      errors.push('Shadow clear test failed');
    }
    const occRay=new RenderRay([0,0,-1],[0,0,1],1e-4,10);
    if(acceleration.occluded(occRay)!==true)errors.push('BVH any-hit test failed');
  }

  {
    const light=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'r',name:'r',type:'rectangle',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
      range:0,size:1,width:2,height:2,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0,castShadows:true,
      position:[0,0,5],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const s=AreaLightSampler.sample(light,[0,0,0],.5,.5);
    if(!s||Math.abs(s.distance-5)>1e-4||s.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Rectangle area sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const a=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4),b=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4);
    if(a[0]!==b[0]||a[1]!==b[1])errors.push('Deterministic sampling test failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup7SelfTests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup7(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof ShadowVisibility.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
  if(typeof AreaLightSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
  if(typeof DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('DirectLightingIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof EnvironmentSampler.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const PathSampler=Object.freeze({
  scalar(seed,bounce,dimension){
    return RenderSampling.hash32(
      ((seed>>>0) ^ Math.imul((bounce+1),0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul((dimension+1),0x85EBCA77))>>>0
    );
  },

  cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce){
    const u1=this.scalar(seed,bounce,0);
    const u2=this.scalar(seed,bounce,1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(u1);
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const x=r*Math.cos(phi),y=r*Math.sin(phi),z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-u1));

    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,x),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,y)),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,z)
      )
    );
  }
});

var RenderMIS=Object.freeze({
  power(a,b){
    a=Math.max(0,Number(a)||0);b=Math.max(0,Number(b)||0);
    const aa=a*a,bb=b*b,d=aa+bb;
    return d>0?aa/d:1;
  }
});

      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,sinTheta*Math.cos(phi)),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,sinTheta*Math.sin(phi))),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,cosTheta)

const GGXImportanceSampler=Object.freeze({
  sampleHalfVector(n,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness);
    const a2=a*a;
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u1;
    const cosTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,(1-u2)/(1+(a2-1)*u2)));
    const sinTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosTheta*cosTheta));
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,sinTheta*Math.cos(phi)),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,sinTheta*Math.sin(phi))),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,cosTheta)
      )
    );
  },
  sampleVisibleHalfVector(n,v,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness);
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    const vl=[RayMath.dot(v,basis.tangent),RayMath.dot(v,basis.bitangent),Math.max(1e-6,RayMath.dot(v,basis.n))];
    const vh=RayMath.normalize([a*vl[0],a*vl[1],vl[2]]);
    const lensq=vh[0]*vh[0]+vh[1]*vh[1];
    const t1=lensq>1e-12?[-vh[1]/Math.sqrt(lensq),vh[0]/Math.sqrt(lensq),0]:[1,0,0];
    const t2=RayMath.cross(vh,t1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,u1)),phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const p1=r*Math.cos(phi);
    let p2=r*Math.sin(phi);
    const mix=.5*(1+vh[2]);
    p2=(1-mix)*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-p1*p1))+mix*p2;
    const z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-p1*p1-p2*p2));
    const nh=RayMath.add(RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(t1,p1),RayMath.mul(t2,p2)),RayMath.mul(vh,z));
    const ne=RayMath.normalize([a*nh[0],a*nh[1],Math.max(0,nh[2])]);
    return RayMath.normalize(RayMath.add(RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,ne[0]),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,ne[1])),RayMath.mul(basis.n,ne[2])));
  },
  reflect(i,n){return RayMath.sub(i,RayMath.mul(n,2*RayMath.dot(i,n)));},
  distribution(nDotH,roughness){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness),a2=a*a;
    const d=nDotH*nDotH*(a2-1)+1;
    return a2/(Math.PI*d*d+1e-8);
  }
});

const RenderGroup7Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'shadow-ray visibility',
    'shadow bias',
    'stochastic area-light sampling',
    'constant-color dome environment radiance',
    'direct-light sample accumulation'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B acceleration trace',
    'Core C materials and scene lights',
    'Group 5 PhysicalBSDF'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'path GI',
    'MIS',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'HDRI importance sampling',
    'light cache',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup7SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({
      objectId:'shadow-test',
      positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0]),
      normals:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,0,1]),
      uvs:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,.5,1]),
      indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])
    },0);
    store.finalize();
    const acceleration=new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,1));
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[0,0,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==false){
      errors.push('Shadow occlusion test failed');
    }
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[3,3,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==true){
      errors.push('Shadow clear test failed');
    }
    const occRay=new RenderRay([0,0,-1],[0,0,1],1e-4,10);
    if(acceleration.occluded(occRay)!==true)errors.push('BVH any-hit test failed');
  }

  {
    const light=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'r',name:'r',type:'rectangle',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
      range:0,size:1,width:2,height:2,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0,castShadows:true,
      position:[0,0,5],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const s=AreaLightSampler.sample(light,[0,0,0],.5,.5);
    if(!s||Math.abs(s.distance-5)>1e-4||s.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Rectangle area sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const a=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4),b=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4);
    if(a[0]!==b[0]||a[1]!==b[1])errors.push('Deterministic sampling test failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup7SelfTests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup7(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof ShadowVisibility.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
  if(typeof AreaLightSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
  if(typeof DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('DirectLightingIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof EnvironmentSampler.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

Object.assign(window,{
  RenderSampling,LightBasis,EnvironmentSampler,ShadowVisibility,
  AreaLightSampler,DirectLightingIntegrator,RenderGroup7Contract
});

const PathSampler=Object.freeze({
  scalar(seed,bounce,dimension){
    return RenderSampling.hash32(
      ((seed>>>0) ^ Math.imul((bounce+1),0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul((dimension+1),0x85EBCA77))>>>0
    );
  },

  cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce){
    const u1=this.scalar(seed,bounce,0);
    const u2=this.scalar(seed,bounce,1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(u1);
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const x=r*Math.cos(phi),y=r*Math.sin(phi),z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-u1));

    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,x),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,y)),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,z)
      )
    );
  }
});

var RenderMIS=Object.freeze({
  power(a,b){
    a=Math.max(0,Number(a)||0);b=Math.max(0,Number(b)||0);
    const aa=a*a,bb=b*b,d=aa+bb;
    return d>0?aa/d:1;
  }
});

const GGXImportanceSampler=Object.freeze({
  sampleHalfVector(n,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness);
    const a2=a*a;
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u1;
    const cosTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,(1-u2)/(1+(a2-1)*u2)));
    const sinTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosTheta*cosTheta));
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,sinTheta*Math.cos(phi)),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,sinTheta*Math.sin(phi))),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,cosTheta)
      )
    );
  },
  sampleVisibleHalfVector(n,v,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness);
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    const vl=[RayMath.dot(v,basis.tangent),RayMath.dot(v,basis.bitangent),Math.max(1e-6,RayMath.dot(v,basis.n))];
    const vh=RayMath.normalize([a*vl[0],a*vl[1],vl[2]]);
    const lensq=vh[0]*vh[0]+vh[1]*vh[1];
    const t1=lensq>1e-12?[-vh[1]/Math.sqrt(lensq),vh[0]/Math.sqrt(lensq),0]:[1,0,0];
    const t2=RayMath.cross(vh,t1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,u1)),phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const p1=r*Math.cos(phi);
    let p2=r*Math.sin(phi);
    const mix=.5*(1+vh[2]);
    p2=(1-mix)*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-p1*p1))+mix*p2;
    const z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-p1*p1-p2*p2));
    const nh=RayMath.add(RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(t1,p1),RayMath.mul(t2,p2)),RayMath.mul(vh,z));
    const ne=RayMath.normalize([a*nh[0],a*nh[1],Math.max(0,nh[2])]);
    return RayMath.normalize(RayMath.add(RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,ne[0]),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,ne[1])),RayMath.mul(basis.n,ne[2])));
  },
  reflect(i,n){return RayMath.sub(i,RayMath.mul(n,2*RayMath.dot(i,n)));},
  distribution(nDotH,roughness){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness),a2=a*a;
    const d=nDotH*nDotH*(a2-1)+1;
    return a2/(Math.PI*d*d+1e-8);
  }
});

const DielectricTransport=Object.freeze({
  fresnelDielectric(cosI,etaI,etaT){
    cosI=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,cosI));
    let entering=cosI>0;
    if(!entering){const t=etaI;etaI=etaT;etaT=t;cosI=Math.abs(cosI);}
    const sinI=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosI*cosI));
    const sinT=etaI/etaT*sinI;
    if(sinT>=1)return 1;
    const cosT=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-sinT*sinT));
    const rs=((etaT*cosI)-(etaI*cosT))/((etaT*cosI)+(etaI*cosT));
    const rp=((etaI*cosI)-(etaT*cosT))/((etaI*cosI)+(etaT*cosT));
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(rs*rs+rp*rp)*.5));
  },
  refract(i,n,eta){
    const cosi=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,RayMath.dot(i,n)));
    const k=1-eta*eta*(1-cosi*cosi);
    if(k<0)return null;
    return RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(RayMath.mul(i,eta),RayMath.mul(n,eta*cosi+Math.sqrt(k))));
  },
  beerLambert(color,distance,attenuationDistance){
    if(!Number.isFinite(attenuationDistance)||attenuationDistance<=0||distance<=0)return [1,1,1];
    return color.map(c=>Math.pow(Math.max(1e-6,c),distance/attenuationDistance));
  }
});

const DielectricTransport=Object.freeze({
  fresnelDielectric(cosI,etaI,etaT){
    cosI=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,cosI));
    let entering=cosI>0;
    if(!entering){const t=etaI;etaI=etaT;etaT=t;cosI=Math.abs(cosI);}
    const sinI=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosI*cosI));
    const sinT=etaI/etaT*sinI;
    if(sinT>=1)return 1;
    const cosT=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-sinT*sinT));
    const rs=((etaT*cosI)-(etaI*cosT))/((etaT*cosI)+(etaI*cosT));
    const rp=((etaI*cosI)-(etaT*cosT))/((etaI*cosI)+(etaT*cosT));
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(rs*rs+rp*rp)*.5));
  },
  refract(i,n,eta){
    const cosi=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,RayMath.dot(i,n)));
    const k=1-eta*eta*(1-cosi*cosi);
    if(k<0)return null;
    return RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(RayMath.mul(i,eta),RayMath.mul(n,eta*cosi+Math.sqrt(k))));
  },
  beerLambert(color,distance,attenuationDistance){
    if(!Number.isFinite(attenuationDistance)||attenuationDistance<=0||distance<=0)return [1,1,1];
    return color.map(c=>Math.pow(Math.max(1e-6,c),distance/attenuationDistance));
  }
});

window.runRenderGroup7SelfTests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup7(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof ShadowVisibility.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
  if(typeof AreaLightSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
  if(typeof DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('DirectLightingIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof EnvironmentSampler.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const PathSampler=Object.freeze({
  scalar(seed,bounce,dimension){
    return RenderSampling.hash32(
      ((seed>>>0) ^ Math.imul((bounce+1),0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul((dimension+1),0x85EBCA77))>>>0
    );
  },

  cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce){
    const u1=this.scalar(seed,bounce,0);
    const u2=this.scalar(seed,bounce,1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(u1);
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const x=r*Math.cos(phi),y=r*Math.sin(phi),z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-u1));

    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,x),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,y)),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,z)
      )
    );
  }
});

var RenderMIS=Object.freeze({
  power(a,b){
    a=Math.max(0,Number(a)||0);b=Math.max(0,Number(b)||0);
    const aa=a*a,bb=b*b,d=aa+bb;
    return d>0?aa/d:1;
  }
});

const DielectricTransport=Object.freeze({
  fresnelDielectric(cosI,etaI,etaT){
    cosI=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,cosI));
    let entering=cosI>0;

    const rp=((etaI*cosI)-(etaT*cosT))/((etaI*cosI)+(etaT*cosT));
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(rs*rs+rp*rp)*.5));

var PathBSDFSampler=Object.freeze({
  pdf(material,n,v,l){
    const nDotL=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,l));
    if(nDotL<=0)return 0;
    const diffusePdf=nDotL/Math.PI;
    const specProb=Math.max(.08,Math.min(.92,.25+.65*material.metalness+.2*(1-material.roughness)));
    const h=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.add(v,l));
    if(!h.every(Number.isFinite))return (1-specProb)*diffusePdf;
    const nDotH=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,h));
    const vDotH=Math.max(1e-7,RayMath.dot(v,h));
    const nDotV=Math.max(1e-7,RayMath.dot(n,v));
    const D=PhysicalBSDF.distributionGGX(nDotH,material.roughness);
    const G1=PhysicalBSDF.geometrySchlickGGX(nDotV,material.roughness);
    // VNDF half-vector pdf: D(h)*G1(v)*|v.h|/|n.v|.
    // Reflection Jacobian 1/(4|v.h|) cancels the v.h term.
    const specPdf=(D*G1)/(4*nDotV+1e-8);
    return (1-specProb)*diffusePdf+specProb*specPdf;
  },

  sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce){
    if(material.shading==='unlit')return null;
    const transmission=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,material.transmission||0));
    const chooseT=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,6);

    if(transmission>0&&chooseT<transmission){

      const distance=Math.max(1e-6,Number(sample.distance)||1);
      const cosEmitter=Math.max(1e-6,Number(sample.emitterCos)||1);
      return (distance*distance)/(area*cosEmitter);
    }
    return 0;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);
    const maxShadowRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxShadowRaysPerHit)||1024));
    let shadowRays=0;

    if(material.shading==='unlit')return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color);

    const useTree=LightTreePolicy.enabled(job,compiled)&&job.lightTree;
    const selected=useTree?job.lightTree.sample(hit.position,pixelSeed^0x51ED270B):null;
    const candidates=selected
      ? [{light:selected.light,sourceIndex:selected.sourceIndex,selectionPdf:selected.pdf}]
      : compiled.lights.map((light,sourceIndex)=>({light,sourceIndex,selectionPdf:1}));

    for(const candidate of candidates){
      const light=candidate.light;
      if(!light||light.type==='dome')continue;

      const li=candidate.sourceIndex;
      const selectionPdf=Math.max(1e-8,candidate.selectionPdf||1);
      const count=DirectLightingIntegrator.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample?.direction||!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(x=>x<=0||!Number.isFinite(x)))continue;
        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          if(shadowRays>=maxShadowRays)break;
          shadowRays++;
          if(!ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance))continue;
        }

        const scaledRadiance=useTree?RenderColor.mul(sample.radiance,1/selectionPdf):sample.radiance;
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        const conditionalPdf=this.lightPdf(light,sample);
        const lightPdf=Number.isFinite(conditionalPdf)?conditionalPdf*selectionPdf:conditionalPdf;
        const weight=Number.isFinite(lightPdf)?RenderMIS.power(lightPdf,bsdfPdf):1;
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,scaledRadiance),weight));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }
    return color;
  }
});

const LightCachePolicy=Object.freeze({
  sceneScale(acceleration){
    const root=acceleration?.bvh?.root;
    const node=(root!=null&&root>=0)?acceleration.bvh.nodes[root]:null;
    if(!node)return 1;
    const d=RayMath.sub(node.max,node.min);
    return Math.max(1e-4,RayMath.length(d));
  },
  radius(job,acceleration){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    const divisor=q==='Ultra'?64:q==='High'?48:q==='Draft'?20:32;
    return Math.max(RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([0,0,0])*32,this.sceneScale(acceleration)/divisor);
  },
  probes(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?16:q==='High'?12:q==='Draft'?3:8;
  },
  maxRecords(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?12000:q==='High'?8000:q==='Draft'?1500:4000;
  },
  retraceEvery(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?8:q==='High'?12:q==='Draft'?32:16;
  },
  normalThreshold:.75
});

class RenderLightCache{
  constructor(job,acceleration){
    this.radius=LightCachePolicy.radius(job,acceleration);
    this.cellSize=this.radius;
    this.maxRecords=LightCachePolicy.maxRecords(job);
    this.records=[];
    this.grid=new Map();
    this.hits=0;this.misses=0;this.inserts=0;this.rejected=0;this.retraces=0;this.refreshes=0;
  }
  key(ix,iy,iz){return ix+','+iy+','+iz}
  cell(p){return [Math.floor(p[0]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[1]/this.cellSize),Math.floor(p[2]/this.cellSize)]}
  insert(position,normal,radiance){
    if(this.records.length>=this.maxRecords){this.rejected++;return false;}
    if(!position?.every(Number.isFinite)||!normal?.every(Number.isFinite)||!radiance?.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0)){this.rejected++;return false;}
    const rec=Object.freeze({position:position.slice(),normal:RayMath.normalize(normal),radiance:radiance.slice()});
    const id=this.records.length;this.records.push(rec);this.inserts++;
    const c=this.cell(position),k=this.key(c[0],c[1],c[2]);
    let bucket=this.grid.get(k);if(!bucket)this.grid.set(k,bucket=[]);
    bucket.push(id);return true;
  }
  query(position,normal){
    const c=this.cell(position);let sum=[0,0,0],ws=0;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id];
        const delta=RayMath.sub(r.position,position),dist=RayMath.length(delta);
        if(dist>this.radius)continue;
        const nd=RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal);if(nd<LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold)continue;
        const w=(1-dist/this.radius)*Math.max(.05,nd);
        sum=RenderColor.add(sum,RenderColor.mul(r.radiance,w));ws+=w;
      }
    }
    if(ws>0){this.hits++;return RenderColor.mul(sum,1/ws);}
    this.misses++;return null;
  }
  refreshNearest(position,normal,radiance){
    const c=this.cell(position);let best=-1,bestD=Infinity;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id],d=RayMath.length(RayMath.sub(r.position,position));
        if(d<this.radius&&d<bestD&&RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal)>=LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold){best=id;bestD=d;}
      }
    }
    if(best<0)return this.insert(position,normal,radiance);
    const old=this.records[best];
    this.records[best]=Object.freeze({
      position:old.position,normal:old.normal,
      radiance:[
        old.radiance[0]*.75+radiance[0]*.25,
        old.radiance[1]*.75+radiance[1]*.25,
        old.radiance[2]*.75+radiance[2]*.25
      ]
    });
    this.refreshes++;return true;
  }
  stats(){return Object.freeze({records:this.records.length,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,inserts:this.inserts,rejected:this.rejected,retraces:this.retraces,refreshes:this.refreshes,radius:this.radius});}
}

  }catch(err){errors.push('Shadow test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

      id:'bad',name:'bad',type:'unsupported',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:NaN,range:-1,size:0,width:0,height:0,coneAngle:999,penumbra:-2,

       invalid.size<RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize||!invalid.direction.every(Number.isFinite)){
      errors.push('Light sanitization test failed');
    }
  }catch(err){errors.push('Light sanitization test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-d',

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){
    return EnvironmentSampler.background(
      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    return PathIntegrator.trace(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit
    );
  },

  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    return ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g7=validateRenderGroup7();if(!g7.ok)errors.push(...g7.errors);
    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);
    if(typeof this.evaluateSurfaceDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core D direct evaluator missing');
    if(typeof this.tracePath!=='function')errors.push('Core D path tracer missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateEnvironment!=='function')errors.push('Core D environment evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});

const PathSampler=Object.freeze({
  scalar(seed,bounce,dimension){
    return RenderSampling.hash32(
      ((seed>>>0) ^ Math.imul((bounce+1),0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul((dimension+1),0x85EBCA77))>>>0
    );
  },

  cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce){
    const u1=this.scalar(seed,bounce,0);
    const u2=this.scalar(seed,bounce,1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(u1);
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const x=r*Math.cos(phi),y=r*Math.sin(phi),z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-u1));

    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,x),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,y)),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,z)
      )
    );
  }
});

var RenderMIS=Object.freeze({
  power(a,b){
    a=Math.max(0,Number(a)||0);b=Math.max(0,Number(b)||0);
    const aa=a*a,bb=b*b,d=aa+bb;
    return d>0?aa/d:1;
  }
});

function runRenderGroup7SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({
      objectId:'shadow-test',
      positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0]),
      normals:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,0,1]),
      uvs:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,.5,1]),
      indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])
    },0);
    store.finalize();
    const acceleration=new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,1));
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[0,0,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==false){
      errors.push('Shadow occlusion test failed');
    }
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[3,3,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==true){
      errors.push('Shadow clear test failed');
    }
    const occRay=new RenderRay([0,0,-1],[0,0,1],1e-4,10);
    if(acceleration.occluded(occRay)!==true)errors.push('BVH any-hit test failed');
  }

  {
    const light=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'r',name:'r',type:'rectangle',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',intensity:10,
      range:0,size:1,width:2,height:2,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0,castShadows:true,
      position:[0,0,5],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const s=AreaLightSampler.sample(light,[0,0,0],.5,.5);
    if(!s||Math.abs(s.distance-5)>1e-4||s.radiance.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Rectangle area sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const a=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4),b=RenderSampling.sample2D(123,4);
    if(a[0]!==b[0]||a[1]!==b[1])errors.push('Deterministic sampling test failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const PathSampler=Object.freeze({
  scalar(seed,bounce,dimension){
    return RenderSampling.hash32(
      ((seed>>>0) ^ Math.imul((bounce+1),0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul((dimension+1),0x85EBCA77))>>>0
    );
  },

  cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce){
    const u1=this.scalar(seed,bounce,0);
    const u2=this.scalar(seed,bounce,1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(u1);
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const x=r*Math.cos(phi),y=r*Math.sin(phi),z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-u1));

    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,x),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,y)),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,z)
      )
    );
  }
});

var RenderMIS=Object.freeze({
  power(a,b){
    a=Math.max(0,Number(a)||0);b=Math.max(0,Number(b)||0);
    const aa=a*a,bb=b*b,d=aa+bb;
    return d>0?aa/d:1;
  }
});

      RayMath.add(

        RayMath.mul(basis.n,cosTheta)
      )
    );
  },

var RenderPerformanceMetrics=Object.freeze({
  begin(job){job.startedAt=performance.now();job.finishedAt=0;return job.startedAt;},
  beginTracing(job){job.renderStartedAt=performance.now();},
  finish(job){
    job.finishedAt=performance.now();
    job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,job.finishedAt-(job.startedAt||job.finishedAt));
    const seconds=Math.max(1e-6,(job.finishedAt-(job.renderStartedAt||job.startedAt||job.finishedAt))/1000);
    const measured=RenderCoreH.performance(job);
    const actualRays=Math.max(0,Number(measured?.totalRays)||0);
    job.progress.raysPerSecond=actualRays?Math.round(actualRays/seconds):0;
    job.performance=Object.freeze({
      totalMs:job.progress.elapsedMs,
      traceMs:seconds*1000,
      actualRays,
      raysPerSecond:job.progress.raysPerSecond,
      samples:Number(measured?.samples)||0,
      yields:Number(measured?.yields)||0,
      bvhBuildMs:job.progress.bvhBuildMs
    });
    return job.performance;
  }
});

class RenderJobController{
  constructor(onProgress){
    this.activeJob=null;
    this.lastJob=null;
    this.onProgress=onProgress||(()=>{});
    this.workerPool=new RenderWorkerPool();
    this.generation=0;
  }

  configureThreads(setting){
    return this.workerPool.setThreadSetting(setting);
  }

  workerStats(){
    return this.workerPool.stats();
  }

  start(job){
    if(this.activeJob&&!this.activeJob.finished&&!this.activeJob.cancelled)this.cancel(false);
    this.configureThreads(job?.settings?.cpuThreads||'Auto');
    this.activeJob=job;
    this.lastJob=job;
    RenderPerformanceMetrics.begin(job);
    const generation=++this.generation;

    job.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    job.progress.percent=0;
    this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());

    this._prepare(job,generation);
    return job;
  }

  async _prepare(job,generation){
    try{
      if(!job.renderScene)th

var RenderTileScheduler16=Object.freeze({
  makeTiles(width,height,tileSize){
    const size=Math.max(4,Math.min(128,Number(tileSize)||16));
    const tiles=[];
    let id=0;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y+=size){
      for(let x=0;x<width;x+=size){
        tiles.push(Object.freeze({
          id:id++,x,y,
          width:Math.min(size,width-x),
          height:Math.min(size,height-y)
        }));
      }
    }
    return tiles;
  },
  async runLocal(job,tiles,runTile,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const total=Math.max(1,tiles.length);
    for(let i=0;i<tiles.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      await runTile(tiles[i],i);
      onProgress((i+1)/total);
      if((i&1)===1)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
  }
});

var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));

        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }

          const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x,rr.y0+y,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
          const o=(y*width+x)*4;
  
          if(hit.hit){
            const pixelSeed=((y*width+x+1)>>>0);
            const linear=RenderPart4Core.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed);
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed));
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,linear);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,[0,0,0]);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=255;
          }else{
            const env=RenderPart4Core.environment(job,compiled,ray.direction)||backgroundFallback;
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,null);
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(env));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=job.settings.transparent?0:255;
          }
        }
      }
      try{window.ThreeDLiteRenderWindow3979?.tile?.(job,width,height,rgba,tile,{total:tiles16.length});}catch(_){}
      perf16.yields++;
    },onProgress);
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Part 4 Fast Direct',
        materialCount:compiled.materials.list.length,
        lightCount:compiled.lights.length,
        toneMapping:'ACES',
        colorSpace:'sRGB output / linear shading',
        diagnostics:RenderPart4Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});

function runRenderGroup5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'t',name:'t',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.3,.2],
    roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(brdf.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Physical BRDF finite/positive test failed');

  const metallic=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'m',name:'m',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.9,.7,.2],
    roughness:.25,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const mb=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(metallic,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(mb.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Metallic BRDF test failed');

  const tm=RenderColor.aces([10,3,1]);
  if(tm.some(x=>x<0||x>1||!Number.isFinite(x)))errors.push('Tone mapping range test failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup5SelfTests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderSurfaceMaterial!=='function')errors.push('RenderSurfaceMaterial missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('BasicDirectRenderer missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator.normal!=='function')errors.push('SurfaceInterpolator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;
window.__3DLiteLightEnergy3983=true;
window.__3DLiteLightDefaultDirection3983=true;
window.ThreeDLiteLightEnergy3983=Object.freeze({version:'3.99.0',baselineIntensity:1,referenceDistance:100});

const LightTypes=Object.freeze(['point','spot','rectangle','disc','sphere','sun','dome','mesh']);

const LightDefaults=Object.freeze({
  point:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:2,range:0,coneAngle:45,penumbra:.15}),
  spot:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:2,range:0,coneAngle:45,penumbra:.15}),
  rectangle:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,width:50,height:50,range:0,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0}),
  disc:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0}),
  sphere:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0}),
  sun:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:.5,range:0,coneAngle:.53,penumbra:0}),
  dome:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:100,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0}),
  mesh:Object.freeze({intensity:1,size:25,range:0,coneAngle:180,penumbra:0})
});

// v3.98.3: one artistic intensity scale for every light type.
// Intensity=1 is the common baseline. Local lights use a gentle scene-unit falloff
// so they remain useful instead of becoming nearly black from raw 1/r^2 at cm-scale distances.
const LightEnergyPolicy=Object.freeze({
  referenceDistance:100,
  distanceAttenuation(light,distance){
    const d=Math.max(0,Number(distance)||0);
    const ref=this.referenceDistance;
    let a=1/(1+(d/ref)*(d/ref));
    const range=Math.max(0,Number(light?.range)||0);
    if(range>0){
      if(d>=range)return 0;
      const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-d/range));
      // smooth finite-range fade without changing the shared intensity unit.
      a*=t*t*(3-2*t);
    }
    return a;
  },
  radiance(light,factor=1){
    const intensity=Math.max(0,Number(light?.intensity)||0);
    return RenderColor.mul(light.color,intensity*Math.max(0,Number(factor)||0));
  }
});
window.LightEnergyPolicy=LightEnergyPolicy;
window.__3DLiteLightEnergy3983=true;
window.__3DLiteLightDefaultDirection3983=true;
window.ThreeDLiteLightEnergy3983=Object.freeze({version:'3.99.0',baselineIntensity:1,referenceDistance:100});

var DirectLightingIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  samplesFor(light,settings){
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const preset=String(settings?.quality||'Preview');
      if(preset==='Ultra')return 16;
      if(preset==='High')return 8;
      if(preset==='Draft')return 1;
      return 4;
    }
    return 1;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    const includeEmission=options.includeEmission!==false;
    const includeDome=options.includeDome!==false;
    let color=includeEmission?RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity):[0,0,0];

    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return includeEmission?RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color):[0,0,0];
    }

    const emissiveSet=compiled.emissiveTriangles||job.emissiveTriangles||null;
    if(emissiveSet?.triangles?.length){
      const es=EmissiveTriangleSet.sample(emissiveSet,pixelSeed,0,hit.position);
      if(es&&ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,es.direction,es.distance)){
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,es.direction);
        const w=RenderMIS.power(es.pdf,bsdfPdf);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,es.radiance),w));
      }
    }

    for(let li=0;li<compiled.lights.length;li++){
      const light=compiled.lights[li];

      if(light.type==='dome'){
        if(includeDome){
          const ambient=EnvironmentSampler.radiance(light,n);
          const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
          color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,ambient),Math.PI));
        }
        continue;
      }

      const count=this.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample||!sample.direction)continue;
        if(!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(v=>v<=0||!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;

        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          const visible=ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance);
          if(!visible)continue;
        }

        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }

    return color;
  }
});

var RenderCoreD=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.90.0-core-d',

  evaluateSurfaceDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options
    );
  },

  evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,rayDirection){
    return EnvironmentSampler.background(
      job.renderScene,job.settings,compiled.lights,rayDirection
    );
  },

  tracePath(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    return PathIntegrator.trace(
      job,acceleration,compiled,ray,pixelSeed,initialHit
    );
  },

  visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance){
    return ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,origin,direction,maxDistance);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g7=validateRenderGroup7();if(!g7.ok)errors.push(...g7.errors);
    const g8=validateRenderGroup8();if(!g8.ok)errors.push(...g8.errors);
    if(typeof this.evaluateSurfaceDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core D direct evaluator missing');
    if(typeof this.tracePath!=='function')errors.push('Core D path tracer missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateEnvironment!=='function')errors.push('Core D environment evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreD=RenderCoreD;

    },0);

    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[0,0,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==false){
      errors.push('Shadow occlusion test failed');
    }
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[3,3,-1],[0,0,1],10)!==true){
      errors.push('Shadow clear test failed');

    if(a[0]!==b[0]||a[1]!==b[1])errors.push('Deterministic sampling test failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup7SelfTests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup7(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof ShadowVisibility.visible!=='function')errors.push('ShadowVisibility missing');
  if(typeof AreaLightSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('AreaLightSampler missing');
  if(typeof DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('DirectLightingIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof EnvironmentSampler.background!=='function')errors.push('EnvironmentSampler missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup7SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const PathSampler=Object.freeze({
  scalar(seed,bounce,dimension){
    return RenderSampling.hash32(
      ((seed>>>0) ^ Math.imul((bounce+1),0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul((dimension+1),0x85EBCA77))>>>0
    );
  },

  cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce){
    const u1=this.scalar(seed,bounce,0);
    con
-- hit 7 line 7755 --
specPdf=(D*G1)/(4*nDotV+1e-8);
    return (1-specProb)*diffusePdf+specProb*specPdf;
  },

  sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce){
    if(material.shading==='unlit')return null;
    const transmission=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,material.transmission||0));
    const chooseT=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,6);

    if(transmission>0&&chooseT<transmission){
      const incident=RayMath.mul(v,-1);
      const entering=RayMath.dot(incident,n)<0;
      const normal=entering?n:RayMath.mul(n,-1);
      const etaI=entering?1:material.ior,etaT=entering?material.ior:1;
      const eta=etaI/etaT;
      const cosI=Math.abs(RayMath.dot(incident,normal));
      const F=DielectricTransport.fresnelDielectric(cosI,etaI,etaT);
      const chooseF=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,16);
      let direction,lobe,weight;
      if(chooseF<F){
        direction=GGXImportanceSampler.reflect(incident,normal);
        lobe='dielectric-reflect';
        weight=[1,1,1];
      }else{
        direction=DielectricTransport.refract(incident,normal,eta);
        if(!direction){
          direction=GGXImportanceSampler.reflect(incident,normal);
          lobe='dielectric-tir';
          weight=[1,1,1];
        }else{
          lobe='transmission';
          weight=material.attenuationColor.slice();
        }
      }
      return {direction:RayMath.normalize(direction),pdf:1,weight,lobe,transmission:true};
    }

    const specProb=Math.max(.08,Math.min(.92,.25+.65*material.metalness+.2*(1-material.roughness)));
    const choose=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,7);
    let direction,lobe='diffuse';

    if(choose<specProb){
      const u1=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,8),u2=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,9);
      const h=GGXImportanceSampler.sampleVisibleHalfVector(n,v,material.roughness,u1,u2);
      direction=GGXImportanceSampler.reflect(RayMath.mul(v,-1),h);
      direction=RayMath.normalize(direction);
      lobe='ggx';
      if(RayMath.dot(n,direction)<=0)direction=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^(0xA511E9B3>>>0),bounce);
    }else{
      direction=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce);
    }

    const cosTheta=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,direction));
    if(cosTheta<=0)return null;
    const value=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,direction);
    const pdf=Math.max(this.pdf(material,n,v,direction),1e-8);
    return {direction,pdf,weight:RenderColor.mul(value,1/pdf),lobe};
  }
});

var EmissiveTriangleSet=Object.freeze({
  compile(job,acceleration,compiled){
    const store=acceleration?.store;
    if(!store)return Object.freeze({triangles:Object.freeze([]),totalArea:0});
    const list=[];let totalArea=0;
    for(let t=0;t<store.triangleCount();t++){
      const objectIndex=store.objectIndices[t];
      const ro=job.renderScene.objects[objectIndex];if(!ro)continue;
      const matId=ro.materialIds[store.materialIndices[t]]??ro.materialIds[0];
      const mat=compiled.materials.get(matId);
      const power=(mat?.emissiveIntensity||0)*Math.max(...(mat?.emissive||[0,0,0]));
      if(!(power>0))continue;
      const k=t*3,a=store.vertex(store.triangles[k]),b=store.vertex(store.triangles[k+1]),c=store.vertex(store.triangles[k+2]);
      const e1=RayMath.sub(b,a),e2=RayMath.sub(c,a);
      const cross=[e1[1]*e2[2]-e1[2]*e2[1],e1[2]*e2[0]-e1[0]*e2[2],e1[0]*e2[1]-e1[1]*e2[0]];
      const area=.5*RayMath.length(cross);
      if(!(area>1e-10))continue;
      const normal=RayMath.normalize(cross);
      totalArea+=area;
      list.push(Object.freeze({triangleIndex:t,objectIndex,material:mat,a,b,c,normal,area,cumulative:totalArea}));
    }
    return Object.freeze({triangles:Object.freeze(list),totalArea});
  },
  sample(set,seed,bounce,point){
    if(!set?.triangles?.length||!(set.totalArea>0))return null;
    const u=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,30)*set.totalArea;
    let tri=set.triangles[set.triangles.length-1];
    for(const t of set.triangles){if(u<=t.cumulative){tri=t;break;}}
    const r1=Math.sqrt(PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,31));
    const r2=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,32);
    const wa=1-r1,wb=r1*(1-r2),wc=r1*r2;
    const lp=[
      tri.a[0]*wa+tri.b[0]*wb+tri.c[0]*wc,
      tri.a[1]*wa+tri.b[1]*wb+tri.c[1]*wc,
      tri.a[2]*wa+tri.b[2]*wb+tri.c[2]*wc
    ];
    const to=RayMath.sub(lp,point),distance=Math.max(1e-6,RayMath.length(to)),direction=RayMath.mul(to,1/distance);
    const cosEmitter=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(tri.normal,RayMath.mul(direction,-1)));
    if(cosEmitter<=0)return null;
    const pdfArea=1/set.totalArea;
    const pdfSolid=pdfArea*(distance*distance)/Math.max(1e-6,cosEmitter);
    const radiance=RenderColor.mul(tri.material.emissive,tri.material.emissiveIntensity);
    return {triangle:tri,point:lp,direction,distance,cosEmitter,pdf:pdfSolid,radiance};
  }
});

const LightTreePolicy=Object.freeze({
  threshold:6,
  enabled(job,compiled){
    return !!job?.settings?.lightTree && (compiled?.lights?.filter?.(l=>l.type!=='dome').length||0)>=this.threshold;
  },
  luminance(c){return .2126*c[0]+.7152*c[1]+.0722*c[2]},
  area(light){
    if(light.type==='rectangle'||light.type==='mesh')return Math.max(.01,light.width*light.height);
    if(light.type==='disc')return Math.max(.01,Math.PI*Math.pow(light.size*.5,2));
    if(light.type==='sphere')return Math.max(.01,4*Math.PI*Math.pow(light.size*.5,2));
    return 1;
  },
  power(light){
    const base=Math.max(1e-8,this.luminance(light.color||[1,1,1])*(light.intensity||0));
    return base;
  }
});

class RenderLightTree{
  constructor(lights){
    this.lights=(lights||[])
      .map((light,sourceIndex)=>({light,sourceIndex}))
      .filter(x=>x.light.type!=='dome')
      .map((x,index)=>({...x,index}));
    this.nodes=[];
    this.root=this.lights.length?this.build(this.lights):-1;
  }
  bounds(items){
    let mn=[Infinity,Infinity,Infinity],mx=[-Infinity,-Infinity,-Infinity],power=0,infinite=false;
    for(const it of items){
      const l=it.light;power+=LightTreePolicy.power(l);
      if(l.type==='sun'){infinite=true;continue;}
      const p=l.position||[0,0,0],r=Math.max(.001,(l.size||Math.max(l.width||0,l.height||0)||1)*.5);
      mn=RayMath.min3(mn,[p[0]-r,p[1]-r,p[2]-r]);
      mx=RayMath.max3(mx,[p[0]+r,p[1]+r,p[2]+r]);
    }
    if(!Number.isFinite(mn[0]))mn=[0,0,0],mx=[0,0,0];
    return {min:mn,max:mx,power:Math.max(1e-8,power),infinite};
  }
  build(items){
    const b=this.bounds(items),id=this.nodes.length;
    if(items.length===1){
      this.nodes.push(Object.freeze({...b,left:-1,right:-1,leaf:true,lightIndex:items[0].index}));
      return id;
    }
    const ext=RayMath.sub(b.max,b.min);let axis=0;if(ext[1]>ext[axis])axis=1;if(ext[2]>ext[axis])axis=2;
    const sorted=items.slice().sort((a,c)=>(a.light.position?.[axis]||0)-(c.light.position?.[axis]||0));
    const mid=Math.max(1,Math.floor(sorted.length/2));
    this.nodes.push(null);
    const left=this.build(sorted.slice(0,mid)),right=this.build(sorted.slice(mid));
    this.nodes[id]=Object.freeze({...b,left,right,leaf:false,lightIndex:-1});
    return id;
  }
  distanceToBounds(point,node){
    if(node.infinite)return 0;
    let d2=0;
    for(let a=0;a<3;a++){
      const v=point[a];
      const q=v<node.min[a]?node.min[a]-v:v>node.max[a]?v-node.max[a]:0;
      d2+=q*q;
    }
    return Math.sqrt(d2);
  }
  score(point,node){
    if(node.infinite)return node.power;
    const d=this.distanceToBounds(point,node);
    const ex=Math.max(1e-6,node.max[0]-node.min[0]);
    const ey
-- hit 8 line 7996 --
seed,bounce,41);
    const cosTheta=1-u1*(1-PathGuidePolicy.coneCos);
    const sinTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosTheta*cosTheta)),phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const {t,b}=this.basis(axis);
    let d=RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(axis,cosTheta),RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(t,Math.cos(phi)*sinTheta),RayMath.mul(b,Math.sin(phi)*sinTheta)));
    d=RayMath.normalize(d);
    if(RayMath.dot(d,normal)<=0){this.fallbacks++;return null;}
    return d;
  }
  pdf(position,normal,direction){
    const g=this.lookup(position,normal);if(!g)return 0;
    const c=RayMath.dot(g.direction,direction);
    if(c<PathGuidePolicy.coneCos||RayMath.dot(normal,direction)<=0)return 0;
    return 1/(2*Math.PI*(1-PathGuidePolicy.coneCos));
  }
  sample(position,normal,seed,bounce){
    const g=this.lookup(position,normal);if(!g)return null;
    const d=this.sampleCone(g.direction,normal,seed,bounce);if(!d)return null;
    this.samples++;this.guided++;
    return {direction:d,pdf:this.pdf(position,normal,d),confidence:g.confidence};
  }
  stats(){return Object.freeze({cells:this.cells.size,updates:this.updates,samples:this.samples,guided:this.guided,fallbacks:this.fallbacks,cellSize:this.cellSize});}
}

const GuidedBSDFSampler=Object.freeze({
  sample(job,material,n,v,position,seed,bounce){
    const field=job?.pathGuide;
    if(!PathGuidePolicy.enabled(job)||!field)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
    const guide=field.lookup(position,n);
    if(!guide)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);

    const mix=Math.min(PathGuidePolicy.maxMix,.15+.3*guide.confidence);
    const choose=PathSampler.scalar(seed,bounce,42);
    let direction,lobe='guided';
    if(choose<mix){
      const gs=field.sample(position,n,seed,bounce);
      if(!gs)return PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
      direction=gs.direction;
    }else{
      const bs=PathBSDFSampler.sample(material,n,v,seed,bounce);
      if(!bs||bs.transmission)return bs;
      direction=bs.direction;lobe=bs.lobe;
    }
    const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,direction);
    const guidePdf=field.pdf(position,n,direction);
    const pdf=Math.max(1e-8,(1-mix)*bsdfPdf+mix*guidePdf);
    const value=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,direction);
    return {direction,pdf,weight:RenderColor.mul(value,1/pdf),lobe,guided:true};
  }
});


const Group9DirectMIS=Object.freeze({
  lightPdf(light,sample){
    if(!light||!sample)return 0;
    if(['point','spot','sun'].includes(light.type))return Infinity; // delta lights: light sampling owns the event
    if(['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)){
      const area=Math.max(RenderLightPolicy.minArea,Number(sample.area)||RenderLightPolicy.area(light));
      const distance=Math.max(1e-6,Number(sample.distance)||1);
      const cosEmitter=Math.max(1e-6,Number(sample.emitterCos)||1);
      return (distance*distance)/(area*cosEmitter);
    }
    return 0;
  },

  evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);
    const maxShadowRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxShadowRaysPerHit)||1024));
    let shadowRays=0;

    if(material.shading==='unlit')return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,color);

    const useTree=LightTreePolicy.enabled(job,compiled)&&job.lightTree;
    const selected=useTree?job.lightTree.sample(hit.position,pixelSeed^0x51ED270B):null;
    const candidates=selected
      ? [{light:selected.light,sourceIndex:selected.sourceIndex,selectionPdf:selected.pdf}]
      : compiled.lights.map((light,sourceIndex)=>({light,sourceIndex,selectionPdf:1}));

    for(const candidate of candidates){
      const light=candidate.light;
      if(!light||light.type==='dome')continue;

      const li=candidate.sourceIndex;
      const selectionPdf=Math.max(1e-8,candidate.selectionPdf||1);
      const count=DirectLightingIntegrator.samplesFor(light,job.settings);
      let accum=[0,0,0];

      for(let si=0;si<count;si++){
        const uv=RenderSampling.sample2D(pixelSeed^(li*2654435761),si);
        const sample=['rectangle','disc','sphere','mesh'].includes(light.type)
          ? AreaLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position,uv[0],uv[1])
          : DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);

        if(!sample?.direction||!sample.radiance||sample.radiance.every(x=>x<=0||!Number.isFinite(x)))continue;
        if(light.castShadows!==false){
          if(shadowRays>=maxShadowRays)break;
          shadowRays++;
          if(!ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,hit.position,sample.direction,sample.distance))continue;
        }

        const scaledRadiance=useTree?RenderColor.mul(sample.radiance,1/selectionPdf):sample.radiance;
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        const bsdfPdf=PathBSDFSampler.pdf(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        const conditionalPdf=this.lightPdf(light,sample);
        const lightPdf=Number.isFinite(conditionalPdf)?conditionalPdf*selectionPdf:conditionalPdf;
        const weight=Number.isFinite(lightPdf)?RenderMIS.power(lightPdf,bsdfPdf):1;
        accum=RenderColor.add(accum,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(brdf,scaledRadiance),weight));
      }

      if(count>1)accum=RenderColor.mul(accum,1/count);
      color=RenderColor.add(color,accum);
    }
    return color;
  }
});

const LightCachePolicy=Object.freeze({
  sceneScale(acceleration){
    const root=acceleration?.bvh?.root;
    const node=(root!=null&&root>=0)?acceleration.bvh.nodes[root]:null;
    if(!node)return 1;
    const d=RayMath.sub(node.max,node.min);
    return Math.max(1e-4,RayMath.length(d));
  },
  radius(job,acceleration){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    const divisor=q==='Ultra'?64:q==='High'?48:q==='Draft'?20:32;
    return Math.max(RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([0,0,0])*32,this.sceneScale(acceleration)/divisor);
  },
  probes(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?16:q==='High'?12:q==='Draft'?3:8;
  },
  maxRecords(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?12000:q==='High'?8000:q==='Draft'?1500:4000;
  },
  retraceEvery(job){
    const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
    return q==='Ultra'?8:q==='High'?12:q==='Draft'?32:16;
  },
  normalThreshold:.75
});

  insert(position,normal,radiance){
    if(this.records.length>=this.maxRecords){this.rejected++;return false;}
    if(!position?.every(Number.isFinite)||!normal?.every(Number.isFinite)||!radiance?.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0)){this.rejected++;return false;}
    const rec=Object.freeze({position:position.slice(),normal:RayMath.normalize(normal),radiance:radiance.slice()});
    const id=this.records.length;this.records.push(rec);this.inserts++;

    let bucket=this.grid.get(k);if(!bucket)this.grid.set(k,bucket=[]);
    bucket.push(id);return true;
  }
  query(position,normal){
    const c=this.cell(position);let sum=[0,0,0],ws=0;
    for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let y=-1;y<=1;y++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
      const bucket=this.grid.get(this.key(c[0]+x,c[1]+y,c[2]+z));if(!bucket)continue;
      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id];
        const delta=RayMath.sub(r.position,position),dist=RayMath.length(delta);
        if(dist>this.radius)continue;
        const nd=RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal);if(nd<LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold)continue;
        const w=(1-dist/this.radius)*Math.max(.05,nd);
        sum=RenderColor.add(sum,RenderColor.mul(r.radiance,w));ws+=w;
      }
    }

      for(const id of bucket){
        const r=this.records[id],d=RayMath.length(RayMath.sub(r.position,position));
        if(d<this.radius&&d<bestD&&RayMath.dot(r.normal,normal)>=LightCachePolicy.normalThreshold){best=id;bestD=d;}
      }
    }
    if(best<0)return this.insert(position,normal,radiance);
    const old=this.records[best];
    this.records[best]=Object.freeze({
      position:old.position,normal:old.normal,
      radiance:[
        old.radiance[0]*.75+radiance[0]*.25,
        old.radiance[1]*.75+radiance[1]*.25,
        old.radiance[2]*.75+radiance[2]*.25
      ]
    });
    this.refreshes++;return true;
  }
  stats(){return Object.freeze({records:this.records.length,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,inserts:this.inserts,rejected:this.rejected,retraces:this.retraces,refreshes:this.refreshes,radius:this.radius});}
}

function runRenderPart5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const finite3=v=>Array.isArray(v)&&v.length===3&&v.every(Number.isFinite);

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p5mat',name:'P5',shading:'physical',
      baseColor:[2,-1,NaN],roughness:-5,metalness:4,opacity:2,
      emissive:[NaN,.5,2],emissiveIntensity:-5
    });
    if(!finite3(mat.baseColor)||!finite3(mat.emissive))errors.push('Material finite-color sanitization failed');
    if(mat.roughness<.045||mat.roughness>1)errors.push('Roughness clamp failed');
    if(mat.metalness!==1||mat.opacity!==1||mat.emissiveIntensity!==0)errors.push('Material scalar clamp failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Material policy test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'ggx',name:'GGX',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.5,.5,.5],
      roughness:.045,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const out=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[1,0,.000001],[1,0,.000001]);
    if(!finite3(out)||out.some(v=>v<0))errors.push('GGX grazing-angle finite test failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('GGX finite test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const c=RenderCameraPolicy.sanitize({
      position:[NaN,0,0],quaternion:[0,0,0,0],up:[0,0,1],

    });

      {position:[0,0,0],quaternion:[0,0,0,1],up:[0,0,1],fov:50,near:.01,far:100},

      errors.push('Camera center-ray test failed');

  }catch(err){errors.push('Camera test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:999}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path upper bounce clamp failed');
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth({settings:{maxBounces:0}})!==5)errors.push('Path invalid bounce fallback failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Path depth test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const envCompiled={lights:[]};
    const envJob={settings:{background:'#202020',maxBounces:5},renderScene:{objects:[]}};
    const missAccel={trace(){return {hit:false};}};

    const miss=PathIntegrator.tracePixel(envJob,missAccel,envCompiled,ray,1);
    if(miss.primaryHit!==false||!finite3(miss.radiance))errors.push('Path environment escape failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Path environment test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const unlit=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'unlit',name:'Unlit',shading:'unlit',baseColor:[.5,.5,.5],
      roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[.1,0,0],emissiveIntensity:2
    });

      errors.push('Unlit path termination failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Unlit termination test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderPart5(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderMaterialPolicy?.finiteColor!=='function')errors.push('RenderMaterialPolicy missing');
  if(typeof PhysicalBSDF?.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('PhysicalBSDF missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator?.normal!=='function')errors.push('Surface normal interpolator missing');
  if(typeof RenderCameraPolicy?.sanitize!=='function')errors.push('RenderCameraPolicy missing');
  if(typeof RenderCameraRayGenerator?.ray!=='function')errors.push('Render camera ray generator missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator?.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer?.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const t=runRenderPart5SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const PathSampler=Object.freeze({
  scalar(seed,bounce,dimension){
    return RenderSampling.hash32(
      ((seed>>>0) ^ Math.imul((bounce+1),0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul((dimension+1),0x85EBCA77))>>>0
    );
  },

  cosineHemisphere(n,seed,bounce){
    const u1=this.scalar(seed,bounce,0);
    const u2=this.scalar(seed,bounce,1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(u1);
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const x=r*Math.cos(phi),y=r*Math.sin(phi),z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-u1));

    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,x),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,y)),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,z)
      )
    );
  }
});

var RenderMIS=Object.freeze({
  power(a,b){
    a=Math.max(0,Number(a)||0);b=Math.max(0,Number(b)||0);
    const aa=a*a,bb=b*b,d=aa+bb;
    return d>0?aa/d:1;
  }
});

const GGXImportanceSampler=Object.freeze({
  sampleHalfVector(n,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness);
    const a2=a*a;
    const phi=2*Math.PI*u1;
    const cosTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,(1-u2)/(1+(a2-1)*u2)));
    const sinTheta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosTheta*cosTheta));
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    return RayMath.normalize(
      RayMath.add(
        RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,sinTheta*Math.cos(phi)),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,sinTheta*Math.sin(phi))),
        RayMath.mul(basis.n,cosTheta)
      )
    );
  },
  sampleVisibleHalfVector(n,v,roughness,u1,u2){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness);
    const basis=LightBasis.fromDirection(n);
    const vl=[RayMath.dot(v,basis.tangent),RayMath.dot(v,basis.bitangent),Math.max(1e-6,RayMath.dot(v,basis.n))];
    const vh=RayMath.normalize([a*vl[0],a*vl[1],vl[2]]);
    const lensq=vh[0]*vh[0]+vh[1]*vh[1];
    const t1=lensq>1e-12?[-vh[1]/Math.sqrt(lensq),vh[0]/Math.sqrt(lensq),0]:[1,0,0];
    const t2=RayMath.cross(vh,t1);
    const r=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,u1)),phi=2*Math.PI*u2;
    const p1=r*Math.cos(phi);
    let p2=r*Math.sin(phi);
    const mix=.5*(1+vh[2]);
    p2=(1-mix)*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-p1*p1))+mix*p2;
    const z=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-p1*p1-p2*p2));
    const nh=RayMath.add(RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(t1,p1),RayMath.mul(t2,p2)),RayMath.mul(vh,z));
    const ne=RayMath.normalize([a*nh[0],a*nh[1],Math.max(0,nh[2])]);
    return RayMath.normalize(RayMath.add(RayMath.add(RayMath.mul(basis.tangent,ne[0]),RayMath.mul(basis.bitangent,ne[1])),RayMath.mul(basis.n,ne[2])));
  },
  reflect(i,n){return RayMath.sub(i,RayMath.mul(n,2*RayMath.dot(i,n)));},
  distribution(nDotH,roughness){
    const a=Math.max(.002025,roughness*roughness),a2=a*a;
    const d=nDotH*nDotH*(a2-1)+1;
    return a2/(Math.PI*d*d+1e-8);
  }
});

const DielectricTransport=Object.freeze({
  fresnelDielectric(cosI,etaI,etaT){
    cosI=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,cosI));
    let entering=cosI>0;
    if(!entering){const t=etaI;etaI=etaT;etaT=t;cosI=Math.abs(cosI);}
    const sinI=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-cosI*cosI));
    const sinT=etaI/etaT*sinI;
    if(sinT>=1)return 1;
    const cosT=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-sinT*sinT));
    const rs=((etaT*cosI)-(etaI*cosT))/((etaT*cosI)+(etaI*cosT));
    const rp=((etaI*cosI)-(etaT*cosT))/((etaI*cosI)+(etaT*cosT));
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(rs*rs+rp*rp)*.5));
  },
  refract(i,n,eta){
    const cosi=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,RayMath.dot(i,n)));
    const k=1-eta*eta*(1-cosi*cosi);
    if(k<0)return null;
    return RayMath.normalize(RayMath.sub(RayMath.mul(i,eta),RayMath.mul(n,eta*cosi+Math.sqrt(k))));
  },
  beerLambert(color,distance,attenuationDistance){
    if(!Number.isFinite(attenuationDistance)||attenuationDistance<=0||distance<=0)return [1,1,1];
    return color.map(c=>Math.pow(Math.max(1e-6,c),distance/attenuationDistance));
  }
});

var PathBSDFSampler=Object.freeze({
  pdf(material,n,v,l){
    const nDotL=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,l));
    if(nDotL<=0)return 0;
    const diffusePdf=nDotL/Math.PI;
    const specProb=Math.max(.08,Math.min(.92,.25+.65*material.metalness+.2*(1-material.roughness)));
    const h=RayMath.normalize(RayMath.add(v,l));
    if(!h.every(Number.isFinite))return (1-specProb)*diffusePdf;
    const nDotH=Math.max(0,RayMath.dot(n,h));
    const vDotH=Math.max(1e-7,RayMath.dot(v,h));
    const nDotV=Math.max(1e-7,RayMath.dot(n,v));
    const D=PhysicalBSDF.distributionGGX(nDotH,material.roughness);
    const G1=PhysicalBSDF.geometrySchlickGGX(nDotV,material.roughness);
    // VNDF half-vector pdf: D(h)*G1(v)*|v.h|/|n.v|.
    // Reflection Jacobian 1/(4|v.h|) cancels the v.h term.
    const specPdf=(D*G1)/(4*nDotV+1e-8);
    return (1-specProb)*diffusePdf+specProb*specPdf;
  },

          sampledThisPass++;
        }
        perf16.yields++;
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
      });
      completedPasses=pass+1;
      const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
      job.previewFrame={
        width,height,rgba,
        metadata:Object.freeze({
          renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
          samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
          currentPass:completedPasses,
          maxSamples,
          adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
          noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
          activePixels:accumulator.active
        })
      };
      job.progress.currentSamples=Math.round(accumulator.averageSamples());
      job.progress.maximumSamples=maxSamples;
      job.progress.noise=accumulator.averageNoise();
      onProgress(Math.min(1,completedPasses/passLimit));

      if(!progressive)break;
      if(settings.adaptive&&completedPasses>=settings.minSamples&&accumulator.active===0)break;
      if(sampledThisPass===0)break;
    }

    const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
    for(let i=0;i<width*height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,accumulator.samples[i]),o=i*3;
      const beauty=[accumulator.sum[o]/s,accumulator.sum[o+1]/s,accumulator.sum[o+2]/s];
      aovs.set3('Beauty',i,beauty);
      aovs.set1('Noise',i,Number.isFinite(accumulator.noise(i))?accumulator.noise(i):0);
      const d=aovs.get('Direct Lighting'),e=aovs.get('Emission');
      const direct=d?[d[o],d[o+1],d[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      const emission=e?[e[o],e[o+1],e[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      aovs.set3('Global Illumination',i,[
        Math.max(0,beauty[0]-direct[0]-emission[0]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[1]-direct[1]-emission[1]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[2]-direct[2]-emission[2])
      ]);
    }
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
        samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
        passes:completedPasses,
        maxBounces:PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job),
        estimator:'Path GI + direct NEE + power-heuristic MIS',
        mis:true,
        adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
        progressive:!!settings.progressive,
        russianRoulette:true,
        importanceSampling:'GGX + cosine mixture',
        noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
        transmission:true,
        secondaryGI:settings.secondaryGI,
        lightCache:job.lightCache?.stats?.()||null,
        emissiveTriangleLights:compiled.emissiveTriangles?.triangles?.length||0,
        diagnostics:RenderPart5Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});
