function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

Object.assign(window,{
  PathSampler,PathBSDFSampler,PathIntegrator,PathGIRenderer,RenderGroup8Contract

function runRenderGroup9SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const w=RenderMIS.power(.5,.5);
    expect(Math.abs(w-.5)<1e-9,'MIS equal-PDF power heuristic failed');
    expect(RenderMIS.power(1,0)===1,'MIS zero competitor failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('MIS test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const h=GGXImportanceSampler.sampleHalfVector(n,.25,.37,.61);
    expect(h.every(Number.isFinite)&&RayMath.dot(n,h)>=0,'GGX half-vector sample failed');
    const D=GGXImportanceSampler.distribution(.8,.25);
    expect(Number.isFinite(D)&&D>0,'GGX distribution failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('GGX test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'g9',name:'g9',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.7,.6,.5],
      roughness:.2,metalness:.8,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],12345,0);
    expect(!!s&&s.pdf>0&&Number.isFinite(s.pdf),'Mixed BSDF sampler failed');
    expect(s.weight.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0),'Mixed BSDF weight invalid');
  }catch(e){errors.push('BSDF importance test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const a=new ProgressiveAccumulator(2,1);
    a.add(0,[1,1,1]);a.add(0,[1,1,1]);
    a.add(1,[0,0,0]);a.add(1,[2,2,2]);
    expect(a.samples[0]===2&&a.samples[1]===2,'Accumulator sample count failed');
    expect(a.noise(0)===0,'Stable pixel noise should be zero');
    expect(Number.isFinite(a.noise(1))&&a.noise(1)>0,'Variable pixel noise estimation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Accumulator test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const s=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      progressive:true,adaptive:true,minSamples:4,maxSamples:32,noiseThreshold:.02
    });
    expect(s.progressive&&s.adaptive,'Sampling toggles lost');
    expect(s.minSamples===4&&s.maxSamples===32&&Math.abs(s.noiseThreshold-.02)<1e-9,'Sampling settings lost');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Sampling settings test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const fake={type:'rectangle',width:2,height:2};
    const pdf=Group9DirectMIS.lightPdf(fake,{area:4,distance:2,emitterCos:1});
    expect(Math.abs(pdf-1)<1e-9,'Area light solid-angle PDF conversion failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Direct MIS PDF test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup9(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderMIS?.power!=='function')errors.push('MIS helper missing');
  if(typeof GGXImportanceSampler?.sampleHalfVector!=='function')errors.push('GGX importance sampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler?.pdf!=='function')errors.push('BSDF PDF missing');
  if(typeof Group9DirectMIS?.evaluate!=='function')errors.push('Direct MIS evaluator missing');
  if(typeof ProgressiveAccumulator!=='function')errors.push('Progressive accumulator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer?.render!=='function')errors.push('Progressive Path renderer missing');
  const t=runRenderGroup9SelfTests();if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

  async _prepare(job,generation){
    try{
      if(!job.renderScene)throw new Error('Render job missing RenderSceneSnapshot');

      RendererRuntimeState.workerReady=this.workerPool.available;
      job.progress.threads=this.workerPool.size;

      job.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.RENDERING;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.beginTracing(job);
      job.progress.gi=(job.settings.engine==='path'&&job.settings.globalIllumination)
        ? `Path GI • ${PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job)} bounces`

        try{window.ThreeDLiteRenderWindow3979?.renderProgress?.(job,ratio);}catch(_){}
        if(job.cancelled||generation!==this.generation)return;
        job.progress.percent=96+Math.round(ratio*4);

        try{ViewportDebugLine?.renderer?.();}catch(_){}
      });

      const selectedChannel=document.getElementById('rfChannel')?.value||'Beauty';
      if(!RenderFramework.showChannel(selectedChannel))RenderFramework.ui.frameBuffer.present(diagnostic);
      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions();
      try{window.ThreeDLiteRenderWindow3979?.stage?.(job,'Composite',1);}catch(_){}

      job.finished=true;
      RenderFramework.recordCompleted?.(job);
      job.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.FINISHED;
      job.progress.percent=100;
      RenderPerformanceMetrics.finish(job);

      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice){
        const a=job.acceleration;
        notice.style.display='none';
        notice.innerHTML=`Render complete.<br>${a.triangleCount} triangles • ${a.nodeCount} BVH nodes.`;
      }

      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }catch(err){
      if(generation!==this.generation)return;

      job.progress.elapsedMs=Math.max(0,performance.now()-(job.startedAt||performance.now()));
      if(err?.cancelled||job.cancelled){
        job.cancelled=true;
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
        job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
      }else{
        job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new RenderExecutionError(String(err?.message||err),err);
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;
        RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'render',job.error);
        const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
        if(notice){notice.style.display='flex';notice.innerHTML=`Render failed (${job.error.stage||'render'}).<br>${String(job.error.message||job.error)}`;}
      }

      RenderFramework.updateFrameActions?.();
      this.onProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
    }
  }

  cancel(report=true){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled)return false;

    j.setCancelled?.(true);
    j.setPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

      renderer:'Fast Direct',
      lights:(compiled?.lights||[]).length,

      materials:compiled?.materials?.list?.length||0,
      triangles:acceleration?.store?.triangleCount?.()||0,

    });
  }

function runRenderPart4SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const finiteColor=c=>Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);

  try{
    const p=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'p4-point',name:'Point',type:'point',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:100,range:5,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:45,penumbra:0,
      castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const smp=DirectLightSampler.sample(p,[0,0,0]);
    if(!finiteColor(smp.radiance)||smp.radiance.some(v=>v!==0))errors.push('Point range cutoff failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Point light test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const spot=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'p4-spot',name:'Spot',type:'spot',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:10,range:100,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:20,penumbra:.1,
      castShadows:true,position:[0,0,10],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const outside=DirectLightSampler.sample(spot,[10,0,0]);
    if(!finiteColor(outside.radiance))errors.push('Spot radiance non-finite');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Spot test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const rect=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'p4-rect',name:'Rect',type:'rectangle',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:10,range:0,size:1,width:.01,height:.01,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0,
      castShadows:true,position:[0,0,5],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const front=AreaLightSampler.sample(rect,[0,0,0],.5,.5);
    const back=AreaLightSampler.sample(rect,[0,0,10],.5,.5);
    if(!finiteColor(front.radiance)||front.area<RenderLightPolicy.minArea)errors.push('Rectangle safe-area test failed');
    if(back.radiance.some(v=>v!==0))errors.push('One-sided rectangle emitter test failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Area light test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const dome=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'p4-dome',name:'Dome',type:'dome',enabled:true,color:'#808080',
      intensity:2,range:0,size:1,width:1,height:1,coneAngle:90,penumbra:0,
      castShadows:false,position:[0,0,0],direction:[0,0,-1]
    });
    const c=EnvironmentSampler.background(null,{background:'#000000'},[dome],[0,0,1]);
    if(!finiteColor(c)||c.every(v=>v===0))errors.push('Dome environment test failed');
    const f=EnvironmentSampler.background(null,{background:'#202020'},[],[0,0,1]);
    if(!finiteColor(f))errors.push('Environment fallback test failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Environment test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({
      objectId:'p4-shadow',
      positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,2,1,-1,2,0,1,2]),
      normals:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,0,1]),
      uvs:new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,.5,1]),
      indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])
    },0);
    store.finalize();
    const acceleration=new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,1));
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[0,0,0],[0,0,1],1)!==true)errors.push('Finite shadow max-distance test failed');
    if(ShadowVisibility.visible(acceleration,[0,0,0],[0,0,1],4)!==false)errors.push('Shadow blocker test failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Shadow test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const invalid=new RenderSceneLight({
      id:'bad',name:'bad',type:'unsupported',enabled:true,color:'#ffffff',
      intensity:NaN,range:-1,size:0,width:0,height:0,coneAngle:999,penumbra:-2,
      castShadows:true,position:[NaN,0,0],direction:[0,0,0]
    });
    if(invalid.type!=='point'||invalid.intensity!==0||invalid.range!==0||
       invalid.size<RenderLightPolicy.minLinearSize||!invalid.direction.every(Number.isFinite)){
      errors.push('Light sanitization test failed');
    }
  }catch(err){errors.push('Light sanitization test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function runRenderPart5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const finite3=v=>Array.isArray(v)&&v.length===3&&v.every(Number.isFinite);

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p5mat',name:'P5',shading:'physical',
      baseColor:[2,-1,NaN],roughness:-5,metalness:4,opacity:2,
      emissive:[NaN,.5,2],emissiveIntensity:-5
    });
    if(!finite3(mat.baseColor)||!finite3(mat.emissive))errors.push('Material finite-color sanitization failed');
    if(mat.roughness<.045||mat.roughness>1)errors.push('Roughness clamp failed');
    if(mat.metalness!==1||mat.opacity!==1||mat.emissiveIntensity!==0)errors.push('Material scalar clamp failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Material policy test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'ggx',name:'GGX',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.5,.5,.5],
      roughness:.045,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const out=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[1,0,.000001],[1,0,.000001]);
    if(!finite3(out)||out.some(v=>v<0))errors.push('GGX grazing-angle finite test failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('GGX finite test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const c=RenderCameraPolicy.sanitize({
      position:[NaN,0,0],quaternion:[0,0,0,0],up:[0,0,1],
      fov:999,aspect:0,near:-1,far:0
    });
    if(!finite3(c.position)||!finite3(c.quaternion.slice(0,3))||c.fov!==179||c.near<=0||c.far!==Infinity)
      errors.push('Camera sanitization failed');

    const center=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
      {position:[0,0,0],quaternion:[0,0,0,1],up:[0,0,1],fov:50,near:.01,far:100},
      49.5,49.5,100,100
    );
    if(Math.abs(center.direction[0])>1e-6||Math.abs(center.direction[1])>1e-6||center.direction[2]>-.999)
      errors.push('Camera center-ray test failed');

    if(Math.abs(RenderCameraPolicy.outputAspect(1920,1080)-(16/9))>1e-9)
      errors.push('Camera output-aspect test failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Camera test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:999}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path upper bounce clamp failed');
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth({settings:{maxBounces:0}})!==5)errors.push('Path invalid bounce fallback failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Path depth test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const envCompiled={lights:[]};
    const envJob={settings:{background:'#202020',maxBounces:5},renderScene:{objects:[]}};
    const missAccel={trace(){return {hit:false};}};
    const ray=new RenderRay([0,0,0],[0,0,-1],1e-4,Infinity,RenderRayType.CAMERA,0);
    const miss=PathIntegrator.tracePixel(envJob,missAccel,envCompiled,ray,1);
    if(miss.primaryHit!==false||!finite3(miss.radiance))errors.push('Path environment escape failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Path environment test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{
    const unlit=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'unlit',name:'Unlit',shading:'unlit',baseColor:[.5,.5,.5],
      roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[.1,0,0],emissiveIntensity:2
    });
    if(PathBSDFSampler.sample(unlit,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],1,0)!==null)
      errors.push('Unlit path termination failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Unlit termination test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function runRenderPart5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const finite3=v=>Array.isArray(v)&&v.length===3&&v.every(Number.isFinite);

      id:'p5mat',name:'P5',shading:'physical',

    if(mat.metalness!==1||mat.opacity!==1||mat.emissiveIntensity!==0)errors.push('Material scalar clamp failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Material policy test: '+String(err?.message||err));}

  try{

      id:'ggx',name:'GGX',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.5,.5,.5],
      roughness:.045,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0

    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path upper bounce clamp failed');
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth({settings:{maxBounces:0}})!==5)errors.push('Path invalid bounce fallback failed');

class RenderSettingsModel{
  constructor(){
    this.engine='fast';
    this.quality='Preview';
    this.width=1280;this.height=720;this.percentage=100;
    this.aspectLock=true;this.transparent=false;
    this.format='PNG';this.colorDepth='8-bit';
    this.camera='Active View';this.focalLength=50;this.sensorWidth=36;
    this.fStop=8;this.iso=100;
    this.globalIllumination=true;this.primaryGI='Brute Force';this.secondaryGI='Brute Force';
    this.progressive=true;this.adaptive=true;this.minSamples=8;this.maxSamples=64;this.noiseThreshold=.03;
    this.maxBounces=5;this.denoise='Off';this.toneMapping='ACES';this.exposure=0;
    this.background='#202020';this.environmentStrength=1;
    this.cpuThreads='Auto';this.tileSize='Auto';this.acceleration='BVH';
    this.lightTree=false;this.pathGuiding=false;
  }
  clone(){return Object.freeze({...this});}
}

class RenderProgressState{
  constructor(){
    this.status=RenderJobStatus.IDLE;
    this.percent=0;this.currentSamples=0;this.maximumSamples=0;
    this.noise=null;this.threads=0;this.resolution=[0,0];this.gi='—';
    this.elapsedMs=0;this.raysPerSecond=null;this.bvhBuildMs=null;
  }
  snapshot(){return Object.freeze({...this,resolution:Object.freeze([...this.resolution])});}
}

class RenderJob{
  constructor(id,settings,mode='full',region=null){
    this.id=id;
    this.settings=settings;
    this.mode=mode;
    this.region=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    this.status=RenderJobStatus.IDLE;
    this.cancelled=false;this.paused=false;this.finished=false;this.error=null;
    this.startedAt=0;this.finishedAt=0;this.renderStartedAt=0;
    this.progress=new RenderProgressState();
    this.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    this.controlToken16=new RenderControlToken16();
    this.setCancelled=v=>{this.cancelled=!!v;if(v)this.controlToken16.cancel();};
    this.setPaused=v=>{this.paused=!!v;v?this.controlToken16.pause():this.controlToken16.resume();};
  }
}

class RenderFrameBufferView{
  constructor(canvas){
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});
    this.zoom=1;
    this.channel='Beauty';
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=0;
    this.clear();
  }
  clear(){
    const c=this.canvas,x=this.ctx;
    x.save();
    x.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
    x.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.fillStyle='#111';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
    x.strokeStyle='#242424';x.lineWidth=1;
    for(let px=0;px<c.width;px+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(px,0);x.lineTo(px,c.height);x.stroke();}
    for(let py=0;py<c.height;py+=32){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,py);x.lineTo(c.width,py);x.stroke();}
    x.restore();
    this.hasValidFrame=false;
    this.lastFrame=null;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
  }
  resize(w,h,{preserve=false}={}){
    const old=preserve&&this.hasValidFrame?this.snapshot():null;
    this.canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w));
    this.canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h));
    if(old&&old.width===this.canvas.width&&old.height===this.canvas.height)this.present(old);
    else this.clear();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
  }
  present(frame){
    if(!frame||!Number.isFinite(frame.width)||!Number.isFinite(frame.height)||!frame.rgba)throw new RenderExecutionError('Invalid framebuffer payload');
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.height));
    const expected=w*h*4;
    if(frame.rgba.length!==expected)throw new RenderExecutionError(`Framebuffer byte length mismatch: ${frame.rgba.length} != ${expected}`);
    if(this.canvas.width!==w||this.canvas.height!==h){this.canvas.width=w;this.canvas.height=h;}
    const pixels=frame.rgba instanceof Uint8ClampedArray?frame.rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(frame.rgba);
    this.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(pixels,w,h),0,0);
    this.lastFrame={width:w,height:h,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels),metadata:frame.metadata||null};
    this.hasValidFrame=true;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
    return true;
  }
  snapshot(){
    if(!this.hasValidFrame||!this.lastFrame)return null;
    return {width:this.lastFrame.width,height:this.lastFrame.height,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(this.lastFrame.rgba),metadata:this.lastFrame.metadata};
  }
  setZoom(value){
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(8,Number(value)||1));
    this.canvas.style.width=(this.canvas.width*this.zoom)+'px';
    this.canvas.style.height=(this.canvas.height*this.zoom)+'px';
  }
  fit(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById('renderCanvasWrap');if(!wrap)return;
    const zx=(wrap.clientWidth-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.width);
    const zy=(wrap.clientHeight-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.height);
    this.setZoom(Math.max(.1,Math.min(zx,zy)));
  }
  oneToOne(){this.setZoom(1);}
}

class RenderUIShell{
  constructor(){
    this.menu=null;this.settingsWindow=null;this.frameWindow=null;this.frameBuffer=null;
  }
  cache(){
    this.menu=document.getElementById('renderMenuPopup');
    this.settingsWindow=document.getElementById('renderSettingsWindow');
    this.frameWindow=document.getElementById('renderFrameWindow');
    this.frameBuffer=new RenderFrameBufferView(document.getElementById('renderCanvas'));
  }
  open(el){el?.classList.add('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
  close(el){el?.classList.remove('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
  updateProgress(p){
    const text=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    text('rfStatus',p.status);
    const bar=document.getElementById('renderProgress');if(bar)bar.value=p.percent||
-- hit 2 line 10656 --
etPaused?.(false);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.CANCELLED;

    const cancelledTasks=this.workerPool.cancelJob(j.id);
    this.generation++;
    j.workerCancellationCount=cancelledTasks;

    if(report)this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  pause(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||j.paused)return false;

    j.setPaused?.(true);
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PAUSED;
    this.workerPool.pause();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  resume(){
    const j=this.activeJob;
    if(!j||j.finished||j.cancelled||!j.paused)return false;

    j.paused=false;
    j.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    j.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.PREPARING_GEOMETRY;
    this.workerPool.resume();

    this.onProgress(j.progress.snapshot());
    return true;
  }

  dispose(){
    this.cancel(false);
    this.workerPool.dispose();
  }
}

var RenderFramework={
  version:'3.79.01-group1-gen3',
  settings:new RenderSettingsModel(),
  ui:new RenderUIShell(),
  jobs:null,
  nextJobId:1,
  lastSettings:null,
  lastRegion:null,
  lastCompletedSettings:null,
  lastCompletedRegion:null,
  pendingRegion:null,
  regionSelectArmed:false,
  initialized:false,

  readSettings(){
    const value=id=>document.getElementById(id)?.value;
    const checked=id=>!!document.getElementById(id)?.checked;
    const x=this.settings;
    x.engine=value('rsEngine')||x.engine;
    x.quality=value('rsQuality')||x.quality;
    x.width=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsWidth'))||1280);
    x.height=Math.max(16,Number(value('rsHeight'))||720);
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsPercent'))||100);
    x.aspectLock=checked('rsAspectLock');
    x.transparent=checked('rsTransparent');
    x.format=value('rsFormat')||'PNG';
    x.colorDepth=value('rsDepth')||'8-bit';
    x.camera=value('rsCamera')||'Active View';
    x.focalLength=Number(value('rsFocal'))||50;
    x.sensorWidth=Number(value('rsSensor'))||36;
    x.fStop=Number(value('rsFStop'))||8;
    x.iso=Number(value('rsISO'))||100;
    x.globalIllumination=checked('rsGI');
    x.primaryGI=value('rsPrimaryGI')||'Brute Force';
    x.secondaryGI=value('rsSecondaryGI')||'Light Cache';
    x.progressive=checked('rsProgressive');
    x.adaptive=checked('rsAdaptive');
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsMinSamples'))||8);
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Number(value('rsMaxSamples'))||256);
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsNoise'))||.025);
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Number(value('rsBounces'))||5);
    x.denoise=value('rsDenoise')||'Off';
    x.toneMapping=value('rsToneMap')||'ACES';
    x.exposure=Number(value('rsExposure'))||0;
    x.background=value('rsBackground')||'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Number(value('rsEnvStrength'))||0);
    x.cpuThreads=value('rsThreads')||'Auto';
    x.tileSize=value('rsTile')||'Auto';
    x.acceleration=value('rsAcceleration')||'BVH';
    x.lightTree=checked('rsLightTree');
    x.pathGuiding=checked('rsPathGuiding');
    return RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize(x.clone());
  },

  effectiveResolution(settings){
    const scale=(settings.percentage||100)/100;
    return [
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.width*scale)),
      Math.max(1,Math.round(settings.height*scale))
    ];
  },

  createJob(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    const settings=settingsOverride||this.readSettings();
    const job=new RenderJob(this.nextJobId++,settings,mode,region);
    job.renderScene=null;
    const res=this.effectiveResolution(settings);
    job.progress.maximumSamples=settings.maxSamples;
    job.progress.resolution=res;
    job.progress.gi=settings.globalIllumination
      ? `${settings.primaryGI} + ${settings.secondaryGI}`
      : 'Off';
    return job;
  },

  render(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){
    let job;
    if(mode==='full'){
      region=null;
      this.pendingRegion=null;
      this.regionSelectArmed=false;
    }else if(region){
      region=RenderRegion14.sanitize(region);
      if(!region)return false;
    }
    if(RendererRuntimeState.degraded){
      const criticalReady=
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.createRequest==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.prepare==='function' &&
        typeof RendererMasterCore?.rendererFor==='function' &&
        !!this.jobs &&
        !!this.ui?.frameBuffer;

      const recovery=criticalReady
        ? (RendererRuntimeState.markReady(),{ok:true,errors:[]})
        : RendererBootstrap.recover();

      if(!recovery.ok){
        job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
        job.error=new RenderExecutionError('Renderer recovery failed: '+recovery.errors.join('; '));

  createJob(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){

    job.renderScene=null;

    job.progress.gi=settings.globalIllumination
      ? `${settings.primaryGI} + ${settings.secondaryGI}`
      : 'Off';
    return job;

  render(mode='full',region=null,settingsOverride=null){

    if(mode==='full'){
      region=null;
      this.pendingRegion=null;

        !!this.jobs &&

      const recovery=criticalReady
        ? (RendererRuntimeState.markReady(),{ok:true,errors:[]})
        : RendererBootstrap.recover();

      if(!recovery.ok){
        job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
        job.error=new RenderExecutionError('Renderer recovery failed: '+recovery.errors.join('; '));
        job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;

      job=this.createJob(mode,region,settingsOverride);
      job.error=err instanceof RendererError?err:new SnapshotError(String(err?.message||err),err);job.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;job.progress.status=RenderJobStatus.ERROR;RendererRuntimeState.fail(job.error.stage||'snapshot',job.error);
      this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);this.ui.updateProgress(job.progress.snapshot());
      const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
      if(notice)notice.innerHTML='Render snapshot failed.<br>'+String(err?.message||err);
      console.error('3DLite Render snapshot:',err);
      return job;
    }

    this.lastRegion=region?Object.freeze({...region}):null;
    RendererRuntimeState.markReady();
    this.ui.open(this.ui.frameWindow);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.resize(...job.progress.resolution);
    this.ui.frameBuffer.fit();
    const notice=document.getElementById('renderFrameNotice');
    if(notice)notice.innerHTML=`Render snapshot ready.<br>${job.renderScene.objects.length} object(s) • ${job.renderScene.materials.length} material(s).`;

    return job;
  },

  stop(){return this.jobs.cancel(true);},

    this.ui.frameBuffer.channel=name;

      if(!blob||!navigator.clipboard?.write||typeof ClipboardItem==='undefined')return false;

const RenderSettingsPolicy=Object.freeze({
  sanitize(s){
    const x={...s};
    x.engine=x.engine==='path'?'path':'fast';
    x.quality=['Draft','Preview','High','Ultra','Custom'].includes(x.quality)?x.quality:'Preview';
    x.width=Math.max(16,Math.min(8192,Math.round(Number(x.width)||1280)));
    x.height=Math.max(16,Math.min(8192,Math.round(Number(x.height)||720)));
    x.percentage=Math.max(1,Math.min(200,Number(x.percentage)||100));
    x.maxBounces=Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(x.maxBounces)||5));
    x.background=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(x.background||'')?x.background:'#202020';
    x.environmentStrength=Math.max(0,Math.min(1e6,Number(x.environmentStrength)||0));
    x.cpuThreads=x.cpuThreads==='Auto'?'Auto':String(Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(x.cpuThreads)||1)));
    x.tileSize=String(x.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(!['Auto','8','16','32','64'].includes(x.tileSize))x.tileSize='Auto';
    x.acceleration='BVH';
    x.progressive=x.progressive!==false;
    x.adaptive=x.adaptive!==false;
    x.minSamples=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Math.round(Number(x.minSamples)||8)));
    x.maxSamples=Math.max(x.minSamples,Math.min(4096,Math.round(Number(x.maxSamples)||64)));
    x.noiseThreshold=Math.max(0.0001,Math.min(1,Number(x.noiseThreshold)||.03));
    x.denoise=['Off','Fast','Quality'].includes(x.denoise)?x.denoise:'Off';
    x.toneMapping=['ACES','Reinhard','Linear'].includes(x.toneMapping)?x.toneMapping:'ACES';
    x.exposure=Math.max(-10,Math.min(10,Number(x.exposure)||0));
    x.primaryGI='Brute Force';x.secondaryGI=['None','Brute Force','Light Cache'].includes(x.secondaryGI)?x.secondaryGI:'Brute Force';
    x.format=x.format==='JPEG'?'JPEG':'PNG';
    x.colorDepth='8-bit';
    x.lightTree=!!x.lightTree;
    x.pathGuiding=!!x.pathGuiding;
    return Object.freeze(x);
  }
});

function markRenderSettingsCustom(event){
  const id=event?.target?.id||'';
  if(!id||id==='rsQuality'||id==='rsPreset')return;
  const q=document.getElementById('rsQuality');
  if(q&&q.value!=='Custom')q.value='Custom';
}

function applyRenderQualityPreset(name){
  const table={
    Draft:{bounces:2,min:2,max:8,noise:.08},
    Preview:{bounces:5,min:8,max:64,noise:.03},
    High:{bounces:8,min:16,max:256,noise:.015},
    Ultra:{bounces:12,min:32,max:512,noise:.008}
  };
  const p=table[name];if(!p)return;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=v;};
  set('rsBounces',p.bounces);set('rsMinSamples',p.min);set('rsMaxSamples',p.max);set('rsNoise',p.noise);
}


function setRenderGroup1FutureControlsState(){
  const deferred=['rsPrimaryGI'];
  for(const id of deferred){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=true;el.title='Deferred to a later renderer group';}
  }
  for(const id of ['rsGI','rsSecondaryGI','rsBounces','rsProgressive','rsAdaptive','rsMinSamples','rsMaxSamples','rsNoise','rsDenoise','rsToneMap','rsExposure','rsLightTree','rsPathGuiding']){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=false;el.title='';}
  }
  const denoise=document.getElementById('rsDenoise');if(denoise&&!['Off','Fast','Quality'].includes(denoise.value))denoise.value='Off';

  const format=document.getElementById('rsFormat');
  if(format){
    [...format.options].forEach(o=>{if(o.value==='EXR'||o.text==='EXR')o.disabled=true;});
    if(format.value==='EXR')format.value='PNG';
  }
  const depth=document.getElementById('rsDepth');
  if(depth){
    [...depth.options].forEach(o=>o.disabled=o.text!=='8-bit');
    depth.value='8-bit';
    depth.title='16/32-bit output is deferred until the render channel/export architecture is implemented';
  }
}


const RenderGroup1Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'render menu',
    'render settings UI shell',
    'render framebuffer UI shell',
    'render request settings',
    'render job lifecycle state'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor scene objects',
    'MNMesh topology',
    'selection',
    'transforms',
    'viewport materials',
    'editor undo history',
    'render scene extraction',
    'ray tracing',
    'BVH',
    'lighting',
    'path integration'
  ]),
  extensionPoints:Object.freeze([
    'RenderFramework.createJob',
    'RenderJobController.start',
    'RenderFrameBufferView',
    'RenderFramework.render'
  ])

function validateRenderGroup1(){
  const errors=[];
  const ids=[
    'renderMenuBtn','renderMenuPopup','renderSettingsWindow','renderFrameWindow',
    'renderNowBtn','renderSettingsBtn','renderLastBtn','renderRegionBtn',
    'renderStopBtn','showRenderWindowBtn','renderCanvas','renderProgress'
  ];
  for(const id of ids)if(!document.getElementById(id))errors.push('Missing '+id);
  if(!(RenderFramework.settings instanceof RenderSettingsModel))errors.push('Render settings model unavailable');
  if(!(RenderFramework.ui instanceof RenderUIShell))errors.push('Render UI shell unavailable');
  if(typeof RenderFramework.render!=='function')errors.push('Render API unavailable');
  if(!RenderGroup1Contract?.extensionPoints?.length)errors.push('Render Group 1 contract unavailable');
  if(RenderFramework.initialized && !RenderFramework.jobs)errors.push('Render job controller unavailable');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const LightCacheEstimator=Object.freeze({
  enabled(job){return job?.settings?.globalIllumination!==false&&job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='Light Cache';},
  cacheable(material){return material&&material.shading!=='unlit'&&(material.transmission||0)<.05&&(material.metalness||0)<.95;},

  probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const count=LightCachePolicy.probes(job);
    let incoming=[0,0,0];
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,seed^Math.imul(i+1,0x85EBCA77),bounce+17);
      const origin=PathIntegrator.offsetOrigin(hit,n,d);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const probeRay=new RenderRay(origin,d,eps,Infinity,RenderRayType.DIFFUSE,bounce+1);
      try{LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance?.addProbe?.();}catch(_){}
      const ph=acceleration.trace(probeRay);
      let li;
      if(!ph.hit){
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,d));
      }else{
        li=PathIntegrator.safeColor(Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,probeRay,ph,seed^Math.imul(i+3,0x9E3779B9)));
      }
      incoming=RenderColor.add(incoming,li);
    }
    incoming=RenderColor.mul(incoming,1/Math.max(1,count));
    const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,Math.max(0,1-material.metalness));
    return RenderColor.mul(diffuse,incoming);
  },

  estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce){
    const cache=job.lightCache;if(!cache||!this.cacheable(material))return null;
    const found=cache.query(hit.position,n);
    if(found){
      const cadence=LightCachePolicy.retraceEvery(job);
      if(cadence>0&&cache.hits%cadence===0){
        cache.retraces++;
        const fresh=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed^0xB5297A4D,bounce);
        cache.refreshNearest(hit.position,n,fresh);
        return RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.add(RenderColor.mul(found,.75),RenderColor.mul(fresh,.25)),1);
      }
      return found;
    }

    const outgoing=this.probe(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,seed,bounce);
    cache.insert(hit.position,n,outgoing);
    return outgoing;
  }
});

var PathIntegrator=Object.freeze({
  maxDepth(job){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Number(job?.settings?.maxBounces)||5));
  },

  finiteColor(c){
    return Array.isArray(c)&&c.length===3&&c.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0);
  },

  safeColor(c){
    return this.finiteColor(c)?c:[0,0,0];
  },

  offsetOrigin(hit,n,direction){
    const sign=RayMath.dot(direction,n)>=0?1:-1;
    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(hit.position);
    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

  trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    let radiance=[0,0,0];

    let previousGuide=null;
    const maxDepth=this.maxDepth(job);
    const maxPathRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxPathRays)||64));
    let pathRays=1;

    for(let bounce=0;bounce<maxDepth;bounce++){

          job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
        }

      const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
      const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

      // Next-event direct lighting at each hit. Dome is excluded here because
      // environment energy is accounted for by path miss evaluation.
      const direct=Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(
        job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed^(bounce*0x9E3779B9)
      );
      const safeDirect=this.safeColor(direct);
      radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,safeDirect));

      if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
        const w=Math.max(0,.2126*safeDirect[0]+.7152*safeDirect[1]+.0722*safeDirect[2]);
        job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
      }

      if(bounce>=1){
        if(job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='None')break;
        if(LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)){
          const cachedTail=LightCacheEstimator.estimate(job,acceleration,compiled,hit,n,material,pixelSeed,bounce);
          if(cachedTail){
            radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,cachedTail));

      if(!this.finiteColor(throughput))break;

    const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(hit.position);
    return RayMath.add(hit.position,RayMath.mul(n,eps*sign));
  },

  trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,initialHit=null){
    let radiance=[0,0,0];
    let throughput=[1,1,1];
    let ray=primaryRay;
    let previousGuide=null;
    const maxDepth=this.maxDepth(job);
    const maxPathRays=Math.max(1,Math.min(4096,Number(job?.settings?.maxPathRays)||64));
    let pathRays=1;

    for(let bounce=0;bounce<maxDepth;bounce++){
      const hit=(bounce===0&&initialHit)?initialHit:acceleration.trace(ray);

          job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
        }
        break;

      const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,compiled.materials,hit);
      const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
      const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);

      // Next-event direct lighting at each hit. Dome is excluded here because
      // environment energy is accounted for by path miss evaluation.
      const direct=Group9DirectMIS.evaluate(
        job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed^(bounce*0x9E3779B9)
      );
      const safeDirect=this.safeColor(direct);
      radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,safeDirect));

      if(previousGuide&&job.pathGuide){
        const w=Math.max(0,.2126*safeDirect[0]+.7152*safeDirect[1]+.0722*safeDirect[2]);
        job.pathGuide.record(previousGuide.position,previousGuide.normal,previousGuide.direction,w);
      }

        if(job?.settings?.secondaryGI==='None')break;

          if(cachedTail){
            radiance=RenderColor.add(radiance,RenderColor.mul(throughput,cachedTail));
            break;

      if(material.shading==='unlit')break;

      if(!sample)break;

      if(!this.finiteColor(throughput))break;
      if(Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])<1e-6)break;

      // Russian roulette after the first few bounces. Survival probability tracks path throughput.
      if(bounce>=2){
        const survive=Math.max(.08,Math.min(.95,Math.max(throughput[0],throughput[1],throughput[2])));
        if(PathSampler.scalar(pixelSeed,bounce,15)>survive)break;
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(throughput,1/survive);
      }

      if(sample.transmission&&material.thickness>0){
        throughput=RenderColor.mul(
          throughput,
          DielectricTransport.beerLambert(material.attenuationColor,material.thickness,material.attenuationDistance)
        );
      }

      previousGuide=(!sample.transmission&&job.pathGuide&&RayMath.dot(n,sample.direction)>0)
        ? {position:hit.position.slice(),normal:n.slice(),direction:sample.direction.slice()}
        : null;

      if(pathRays>=maxPathRays)break;
      pathRays++;
      const origin=this.offsetOrigin(hit,n,sample.direction);
      const eps=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint(origin);
      const rayType=sample.transmission?RenderRayType.TRANSMISSION:(sample.lobe==='ggx'?RenderRayType.GLOSSY:RenderRayType.DIFFUSE);
      RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job).addRay(rayType);
      ray=new RenderRay(origin,sample.direction,eps,Infinity,rayType,bounce+1);
    }

    return radiance;
  },

  tracePixel(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed){
    const primaryHitRecord=acceleration.trace(primaryRay);
    if(!primaryHitRecord.hit){
      const env=this.safeColor(RenderCoreD.evaluateEnvironment(job,compiled,primaryRay.direction));
      return {radiance:env,primaryHit:false,primaryHitRecord:null};
    }
    return {
      radiance:this.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,primaryRay,pixelSeed,primaryHitRecord),
      primaryHit:true,
      primaryHitRecord
    };
  }
});

class ProgressiveAccumulator{
  constructor(width,height){
    this.width=width;this.height=height;
    const n=width*height;
    this.sum=new Float64Array(n*3);
    this.mean=new Float64Array(n);
    this.m2=new Float64Array(n);
    this.samples=new Uint32Array(n);
    this.converged=new Uint8Array(n);
    this.active=n;
  }
  add(index,color){
    const s=++this.samples[index];
    const o=index*3;
    this.sum[o]+=color[0];this.sum[o+1]+=color[1];this.sum[o+2]+=color[2];
    const lum=.2126*color[0]+.7152*color[1]+.0722*color[2];
    const delta=lum-this.mean[index];
    this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
    this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
  }
  variance(index){
    const s=this.samples[index];
    return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
  }
  noise(index){
    const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
    const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
    return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
  }
  shouldSample(index,settings){
    if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
    if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
    return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
  }
  markConverged(index){
    if(this.converged[index])return;
    this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
  }
  resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
      const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
      const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
      rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
      rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

    return count?sum/count:null;
  }

var PathGIRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;
    const compiled=RenderPart5Core.compile(job);
    const settings=job.settings;
    job.lightCache=LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)?new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration):null;
    job.lightTree=RenderCoreH.createLightTree(job,compiled);
    job.pathGuide=RenderCoreH.createPathGuide(job,acceleration);
    job.progress.gi=settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache'?'Brute Force + Light Cache':settings.secondaryGI;
    const accumulator=new ProgressiveAccumulator(width,height);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const primaryMask=new Uint8Array(width*height);
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const rowChunk16=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;
    const maxSamples=Math.max(1,settings.maxSamples||1);
    const progressive=settings.progressive!==false;
    const passLimit=progressive?maxSamples:1;
    let completedPasses=0;

    for(let pass=0;pass<passLimit;pass++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));

      let sampledThisPass=0;
      const activePixels16=progressive?accumulator.compactActive(settings):(()=>{
        const a=new Uint32Array(width*height);for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)a[i]=i;return a;
      })();

      await workQueue16.run(async batch=>{
        for(const i of batch){
          if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
          const x=i%width,y=(i/width)|0;
          const seed=((i+1)^Math.imul(pass+1,0x9E3779B1))>>>0;
          const jitterX=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,20)-.5;
          const jitterY=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,21)-.5;
          const ray=RenderPart5Core.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x+jitterX,rr.y0+y+jitterY,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
          const sample=RenderPart5Core.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,seed);
          perf16.samples++;
          primaryMask[i]|=sample.primaryHit?1:0;
          accumulator.add(i,sample.radiance);
          if(pass===0){

            else RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,null);
          }
          sampledThisPass++;
        }
        perf16.yields++;
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
      });
      completedPasses=pass+1;
      const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);

          currentPass:completedPasses,
          maxSamples,

          activePixels:accumulator.active

      job.progress.currentSamples=Math.round(accumulator.averageSamples());

ngs.minSamples)return true;
    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
    return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
  }
  markConverged(index){
    if(this.converged[index])return;
    this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
  }
  resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
      const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
      const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
      rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
      rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

var PathGIRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;
    const compiled=RenderPart5Core.compile(job);
    const settings=job.settings;
    job.lightCache=LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)?new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration):null;
    job.lightTree=RenderCoreH.createLightTree(job,compiled);
    job.pathGuide=RenderCoreH.createPathGuide(job,acceleration);
    job.progress.gi=settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache'?'Brute Force + Light Cache':settings.secondaryGI;
    const accumulator=new ProgressiveAccumulator(width,height);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);
    const primaryMask=new Uint8Array(width*height);
    const perf16=RenderCoreH.attachPerformance(job);
    const rowChunk16=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);
    const renderStart16=RenderProfiler16.begin();
    LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=job;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=perf16;
    const maxSamples=Math.max(1,settings.maxSamples||1);
    const progressive=settings.progressive!==false;
    const passLimit=progressive?maxSamples:1;
    let completedPasses=0;

    for(let pass=0;pass<passLimit;pass++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));

      let sampledThisPass=0;
      const activePixels16=progressive?accumulator.compactActive(settings):(()=>{
        const a=new Uint32Array(width*height);for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)a[i]=i;return a;
      })();
      if(activePixels16.length===0)break;

      const workQueue16=RenderWorkQueue16.fromIndices(activePixels16,Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*4));
      await workQueue16.run(async batch=>{
        for(const i of batch){
          if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
          const x=i%width,y=(i/width)|0;
          const seed=((i+1)^Math.imul(pass+1,0x9E3779B1))>>>0;
          const jitterX=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,20)-.5;
          const jitterY=PathSampler.scalar(seed,pass,21)-.5;
          const ray=RenderPart5Core.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x+jitterX,rr.y0+y+jitterY,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
          const sample=RenderPart5Core.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,seed);
          perf16.samples++;
          primaryMask[i]|=sample.primaryHit?1:0;
          accumulator.add(i,sample.radiance);
          if(pass===0){
            const ph=sample.primaryHitRecord;
            if(ph?.hit)RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,ph,seed));
            else RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,null);
          }
          sampledThisPass++;
        }
        perf16.yields++;
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
      });
      completedPasses=pass+1;
      const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
      job.previewFrame={
        width,height,rgba,
        metadata:Object.freeze({
          renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
          samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
          currentPass:completedPasses,
          maxSamples,
          adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
          noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
          activePixels:accumulator.active
        })
      };
      job.progress.currentSamples=Math.round(accumulator.averageSamples());
      job.progress.maximumSamples=maxSamples;
      job.progress.noise=accumulator.averageNoise();
      onProgress(Math.min(1,completedPasses/passLimit));

      if(!progressive)break;

      if(sampledThisPass===0)break;

    for(let i=0;i<width*height;i++){

      aovs.set1('Noise',i,Number.isFinite(accumulator.noise(i))?accumulator.noise(i):0);
      const d=aovs.get('Direct Lighting'),e=aovs.get('Emission');

const RenderGroup8Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'multi-bounce path integration',
    'path throughput',
    'secondary-ray spawning',
    'cosine hemisphere BSDF sampling baseline',
    'environment contribution on path miss',
    'path-GI render mode'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B ray/BVH tracing',
    'Core C physical materials and lights',
    'Group 7 shadow visibility and direct-light sampling'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'progressive accumulation',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'MIS',
    'Light Cache',
    'denoising',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

Object.assign(window,{
  PathSampler,PathBSDFSampler,PathIntegrator,PathGIRenderer,RenderGroup8Contract
});

function runRenderGroup9SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const w=RenderMIS.power(.5,.5);
    expect(Math.abs(w-.5)<1e-9,'MIS equal-PDF power heuristic failed');
    expect(RenderMIS.power(1,0)===1,'MIS zero competitor failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('MIS test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const h=GGXImportanceSampler.sampleHalfVector(n,.25,.37,.61);
    expect(h.every(Number.isFinite)&&RayMath.dot(n,h)>=0,'GGX half-vector sample failed');
    const D=GGXImportanceSampler.distribution(.8,.25);
    expect(Number.isFinite(D)&&D>0,'GGX distribution failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('GGX test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'g9',name:'g9',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.7,.6,.5],
      roughness:.2,metalness:.8,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const s=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],12345,0);
    expect(!!s&&s.pdf>0&&Number.isFinite(s.pdf),'Mixed BSDF sampler failed');
    expect(s.weight.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0),'Mixed BSDF weight invalid');
  }catch(e){errors.push('BSDF importance test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const a=new ProgressiveAccumulator(2,1);
    a.add(0,[1,1,1]);a.add(0,[1,1,1]);
    a.add(1,[0,0,0]);a.add(1,[2,2,2]);
    expect(a.samples[0]===2&&a.samples[1]===2,'Accumulator sample count failed');
    expect(a.noise(0)===0,'Stable pixel noise should be zero');
    expect(Number.isFinite(a.noise(1))&&a.noise(1)>0,'Variable pixel noise estimation failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Accumulator test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const s=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSetting
-- hit 7 line 8983 --

      noiseThreshold:job?.settings?.noiseThreshold??0,
      maxBounces:job?.settings?.maxBounces??1,
      emissiveTriangles:shading?.emissiveTriangles?.triangles?.length||0,
      transmission:true,
      mis:true,
      russianRoulette:true,
      importanceSampling:'GGX + cosine mixture'
    });
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g9=validateRenderGroup9();if(!g9.ok)errors.push(...g9.errors.map(e=>'Group 9: '+e));
    const g10=validateRenderGroup10();if(!g10.ok)errors.push(...g10.errors.map(e=>'Group 10: '+e));
    const own=runRenderCoreESelfTests();if(!own.ok)errors.push(...own.errors);
    if(typeof this.prepareShading!=='function')errors.push('Core E shading preparation missing');
    if(typeof this.rendererFor!=='function')errors.push('Core E renderer selection missing');
    if(typeof this.diagnostics!=='function')errors.push('Core E diagnostics missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});

/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — GROUP 11 GEN3
   LIGHT CACHE
   ============================================================ */
const RenderGroup11Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'secondary-GI Light Cache mode',
    'world-space irradiance record storage',
    'spatial neighborhood lookup',
    'normal-aware cache reuse',
    'quality-scaled cache radius and probe counts',
    'periodic cache retracing/refresh',
    'secondary-GI None termination policy'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core E progressive path transport',
    'Group 9 MIS/direct-light evaluation',
    'Core B acceleration',
    'Part 6 render settings and status UI'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'primary GI replacement',
    'photon mapping',
    'caustic photon maps',
    'AOV architecture',
    'denoising',
    'Light Tree',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup11SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const settings=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      secondaryGI:'Light Cache'
    });
    expect(settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache','Light Cache settings mode was not preserved');
    const none=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      secondaryGI:'None'
    });
    expect(none.secondaryGI==='None','Secondary GI None mode was not preserved');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache settings test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const fakeJob={settings:{quality:'Preview'}};
    const cache=new RenderLightCache(fakeJob,null);
    expect(cache.insert([0,0,0],[0,0,1],[1,.5,.25]),'Light Cache insert failed');
    const hit=cache.query([cache.radius*.1,0,0],[0,0,1]);
    expect(hit&&hit.every(Number.isFinite),'Light Cache nearby lookup failed');
    const miss=cache.query([cache.radius*3,0,0],[0,0,1]);
    expect(miss===null,'Light Cache distant lookup leaked');
    const normalMiss=cache.query([0,0,0],[0,0,-1]);
    expect(normalMiss===null,'Light Cache normal rejection failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache storage test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const draft=LightCachePolicy.probes({settings:{quality:'Draft'}});
    const ultra=LightCachePolicy.probes({settings:{quality:'Ultra'}});
    expect(ultra>draft,'Light Cache quality probe scaling failed');
    expect(LightCachePolicy.maxRecords({settings:{quality:'Ultra'}})>LightCachePolicy.maxRecords({settings:{quality:'Draft'}}),
      'Light Cache record scaling failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache policy test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    expect(LightCacheEstimator.enabled({settings:{globalIllumination:true,secondaryGI:'Light Cache'}})===true,
      'Light Cache enable policy failed');
    expect(LightCacheEstimator.enabled({settings:{globalIllumination:true,secondaryGI:'Brute Force'}})===false,
      'Brute Force incorrectly enabled Light Cache');
    expect(LightCacheEstimator.enabled({settings:{globalIllumination:false,secondaryGI:'Light Cache'}})===false,
      'Disabled GI incorrectly enabled Light Cache');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache mode test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const fakeJob={settings:{quality:'Preview'}};
    const cache=new RenderLightCache(fakeJob,null);
    cache.insert([0,0,0],[0,0,1],[.2,.4,.6]);
    cache.refreshNearest([0,0,0],[0,0,1],[.6,.4,.2]);
    const s=cache.stats();
    expect(s.records===1&&s.refreshes===1,'Light Cache refresh duplicated records');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache retrace refresh test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup11(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderLightCache!=='function')errors.push('RenderLightCache missing');
  if(typeof LightCachePolicy?.radius!=='function')errors.push('LightCachePolicy missing');
  if(typeof LightCacheEstimator?.estimate!=='function')errors.push('LightCacheEstimator missing');
  if(typeof LightCacheEstimator?.probe!=='function')errors.push('Light Cache probe estimator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup11SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderGroup11Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.25-group11-gen3',
  validate:validateRenderGroup11,
  policy:LightCachePolicy,
  estimator:LightCacheEstimator,
  create(job,acceleration){return new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration);}

/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — GROUP 11 GEN3
   LIGHT CACHE
   ============================================================ */
const RenderGroup11Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'secondary-GI Light Cache mode',
    'world-space irradiance record storage',
    'spatial neighborhood lookup',
    'normal-aware cache reuse',
    'quality-scaled cache radius and probe counts',
    'periodic cache retracing/refresh',
    'secondary-GI None termination policy'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core E progressive path transport',
    'Group 9 MIS/direct-light evaluation',
    'Core B acceleration',
    'Part 6 render settings and status UI'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'primary GI replacement',
    'photon mapping',
    'caustic photon maps',
    'AOV architecture',
    'denoising',
    'Light Tree',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup11SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};

  try{
    const settings=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      secondaryGI:'Light Cache'
    });
    expect(settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache','Light Cache settings mode was not preserved');
    const none=RenderSettingsPolicy.sanitize({
      ...new RenderSettingsModel().clone(),
      secondaryGI:'None'
    });
    expect(none.secondaryGI==='None','Secondary GI None mode was not preserved');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache settings test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const fakeJob={settings:{quality:'Preview'}};
    const cache=new RenderLightCache(fakeJob,null);
    expect(cache.insert([0,0,0],[0,0,1],[1,.5,.25]),'Light Cache insert failed');
    const hit=cache.query([cache.radius*.1,0,0],[0,0,1]);
    expect(hit&&hit.every(Number.isFinite),'Light Cache nearby lookup failed');
    const miss=cache.query([cache.radius*3,0,0],[0,0,1]);
    expect(miss===null,'Light Cache distant lookup leaked');
    const normalMiss=cache.query([0,0,0],[0,0,-1]);
    expect(normalMiss===null,'Light Cache normal rejection failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache storage test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const draft=LightCachePolicy.probes({settings:{quality:'Draft'}});
    const ultra=LightCachePolicy.probes({settings:{quality:'Ultra'}});
    expect(ultra>draft,'Light Cache quality probe scaling failed');
    expect(LightCachePolicy.maxRecords({settings:{quality:'Ultra'}})>LightCachePolicy.maxRecords({settings:{quality:'Draft'}}),
      'Light Cache record scaling failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache policy test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    expect(LightCacheEstimator.enabled({settings:{globalIllumination:true,secondaryGI:'Light Cache'}})===true,
      'Light Cache enable policy failed');
    expect(LightCacheEstimator.enabled({settings:{globalIllumination:true,secondaryGI:'Brute Force'}})===false,
      'Brute Force incorrectly enabled Light Cache');
    expect(LightCacheEstimator.enabled({settings:{globalIllumination:false,secondaryGI:'Light Cache'}})===false,
      'Disabled GI incorrectly enabled Light Cache');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache mode test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  try{
    const fakeJob={settings:{quality:'Preview'}};
    const cache=new RenderLightCache(fakeJob,null);
    cache.insert([0,0,0],[0,0,1],[.2,.4,.6]);
    cache.refreshNearest([0,0,0],[0,0,1],[.6,.4,.2]);
    const s=cache.stats();
    expect(s.records===1&&s.refreshes===1,'Light Cache refresh duplicated records');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Light Cache retrace refresh test: '+String(e?.message||e));}

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup11(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderLightCache!=='function')errors.push('RenderLightCache missing');
  if(typeof LightCachePolicy?.radius!=='function')errors.push('LightCachePolicy missing');
  if(typeof LightCacheEstimator?.estimate!=='function')errors.push('LightCacheEstimator missing');
  if(typeof LightCacheEstimator?.probe!=='function')errors.push('Light Cache probe estimator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup11SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderGroup11Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.25-group11-gen3',
  validate:validateRenderGroup11,
  policy:LightCachePolicy,
  estimator:LightCacheEstimator,
  create(job,acceleration){return new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration);}
});

Object.assign(window,{
  LightCachePolicy,RenderLightCache,LightCacheEstimator,
  RenderGroup11Contract,runRenderGroup11SelfTests,validateRenderGroup11,RenderGroup11Core
});
